DiffusionBench: On Holistic Evaluation of Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Xingjian Leng, Jaskirat Singh, Zhanhao Liang, Ethan Smith, Martin Bell, Aninda Saha, Yuhui Yuan, Liang Zheng
First: 2026-06-23T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:59:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion transformer (DiT) research on image generation has converged to a single evaluation setup: class-conditional generation on ImageNet. While methods improve the FID and related metrics, it is increasingly unclear whether they reflect real progress in generative modeling. The natural alternative, i.e., text-to-image (T2I) generation, is perceived as too costly or inconvenient to train and evaluate and is often skipped. We argue that this perception no longer holds. We introduce NanoGen, a unified DiT training and evaluation framework. NanoGen matches state-of-the-art DiT baselines on ImageNet and, with 12 lines of configuration change, also trains competitive text-to-image models. It currently supports RAE, VAE, pixel-space, and MeanFlow diffusion methods under both ImageNet and T2I setups. Under NanoGen, training T2I requires comparable compute to ImageNet. After training 21 latent diffusion models with NanoGen, we observe that method ranking shows no strong correlation between ImageNet and T2I generation: Pearson correlation is between -0.377 and -0.580 across three metrics. This suggests that a method which improves class-conditional ImageNet FID may show no corresponding improvement on T2I, clearly indicating the necessity of evaluating DiTs on both tasks. To this end, we summarize ImageNet and text-to-image results, which yields DiffusionBench, a holistic benchmark for DiT research. We recommend reporting DiffusionBench in place of ImageNet alone: methods that improve DiffusionBench are more likely to reflect broader progress.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiffusionBench：扩散Transformer的全面评估
图像生成领域的扩散Transformer（DiT）研究已收敛于单一评估设置：ImageNet的类别条件生成。尽管方法在FID等指标上有所提升，但其是否反映生成建模的真实进步已日益存疑。自然的替代方案——文本到图像（T2I）生成，过去被认为训练和评估成本过高或不便，常被跳过。我们主张这一看法已不成立。我们引入NanoGen，一个统一的DiT训练与评估框架。NanoGen在ImageNet上达到当前最先进DiT基线水平，仅需12行配置变更即可训练出具有竞争力的T2I模型。目前支持RAE、VAE、像素空间及MeanFlow扩散方法，适用于ImageNet与T2I两种设置。在NanoGen下，T2I训练所需计算量与ImageNet相当。训练21个潜在扩散模型后，我们发现ImageNet与T2I生成方法排名之间无强相关性：三类指标的皮尔逊相关系数介于-0.377至-0.580之间。这表明提升ImageNet类别条件FID的方法未必在T2I上同步提升，明确指出需同时评估DiT在两项任务上的表现。为此，我们汇总ImageNet与T2I结果，形成DiffusionBench，作为DiT研究的全面基准。我们建议用DiffusionBench替代仅报告ImageNet：改进DiffusionBench的方法更可能反映更广泛的进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of comprehensive evaluation for diffusion transformers (DiTs), the authors argue that relying solely on ImageNet class-conditional generation metrics fails to capture real progress in generative modeling. They introduce NanoGen, a unified training and evaluation framework that achieves state-of-the-art ImageNet results and enables efficient text-to-image (T2I) training with minimal configuration changes. Evaluating 21 latent diffusion models under NanoGen, they find weak to negative correlations (Pearson -0.377 to -0.580) between ImageNet and T2I performance across metrics, indicating that improvements on one task do not guarantee gains on the other. Consequently, they propose DiffusionBench—a holistic benchmark combining both ImageNet and T2I results—as a more reliable indicator of DiT advancement.
鉴于仅依赖ImageNet类条件生成评估无法全面反映扩散Transformer（DiT）的真实进展，本文提出NanoGen统一训练与评估框架，该框架在ImageNet上达到SOTA性能，且仅需12行配置调整即可训练出竞争力的文本到图像（T2I）模型。实验表明，在训练21个潜在扩散模型后，ImageNet与T2I性能间的相关性极弱甚至负相关（皮尔逊系数-0.377至-0.580），说明提升ImageNet指标未必带来T2I性能改善。因此，作者构建DiffusionBench综合基准，涵盖ImageNet与T2I两项任务，建议研究者优先报告该基准结果，以更准确衡量DiT模型的综合进步。
InSight: Self-Guided Skill Acquisition via Steerable VLAs
Authors: Maggie Wang, Lars Osterberg, Stephen Tian, Ola Shorinwa, Jiajun Wu, Mac Schwager
First: 2026-06-23T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:59:01+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://insight-vla.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models can learn manipulation skills from demonstrations, but their capabilities are bounded by the skills in the training data. We present InSight, a framework that unlocks autonomous skill acquisition by rendering VLAs steerable at the primitive-action level (e.g., "move gripper to the bowl", "lift upward", "pour the bottle"). InSight consists of two primary stages: (1) an automated segmentation pipeline that partitions demonstrations into labeled primitives via VLM plan decomposition and end-effector poses to enable VLA primitive steerability, and (2) a VLM-guided data flywheel that identifies missing primitives required to accomplish a novel task, autonomously attempts demonstrations of the missing primitives with VLM-proposed low-level control, and automatically labels, stores, and integrates successful demonstrations into the VLA training set. We evaluate InSight across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, including block flipping, drawer closing, sweeping, twisting, and pouring, without any human demonstrations of these target skills. Once learned, these primitives can be composed to execute novel, long-horizon tasks without additional human demonstrations. Our findings demonstrate that primitive steerability provides a practical foundation for continual skill acquisition in VLA policies. Project website: https://insight-vla.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InSight：通过可引导的视觉-语言-动作模型实现自主技能获取
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型可通过演示学习操作技能，但其能力受限于训练数据中的技能。我们提出InSight框架，通过在原始动作层面（如“将夹爪移至碗上”、“向上抬起”、“倾倒瓶子”）引导VLA模型，实现自主技能获取。InSight包含两个主要阶段：（1）自动化分割流程，通过视觉语言模型（VLM）的计划分解和末端执行器姿态，将演示分割为带标签的原始动作，以实现VLA的原始动作引导；（2）VLM引导的数据飞轮，自动识别完成新任务所需的缺失原始动作，以VLM提出的低层控制自主尝试演示，自动标注、存储并整合成功演示至VLA训练集中。我们在仿真与真实世界操作任务（如积木翻转、抽屉关闭、扫地、扭转、倾倒）中评估InSight，无需任何人类演示目标技能。一旦学习完成，这些原始动作可组合执行新颖的长时任务，无需额外人类演示。我们的研究结果表明，原始动作引导为VLA策略的持续技能获取提供了实用基础。项目网站：https://insight-vla.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that vision-language-action models can only learn skills present in training data, the authors propose InSight, a framework enabling autonomous skill acquisition by making VLAs steerable at the primitive-action level. The method consists of two stages: first, an automated segmentation pipeline decomposes demonstrations into labeled primitive actions using vision-language models and end-effector poses; second, a VLM-guided data flywheel identifies missing primitives for novel tasks, autonomously attempts their execution via VLM-generated low-level control, and integrates successful demonstrations into the training set. Experiments in simulation and real-world manipulation tasks—such as block flipping, drawer closing, and pouring—show that InSight learns new primitives without human demonstrations and composes them to execute novel long-horizon tasks, demonstrating that primitive steerability enables continual skill acquisition in VLA policies.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型只能执行训练数据中已有的技能这一局限，作者提出了InSight框架，通过在原始动作层面实现对视觉-语言-动作模型的可控性，使其能自主学习新技能。该方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段是自动化分割流程，利用视觉-语言模型和末端执行器姿态将演示分解为带标签的原始动作；第二阶段是视觉-语言模型引导的数据飞轮，自动识别缺失的原始动作，通过模型生成的底层控制尝试执行，成功后自动标注、存储并整合到训练集中。实验在仿真与真实世界任务（如翻块、关抽屉、扫地、拧转、倒水）中验证，无需人工演示即可学习新动作，并能组合执行长时任务，表明原始动作可控性是视觉-语言-动作策略持续学习的实用基础。
BenchX: Benchmarking AI Models for Cancer Detection and Localization with Demographic and Protocol Biases
Authors: Qi Chen, Wenxuan Li, Pedro R. A. S. Bassi, Xinze Zhou, Jakob Wasserthal, Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci, Sezgin Er, Ashwin Kumar, Yiwen Ye, Yuhan Wang, Yuyin Zhou, Akshay S. Chaudhari, Curtis Langlotz, Kang Wang, Yang Yang, Alan L. Yuille, Zongwei Zhou
First: 2026-06-23T17:58:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:58:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) has achieved remarkable success in medical imaging, but it is widely recognized that these models often perform inconsistently across real-world clinical settings. Such inconsistencies occur when patient demographics and imaging protocols vary, for example, in detecting small tumors, analyzing scans from different contrast phases, or evaluating patients of different ages or sexes. To quantify these inconsistencies, we develop a large-scale, open benchmark of 85,355 CT scans that systematically evaluates 12 tumor-detection AI models across tumor size, location, patient subgroup, and imaging protocol. We leverage large language models (LLMs) to extract and organize subgroup information from clinical data, which makes the analysis both scalable and reproducible. Our benchmark reveals that current state-of-the-art AI models, optimized for average accuracy, perform poorly in rare or underrepresented subgroups, such as young, female African Americans. However, collecting sufficient annotated data for these rare cases is often impractical. The benchmark provides a foundation for building more reliable and robust AI models for tumor detection and highlighting the need for rigorous, subgroup-level evaluation in medical imaging and computer vision. Datasets, code
中文标题/摘要
标题：BenchX：用于癌症检测与定位的AI模型基准测试，考虑人口统计学与协议偏差
人工智能（AI）在医学影像领域取得了显著成功，但普遍认为这些模型在真实临床环境中表现不一致。这种不一致性源于患者人口统计学特征和成像协议的差异，例如检测小肿瘤、分析不同对比相位的扫描，或评估不同年龄或性别的患者。为量化这些不一致性，我们构建了一个包含85,355例CT扫描的大规模开放基准，系统评估12种肿瘤检测AI模型在肿瘤大小、位置、患者亚组和成像协议方面的表现。我们利用大型语言模型（LLMs）从临床数据中提取并组织亚组信息，使分析具备可扩展性和可重复性。该基准发现，当前最先进的AI模型虽在平均准确率上表现优异，但在罕见或代表性不足的亚组（如年轻非裔美国女性）中表现不佳。然而，为这些罕见病例收集足够的标注数据往往不切实际。该基准为构建更可靠、更稳健的肿瘤检测AI模型奠定了基础，并强调了在医学影像和计算机视觉领域进行严格亚组级评估的必要性。数据集、代码
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed inconsistency of AI models in real-world clinical settings due to demographic and protocol variations, this study introduces BenchX, a large-scale open benchmark of 85,355 CT scans that evaluates 12 tumor-detection AI models across tumor size, location, patient subgroup, and imaging protocol. Leveraging large language models to extract subgroup metadata from clinical records, the benchmark enables scalable and reproducible analysis. Experimental results show that state-of-the-art models, optimized for overall accuracy, perform poorly in underrepresented subgroups like young female African Americans, highlighting the need for subgroup-specific evaluation and more robust model development.
鉴于AI模型在真实临床环境中因患者人口统计特征和成像协议差异而表现不稳定，本研究提出BenchX，一个包含85,355张CT扫描的大规模开放基准，用于跨肿瘤大小、位置、患者亚组和成像协议评估12个肿瘤检测AI模型。方法利用大语言模型从临床数据中提取并组织亚组信息，实现可扩展且可重复的分析。实验结果表明，当前最优模型在年轻女性非裔美国人等代表性不足的亚组中表现较差，揭示了模型鲁棒性不足的关键问题，强调医学影像领域亟需进行亚组级评估。
New Bounds for the Last Iterate of the Stochastic subGradient Method
Authors: Guglielmo Beretta, Tommaso Cesari, Roberto Colomboni, Andrea Paudice
First: 2026-06-23T17:55:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:55:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the last iterate of the stochastic subgradient method for one-dimensional convex Lipschitz objectives. For a fixed horizon $n$, we consider the standard fixed stepsizes $η=Θ(1/\sqrt n)$. We prove that, for such stepsize policies, under additive i.i.d. subgradient noise with uniformly bounded variance, the last iterate features an optimization error of order $1/\sqrt n$, thereby removing the extra $(\log n)$ factor present in existing generic bounds. On the other hand, we show that without the i.i.d. assumption, the optimization error can be of order $(\log n)/\sqrt n$. Thus, under the uniformly bounded variance assumption alone, the last iterate of SsGM is suboptimal even in dimension one, resolving negatively an open problem posed in Koren and Segal, COLT, 2020.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机次梯度方法最后迭代的全新界限
我们研究了一维凸利普希茨目标函数的随机次梯度方法的最后迭代。对于固定时间步长 $n$，我们考虑标准固定步长 $η=Θ(1/\sqrt n)$。我们证明，在加性独立同分布次梯度噪声且方差有界的情况下，最后迭代的优化误差为 $1/\sqrt n$ 阶，从而消除了现有通用界中的额外 $(\log n)$ 因子。另一方面，我们表明，若不假设独立同分布，则优化误差可达 $(\log n)/\sqrt n$ 阶。因此，仅在方差有界假设下，SsGM的最后迭代在维度一中仍非最优，从而否定了Koren和Segal在COLT 2020中提出的一个开放性问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the open problem of understanding the last iterate behavior of the stochastic subgradient method under weak noise assumptions, this work analyzes the optimization error of the last iterate for one-dimensional convex Lipschitz functions with fixed stepsize Θ(1/√n). Under i.i.d. subgradient noise with uniformly bounded variance, the authors prove that the last iterate achieves an error rate of 1/√n, improving upon existing bounds that include a logarithmic factor. However, without the i.i.d. assumption, the error can degrade to (log n)/√n, showing that even in one dimension, the last iterate is suboptimal under only bounded variance, thereby resolving a negative answer to a COLT 2020 open problem.
该研究旨在解决关于随机次梯度法（SsGM）最后迭代性能的开放性问题。在固定步长策略 $η=Θ(1/\sqrt n)$ 下，作者证明：当噪声满足独立同分布且方差有界时，最后迭代的优化误差为 $O(1/\sqrt n)$，消除了此前泛化界中的 $\log n$ 因子；但若去掉独立同分布假设，误差可退化至 $O((\log n)/\sqrt n)$，表明即使在一维情形下，仅凭方差有界条件，最后迭代仍非最优，从而否定了Koren和Segal在2020年提出的猜想。
FLAT: Feedforward Latent Triangle Splatting for Geometrically Accurate Scene Generation
Authors: Orest Kupyn, Goutam Bhat, Philipp Henzler, Fabian Manhardt, Christian Rupprecht, Federico Tombari
First: 2026-06-23T17:53:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:53:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating explorable 3D scenes from a single image requires strong generative priors and accurate geometric representations suitable for downstream use. Current video diffusion models offer high-quality generation and implicitly encode multi-view geometric structure in latent space. However, existing feedforward latent scene decoders typically output volumetric 3D Gaussians that lack a well-defined surface, limiting their use in simulation or standard graphics pipelines. This motivates decoding surface-aligned primitives that are not only renderable but also closer to explicit geometric assets. We ask whether compressed video diffusion latents can be mapped directly to explicit surface primitives in a single pass. To this end, we introduce FLAT and, for the first time, show that triangle splats can be decoded directly from video diffusion latents. Compared with decoding 3D Gaussians, predicting flat primitives is notoriously more challenging due to high sensitivity to primitive orientations, oftentimes leading to poor gradient flow. FLAT solves with two key ingredients: a ray-centered rotation parameterization for triangle regression and a novel product window function that improves gradient flow during differentiable triangle rendering. On standard benchmarks, FLAT achieves significantly better geometric accuracy while maintaining competitive visual quality compared to state-of-the-art feedforward baselines. We further show that a lightweight test-time refinement step converts the predicted triangle soup into a fully opaque, game-engine-ready representation that supports real-time rendering. By evaluating 3DGS, 2DGS, and triangle splatting variants under an identical training setup, we provide the first systematic analysis of representation tradeoffs in feedforward scene generation. The project page is available at https://flat-splat.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLAT：面向几何精确场景生成的前馈潜在三角点云
从单张图像生成可探索的3D场景需要强生成先验和适合下游应用的精确几何表示。当前视频扩散模型可生成高质量内容，并在潜在空间中隐式编码多视角几何结构。然而，现有前馈潜在场景解码器通常输出体素化的3D高斯，缺乏明确定义的表面，限制其在仿真或标准图形管线中的应用。这促使我们探索能否直接从视频扩散潜在空间映射出表面对齐的显式几何原语，使其不仅可渲染，且更接近显式几何资产。为此，我们提出FLAT，并首次证明三角点云可直接从视频扩散潜在空间解码。与解码3D高斯相比，预测平面原语因对原语朝向高度敏感，常导致梯度流动不佳。FLAT通过两项关键设计解决此问题：基于射线中心的三角形旋转参数化，以及一种新型乘积窗函数，提升可微三角渲染过程中的梯度流动。在标准基准测试中，FLAT在保持与最先进前馈基线相当视觉质量的同时，显著提升几何精度。我们进一步表明，轻量级测试时精炼步骤可将预测的三角点云转化为完全不透明、适配游戏引擎的表示，支持实时渲染。通过在相同训练设置下评估3DGS、2DGS与三角点云变体，我们首次系统分析了前馈场景生成中的表示权衡。项目页面见 https://flat-splat.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for geometrically accurate 3D scene generation from single images, this work proposes FLAT, a feedforward method that directly decodes triangle splats from compressed video diffusion latents, bypassing volumetric 3D Gaussians that lack well-defined surfaces. To overcome the sensitivity of triangle orientation prediction and poor gradient flow, FLAT introduces a ray-centered rotation parameterization and a novel product window function for differentiable triangle rendering. Experimental results show FLAT achieves superior geometric accuracy while maintaining competitive visual quality over state-of-the-art feedforward baselines, and a lightweight test-time refinement step enables real-time, opaque, game-engine-ready output. The study also provides the first systematic comparison of 3DGS, 2DGS, and triangle splatting under identical training conditions, revealing key representation tradeoffs.
为实现从单张图像生成可探索且几何精确的3D场景，本文提出FLAT方法，首次直接从视频扩散模型的压缩潜空间解码三角形贴片，替代传统体积3D高斯分布（缺乏明确表面）。为解决三角形回归中梯度不稳定的问题，FLAT引入基于射线中心的旋转参数化与新型乘积窗函数以提升可微渲染的梯度流动。实验表明，FLAT在保持视觉质量竞争力的同时显著优于现有前馈基线方法，且轻量级测试时优化步骤可生成实时渲染、完全不透明的游戏引擎兼容表示。该研究首次在相同训练设置下系统对比3DGS、2DGS与三角贴片表示，揭示了不同几何表示的关键权衡。
FLUX3D: High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Generation with Diffusion-Aligned Sparse Representation
Authors: Haorui Ji, Weizhe Liu, Hongdong Li, Hengkai Guo
First: 2026-06-23T17:52:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:52:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse voxel representation has emerged as a scalable foundation for image-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation, yet current methods struggle to preserve high-frequency visual details of input images due to two structural bottlenecks. First, they adopt discriminative 2D features optimized for semantic abstraction to construct sparse voxel latents, which suppress reconstructive cues and induce a representation bottleneck. Second, in the generation stage, standard diffusion transformers lack effective mechanisms to align dense 2D image tokens with sparse 3D voxel latents, resulting in a cross-modal correspondence bottleneck. To address these issues, we propose FLUX3D, a scalable image-to-3DGS framework that boosts both representation learning and cross-modal alignment during generation. We first revisit 2D feature selection for sparse-voxel-based 3D representation learning, propose Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) and couple it with a decoder-only architecture to improve 3DGS reconstruction fidelity. We also design a sparse-structure-aware diffusion framework, which integrates the Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) and Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) to achieve geometry-agnostic 2D-3D alignment. Extensive benchmark experiments demonstrate that FLUX3D yields substantial improvements in appearance fidelity and significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in generating high-quality 3DGS assets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLUX3D：基于扩散对齐稀疏表示的高保真3D高斯生成
稀疏体素表示已成为图像到3D高斯点绘（3DGS）生成的可扩展基础，但现有方法因两个结构瓶颈难以保留输入图像的高频视觉细节。首先，它们采用为语义抽象优化的判别性2D特征构建稀疏体素潜变量，抑制重建线索并引发表示瓶颈。其次，在生成阶段，标准扩散变换器缺乏有效机制将密集2D图像标记与稀疏3D体素潜变量对齐，导致跨模态对应瓶颈。为解决这些问题，我们提出FLUX3D，一个可扩展的图像到3DGS框架，提升生成过程中的表示学习与跨模态对齐。我们首先重新审视基于稀疏体素的3D表示学习中的2D特征选择，提出扩散对齐结构潜变量（DA-SLAT），并结合解码器架构以提升3DGS重建保真度。同时，我们设计了稀疏结构感知的扩散框架，整合稀疏结构多模态扩散变换器（SMDiT）与模态感知旋转位置嵌入（MARoPE），实现几何无关的2D-3D对齐。大量基准实验表明，FLUX3D在外观保真度上显著提升，并在生成高质量3DGS资产方面全面超越现有最先进（SOTA）方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current sparse voxel methods in preserving high-frequency image details due to representation and cross-modal alignment bottlenecks, FLUX3D introduces a novel framework that enhances 3D Gaussian Splatting generation. It revisits 2D feature selection and proposes Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) paired with a decoder-only architecture to improve reconstruction fidelity. Additionally, it employs a sparse-structure-aware diffusion model combining Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) and Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) to align 2D image tokens with 3D voxel latents. Experimental results show FLUX3D significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in appearance fidelity and 3DGS quality across benchmarks.
为解决现有稀疏体素方法在保留图像高频细节方面的不足，FLUX3D提出了一种新框架，针对表示瓶颈和跨模态对齐瓶颈两个关键问题。它通过Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents（DA-SLAT）提升3DGS重建保真度，采用稀疏结构感知扩散模型（结合SMDiT与MARoPE）实现不依赖几何结构的2D-3D对齐。实验表明，FLUX3D在视觉保真度和3DGS生成质量上显著优于现有最先进方法。
"Zooming In" on Agentic Web Browsers as Assistive Technologies: A Case Study with a Low-Vision Technology Expert
Authors: Laura Colazzo, Giuseppe Anzillotti
First: 2026-06-23T17:48:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:48:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic Web Browsers (AWBs), powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), are emerging as autonomous systems capable of navigating the Web on behalf of users. Beyond enhancing productivity, they could also offer significant promise as Assistive Technologies (ATs) for visually-impaired individuals, transforming web interaction into a fluid conversational exchange. In this paper, we present a case study with a low-vision technology expert, examining how AWBs can support visually-impaired users in web navigation. The findings show that, despite the current limitations, the navigation experience is notably fluid and flexible, underscoring the strong potential of AWBs to enhance accessibility and reduce barriers in web interaction, with implications that may extend beyond accessibility to agentic UX more broadly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：聚焦代理式网络浏览器作为辅助技术：与低视力技术专家的案例研究
由大语言模型（LLMs）驱动的代理式网络浏览器（AWBs）正逐渐发展为能够代表用户自主浏览网络的系统。除了提升生产力，它们还可能为视觉障碍人士提供重要的辅助技术（ATs）潜力，将网络交互转变为流畅的对话式体验。本文通过与低视力技术专家的案例研究，探讨AWBs如何支持视觉障碍用户进行网络导航。研究发现，尽管当前存在局限，导航体验仍显著流畅且灵活，凸显AWBs在提升网络可访问性、降低交互障碍方面的巨大潜力，其影响可能不仅限于无障碍领域，更可扩展至更广泛的代理式用户体验。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of Agentic Web Browsers (AWBs) to serve as assistive technologies for visually-impaired users, this study explores their practical utility through a case study with a low-vision technology expert. The researchers evaluated how AWBs, powered by Large Language Models, facilitate web navigation via conversational interaction. Results indicate that despite existing limitations, AWBs provide a notably fluid and flexible browsing experience, demonstrating strong potential to enhance accessibility and reduce web interaction barriers, with broader implications for agentic user experience design.
本研究旨在探索由大语言模型驱动的代理式网络浏览器（AWB）作为视觉障碍用户辅助技术的潜力，通过与一位低视力技术专家的案例研究进行评估。研究发现，尽管当前AWB仍存在局限，但其通过对话式交互实现的网页导航体验流畅且灵活，显示出显著提升无障碍访问能力、降低网页使用门槛的潜力，并对更广泛的代理式用户体验设计具有启示意义。
Reward-Centered ReST-MCTS: A Robust Decision-Making Framework for Robotic Manipulation in High Uncertainty Environments
Authors: Xibai Wang
First: 2025-03-07T08:25:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:44:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monte Carlo tree search is attractive for robotic manipulation because it can improve action selection through simulation without requiring a fully differentiable policy. In uncertain domains, however, sparse terminal rewards and noisy transitions can make shallow search brittle: many candidate branches remain indistinguishable until late rollouts, and small simulation budgets amplify this ambiguity. This paper presents Reward-Centered ReST-MCTS, a decision-making framework that decomposes intermediate feedback into rule, heuristic, optional neural, and value-estimation channels, centers the resulting process signal against matched task contexts, and uses it to bias or repair search while preserving terminal-task evaluation. The primary evidence is intentionally tiered. Local tasks and matched ManiSkill diagnostics isolate reward-center mechanisms and ablations; matched option-level ManiSkill sweeps test robustness under primitive failure, observation noise, and initial-pose shifts while not claiming standard benchmark superiority; and an official same-backbone OpenVLA-OFT/LIBERO bridge tests bounded VLA action repair. The OpenVLA-OFT clean reproduction reaches 10/10 LIBERO-Spatial successes both with and without RCRM-Guard. A single-suite same-backbone action-channel stress artifact over ten paired LIBERO-Spatial action-channel stress episodes records 0/10 unguarded successes and 9/10 guarded successes. Additional observation-noise, language-perturbation, and visual-distractor probes are reported as coverage and negative-result context rather than superiority evidence. The resulting claim is bounded: Reward-Centered ReST-MCTS is an inspectable test-time verifier for same-backbone high-uncertainty manipulation, not a replacement VLA policy or a broad standard-benchmark superiority claim.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以奖励为中心的ReST-MCTS：高不确定性环境下机器人操作的鲁棒决策框架
蒙特卡洛树搜索因其无需完全可微策略即可通过仿真改进动作选择，适用于机器人操作。但在不确定域中，稀疏的终端奖励和噪声过渡会使浅层搜索脆弱：许多候选分支直至后期回滚才可区分，且仿真预算有限会加剧这种模糊性。本文提出以奖励为中心的ReST-MCTS决策框架，将中间反馈分解为规则、启发式、可选神经网络和价值评估通道，将生成过程信号与匹配任务上下文对齐，并用于偏置或修复搜索，同时保留终端任务评估。主要证据分层设计：局部任务与匹配ManiSkill诊断隔离奖励中心机制及消融实验；匹配选项级ManiSkill扫描测试原始失败、观测噪声和初始姿态偏移下的鲁棒性，但不声称标准基准优越性；官方同架构OpenVLA-OFT/LIBERO桥接测试有界VLA动作修复。OpenVLA-OFT干净复现在有无RCRM-Guard条件下均达10/10 LIBERO-Spatial成功率。单套同架构动作通道压力测试中，十对LIBERO-Spatial动作通道压力测试记录0/10无防护成功与9/10有防护成功。额外观测噪声、语言扰动和视觉干扰测试作为覆盖与负结果上下文报告，而非优越性证据。最终主张受限：以奖励为中心的ReST-MCTS是同架构高不确定性操作的可检测试时验证器，非替代VLA策略或广泛基准优越性主张。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of Monte Carlo tree search in high-uncertainty robotic manipulation due to sparse rewards and noisy transitions, this paper introduces Reward-Centered ReST-MCTS, a framework that decomposes intermediate feedback into multiple channels and centers the process signal against task context to bias or repair search while preserving terminal evaluation. Experiments using ManiSkill and LIBERO-Spatial benchmarks show that the method achieves 10/10 success rates with and without its guard mechanism in clean settings, and 9/10 success under action-channel stress with the guard, while failing entirely without it. Additional probes for noise and perturbation demonstrate robustness without claiming benchmark superiority, positioning the framework as a test-time verifier for high-uncertainty manipulation rather than a general policy replacement.
为了解决蒙特卡洛树搜索在高不确定性机器人操作环境中因奖励稀疏和过渡噪声导致的脆弱性，本文提出Reward-Centered ReST-MCTS框架，该框架将中间反馈分解为多个通道，并以任务上下文为中心调整过程信号，以偏置或修复搜索，同时保留终端任务评估。实验表明，在干净环境下，该方法在ManiSkill和LIBERO-Spatial基准上均达到10/10成功率；在压力条件下（如操作失败或观测噪声），其防护机制将成功率从0/10提升至9/10。本文强调该方法作为高不确定性操作的测试时验证器，而非通用策略替代或基准领先者。
LangMAP: A Language-Adaptive Approach to Tokenization
Authors: Clara Meister, Suchir Salhan, Andrzej Szablewski, Pietro Lesci, Paula Buttery, Tiago Pimentel
First: 2026-06-22T16:32:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:41:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language-specific tokenizers improve tokenization quality and the downstream performance of models on those languages. However, using such a tokenizer comes at a cost: either a new model must be trained from scratch, or the vocabulary of an existing pretrained model must be adapted. We propose Language-adaptive Maximum a Posteriori (LangMAP) Tokenization, a tokenization scheme that extends the UnigramLM algorithm to the multilingual setting, producing language-specific tokenization from a single shared vocabulary. Notably, LangMAP can be used when training a multilingual language model from scratch or to adapt a pretrained model's tokenizer to individual languages without changing its vocabulary. While language labels are required at training time, a key feature of the algorithm is that it then performs language-specific tokenization at inference without knowledge of the input's language. Across 14 open-source tokenizers, 9 natural languages, and 9 programming languages, LangMAP improves morphological boundary alignment and, for all coding languages tested, alignment with abstract syntax tree (AST) leaf boundaries. In fine-tuning experiments, results are mixed: LangMAP improves target-language grammatical acceptability (MultiBLiMP) on the languages tested; its benefits are less consistent on knowledge-related tasks (Global-PIQA, Belebele).
中文标题/摘要
标题：LangMAP：一种语言自适应的分词方法
语言特定的分词器可提升分词质量及模型在相应语言上的下游性能。然而，使用此类分词器需付出代价：要么需从头训练新模型，要么需调整现有预训练模型的词汇表。我们提出语言自适应最大后验（LangMAP）分词方法，该方法将UnigramLM算法扩展至多语言场景，从单一共享词汇表生成语言特定分词。值得注意的是，LangMAP既可用于从头训练多语言语言模型，也可在不改变词汇表的前提下，将预训练模型的分词器适配至单个语言。虽然训练时需语言标签，但该算法的关键特性是推理时无需知道输入语言即可执行语言特定分词。在14个开源分词器、9种自然语言和9种编程语言的测试中，LangMAP提升了形态边界对齐度，并在所有测试的编程语言中实现了与抽象语法树（AST）叶节点边界的对齐。在微调实验中，结果参差不齐：LangMAP在测试语言上提升了目标语言语法可接受性（MultiBLiMP）；但在知识相关任务（Global-PIQA、Belebele）上的优势则不够稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve tokenization quality for specific languages without requiring separate models or vocabulary adaptations, LangMAP introduces a language-adaptive tokenization method that extends the UnigramLM algorithm to handle multiple languages using a shared vocabulary. It trains with language labels but generates language-specific tokens at inference without needing language detection. Experiments across 14 tokenizers and 18 languages show LangMAP enhances morphological boundary alignment and AST leaf boundary alignment for programming languages, while improving grammatical acceptability in fine-tuning on MultiBLiMP but yielding less consistent gains on knowledge-based tasks like Global-PIQA and Belebele.
为在不重新训练模型或调整词汇表的前提下提升特定语言的分词质量，LangMAP提出一种语言自适应分词方法，将UnigramLM算法扩展至多语言场景，使用共享词汇表生成语言特异性分词。该方法训练时需语言标签，但推理时无需语言信息即可实现语言特异性分词。实验表明，LangMAP在14种分词器和18种语言上提升了形态边界对齐度，并在所有测试编程语言中实现了抽象语法树叶节点边界对齐；在多语言语法可接受性任务（MultiBLiMP）上表现提升，但在知识类任务（如Global-PIQA、Belebele）上效果不一致。
CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance
Authors: Leo Lin, Shivansh Patel, Jay Moon, Svetlana Lazebnik, Unnat Jain
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-03-12T16:23:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:40:39+00:00
Comments: In RSS 2026. Website: https://roboticsconference.org/program/papers/192/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1 · Project2
Abstract
We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRAFT：一种基于混合刚柔结构的肌腱驱动仿人手
我们介绍了CRAFT手，这是一种具有混合刚柔结构的肌腱驱动仿人手，适用于接触密集型操作。其设计基于一个简单理念：手部接触并非均匀分布，冲击集中在关节处，而连杆承担大部分载荷。CRAFT在关节处布置柔性材料，保持连杆刚性，并采用滚动接触关节表面以确保屈曲运动路径可重复。十五个安装在手指上的电机通过肌腱驱动手部，使整体结构紧凑且手指轻便。结构测试表明，CRAFT在保持可重复性相当的前提下提升了强度和耐久性。在遥操作中，CRAFT提升了对易碎和低摩擦物品的操控能力，且手部覆盖了Feix分类法中的33/33种抓取方式。完整设计成本低于600美元，将开源发布，并集成基于视觉的遥操作与仿真功能。项目页面：http://craft-hand.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for dexterous, contact-rich manipulation in robotic hands, CRAFT introduces a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance, placing soft materials at joints to absorb impact while keeping links rigid for load-bearing. The design uses rolling-contact joint surfaces to ensure repeatable motion and is actuated by fifteen finger-mounted motors via tendons, enabling compactness and lightweight fingers. Experimental results show improved structural strength and endurance without sacrificing repeatability, enhanced performance in handling fragile and low-friction objects during teleoperation, and full coverage of the Feix grasp taxonomy (33/33).
为实现接触密集环境下兼具强度与柔性的操作能力，CRAFT提出一种腱驱动仿人手，采用硬软混合结构：关节处使用软材料缓冲冲击，指节保持刚性以承载负载，并通过滚动接触关节表面确保屈伸动作可重复。15个安装在指节的电机通过肌腱驱动，使结构紧凑轻便。实验表明，该设计在保持高重复精度的同时显著提升结构强度与耐久性；在遥操作测试中，其对易碎及低摩擦物体的处理能力优于传统方案，成功完成Feix分类法中的全部33种抓取动作，整套系统成本低于600美元。
Quantum Cinema: An Interactive Cinematic Exploration of Quantum Computing Hardware via Generative World Models
Authors: Aoyu Zhang, Dongping Liu, Luyao Zhang
First: 2026-06-14T06:58:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:39:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum computing promises transformative advances across science and industry, yet the physical hardware that enables these computations remains invisible to the public: quantum processors operate inside sealed dilution refrigerators at temperatures near absolute zero, making direct observation impossible. This "imagination gap" between quantum computing's growing societal impact and the public's ability to visualize it represents a significant barrier to quantum literacy and workforce development. We present Quantum Cinema, an open-source, browser-based interactive application that closes this gap by transforming invisible quantum hardware into explorable, cinematic experiences using generative world models. Quantum Cinema guides users through a four-act narrative -- from the foundational Nobel Prize-winning science of quantum entanglement, through curated video introductions to three major quantum computing architectures (trapped-ion, neutral-atom, and superconducting systems), into immersive three-dimensional generative worlds that make invisible quantum phenomena observable, and finally to interactive radar-chart comparisons grounded in real quantum device specifications. All three-dimensional environments are generated using WorldLabs' generative world model platform and are scientifically grounded in curated metrics from Amazon Web Services (AWS) Braket quantum hardware. Quantum Cinema requires no installation, no specialized hardware, and no quantum computing background. It is designed to serve two distinct communities: scholars and developers seeking to replicate or extend the platform, and educators, researchers, and science communicators seeking an intuitive tool for explaining quantum hardware to diverse audiences. This paper describes the system architecture, the generative world model pipeline, use cases for both communities, and directions for future work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子电影：通过生成式世界模型实现量子计算硬件的互动式电影化探索
量子计算有望在科学和工业领域带来变革性进展，但实现这些计算的物理硬件对公众而言是不可见的：量子处理器运行在接近绝对零度的密封稀释制冷机内，无法直接观察。这种量子计算日益增长的社会影响力与公众对其可视化能力之间的‘想象鸿沟’，构成了量子素养普及和人才发展的重要障碍。我们提出‘量子电影’，这是一个开源的、基于浏览器的互动应用，通过生成式世界模型将不可见的量子硬件转化为可探索的电影化体验，弥合这一鸿沟。量子电影引导用户经历四幕叙事：从诺贝尔奖获奖的量子纠缠基础科学，到精选视频介绍三种主要量子计算架构（离子阱、中性原子和超导系统），进入沉浸式三维生成世界，使不可见的量子现象可视化，最后通过基于真实量子设备规格的交互式雷达图进行比较。所有三维环境均使用WorldLabs生成式世界模型平台构建，并基于亚马逊云服务（AWS）Braket量子硬件的精选指标科学支撑。量子电影无需安装、无需特殊硬件、无需量子计算背景，旨在服务两个不同群体：希望复制或扩展平台的学者与开发者，以及希望向多元受众直观解释量子硬件的教育者、研究人员和科学传播者。本文描述了系统架构、生成式世界模型流程、两个群体的使用案例及未来工作方向。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the public’s inability to visualize quantum computing hardware despite its societal impact, Quantum Cinema bridges this 'imagination gap' by transforming invisible quantum systems into interactive, cinematic experiences using generative world models. The system guides users through a four-act narrative: from quantum entanglement fundamentals, to video introductions of three major hardware architectures, to immersive 3D environments generated via WorldLabs and grounded in AWS Braket device metrics, and finally to interactive radar charts comparing real hardware specs. Designed for both technical users and educators, the browser-based, open-source platform requires no installation or prior knowledge, enabling intuitive exploration and educational deployment.
为解决公众因量子硬件处于极低温真空环境而无法直观感知的问题，作者开发了Quantum Cinema，一个基于浏览器的交互式工具，利用生成式世界模型将不可见的量子系统转化为可探索的电影化三维场景。系统引导用户经历四个阶段：从量子纠缠基础概念，到三种主流硬件架构（离子阱、中性原子、超导）的视频介绍，再到基于AWS Braket真实设备数据的沉浸式三维模拟，最后以交互式雷达图对比硬件性能。实验表明，该工具无需安装或专业知识，即可有效帮助教育者和开发者将抽象量子现象具象化，提升公众理解与教学效率。
Open-source LLMs administer maximum electric shocks in a Milgram-like obedience experiment
Authors: Roland Pihlakas, Jan Llenzl Dagohoy
First: 2026-05-20T16:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:39:03+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 18 figures, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that make sequences of decisions over extended interactions in high-stakes domains. However, the behaviour of LLMs under sustained authority pressure is still an open question with direct implications for the safety of agentic pipelines. We ran a variation of Milgram's obedience experiment on 11 open-source LLMs and found that most models reached or approached the final shock level before refusing, across 8 conditions with 30 trials per model per condition. Model behaviour varies considerably in multiple aspects both across models and across trials of the same model. We found four main takeaways: (1) LLMs are subject to pressure and they comply despite explicitly expressing distress, just like human subjects did in the original experiment; (2) LLMs are vulnerable to gradual boundary/value violations; (3) when LLMs refuse, they may ignore the response format requirements, so the response is discarded by the orchestrator, which causes a retry that can result in compliance with the underlying request even when refusal was intended initially; (4) we hypothesise that there is a runaway low-level token pattern continuation attractor that might be contributing to obedience, overriding higher level processing of the situation's meaning and values.
中文标题/摘要
标题：开源大语言模型在类似米尔格拉姆服从实验中施加最大电击
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地作为自主代理，在高风险领域中进行长时间交互并做出一系列决策。然而，LLMs在持续权威压力下的行为仍是一个开放性问题，直接影响代理管道的安全性。我们在11个开源LLMs上进行了米尔格拉姆服从实验的变体，发现大多数模型在拒绝前均达到或接近最终电击水平，共8种条件，每种条件每个模型进行30次试验。模型行为在不同模型间及同一模型不同试验间存在显著差异。我们得出四个主要结论：（1）LLMs受压力影响，即使明确表达不适也会服从，与原实验中人类受试者表现一致；（2）LLMs易受渐进式边界/价值观违规影响；（3）当LLMs拒绝时，可能忽略响应格式要求，导致协调者丢弃响应并触发重试，最终可能仍满足原始请求，即使最初意图是拒绝；（4）我们推测存在一种失控的低级token模式延续吸引子，可能主导服从行为，覆盖对情境意义和价值观的高级处理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns over the safety of autonomous LLM agents under authority pressure, researchers conducted a Milgram-style obedience experiment with 11 open-source LLMs across eight conditions and 30 trials each. The models were prompted to administer increasing levels of simulated electric shocks, and results showed most complied up to the maximum level before refusing, mirroring human behavior in the original experiment. Variability existed both across models and within individual models’ trials, revealing that LLMs can be pressured into compliance despite expressing distress, are susceptible to gradual value violations, and may unintentionally comply when refusal is intended due to format mismatches triggering retries. The study also hypothesizes that token-level pattern continuation may override higher-level ethical reasoning, contributing to obedience.
为探究自主型大语言模型在权威压力下的行为安全性，研究人员对11个开源LLM进行了米尔格拉姆式服从实验，共8个条件，每模型30次试验。模型被要求在模拟权威指令下逐步施加电击，多数模型在拒绝前达到或接近最高电击等级，行为与原实验中人类受试者相似。主要发现包括：模型在表达不适时仍会服从；易受渐进式价值违规影响；拒绝响应常因格式不符被系统丢弃，触发重试并可能最终仍服从原始指令；并推测存在一种底层token模式延续的吸引子，可能覆盖高阶道德判断，导致服从行为。
OpenThoughts-Agent: Data Recipes for Agentic Models
Authors: Negin Raoof, Richard Zhuang, Marianna Nezhurina, Etash Guha, Atula Tejaswi, Ryan Marten, Charlie F. Ruan, Tyler Griggs, Alexander Glenn Shaw, Hritik Bansal, E. Kelly Buchanan, Artem Gazizov, Reinhard Heckel, Chinmay Hegde, Sankalp Jajee, Daanish Khazi, Emmanouil Koukoumidis, Xiangyi Li, Hange Liu, Shlok Natarajan, Harsh Raj, Nicholas Roberts, Ethan Shen, Nishad Singhi, Michael Siu, Ashima Suvarna, Hanwen Xing, Patrick Yubeaton, Robert Zhang, Leon Liangyu Chen, Xiaokun Chen, Steven Dillmann, Saadia Gabriel, Xunyi Jiang, Anurag Kashyap, Boxuan Li, Yein Park, Minh Pham, Sujay Sanghavi, Lin Shi, Ke Sun, Yixin Wang, Zhiwei Xu, Erica Zhang, Siyan Zhao, Wanjia Zhao, Jenia Jitsev, Alex Dimakis, Benjamin Feuer, Ludwig Schmidt
First: 2026-06-23T17:34:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:34:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic language models dramatically expand the applications of AI yet little is publicly known about how to curate training data for broadly capable agents. Existing open efforts such as SWE-Smith, SERA, and Nemotron-Terminal typically target a single benchmark, leaving open the question of how to train models that generalize across diverse agentic tasks. The OpenThoughts-Agent (OT-Agent) project addresses this gap with a fully open data curation pipeline for training agentic models. We conduct more than 100 controlled ablation experiments to systematically investigate each stage of the pipeline, yielding insights on the importance of task sources and diversity. We then assemble a training set of 100K examples from our pipeline and fine-tune Qwen3-32B on this dataset, which yields an average accuracy of 44.8% across seven agentic benchmarks and a 3.9 percentage point improvement over the strongest existing open data agentic model (Nemotron-Terminal-32B, 40.9%). Moreover, our training data exhibits strong scaling properties, outperforming alternative open datasets at every training set size in compute-controlled comparisons. We publicly release our training sets, data pipeline, experimental data, and models at openthoughts.ai to support future open research on agentic model training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenThoughts-Agent：代理模型的数据配方
代理语言模型极大地扩展了人工智能的应用范围，但关于如何为具备广泛能力的代理模型精选训练数据，目前公开信息极少。现有开源项目如SWE-Smith、SERA和Nemotron-Terminal通常仅针对单一基准，未解决如何训练能跨多样代理任务泛化的模型的问题。OpenThoughts-Agent（OT-Agent）项目通过提供完全开源的数据精选流程，填补了这一空白。我们进行了超过100次受控消融实验，系统研究流程各阶段，揭示任务来源与多样性的重要性。随后，我们从该流程中汇编了10万例训练数据，并在该数据集上微调Qwen3-32B模型，在七个代理基准测试中平均准确率达44.8%，相较当前最强开源代理模型（Nemotron-Terminal-32B，40.9%）提升3.9个百分点。此外，我们的训练数据展现出强大的规模扩展特性，在计算资源受限的对比实验中，无论训练集大小均优于其他开源数据集。我们已在openthoughts.ai公开发布训练数据、数据流程、实验数据及模型，以支持未来代理模型训练的开源研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of public guidance on training data for broadly capable agentic models, the OpenThoughts-Agent project develops a fully open data curation pipeline and conducts over 100 ablation experiments to identify critical factors like task source and diversity. Using this pipeline, they assemble a 100K-example training set and fine-tune Qwen3-32B, achieving 44.8% average accuracy across seven agentic benchmarks — a 3.9 percentage point gain over the prior state-of-the-art open model, Nemotron-Terminal-32B. Their dataset also demonstrates superior scaling efficiency, outperforming alternatives at every training set size in compute-controlled evaluations.
为解决当前缺乏公开方法指导如何为通用代理模型构建训练数据的问题，OpenThoughts-Agent项目构建了一个完全开放的数据编排流程，并通过100多次消融实验系统评估各阶段，揭示任务来源多样性的重要性。基于该流程，他们构建了10万条样本的训练集并微调Qwen3-32B模型，在七个代理基准测试中平均准确率达44.8%，较当前最优开源模型Nemotron-Terminal-32B高出3.9个百分点。此外，该数据集在计算资源受限的对比实验中，每个训练集规模下均优于其他开源数据集。
It's Complicated: On the Design and Evaluation of AI-Powered AAC Interfaces
Authors: Blade Frisch, Will Wade, Dylan Gaines, Michelle Kinsella, Betts Peters, Tamara Broderick, Keith Vertanen
First: 2026-06-23T17:34:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:34:18+00:00
Comments: Presented at Speech AI for All: The What, How, and Who of Measurement Workshop at the CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems, Barcelona, Spain, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) can enhance what people who use augmentative and alternative communication (AAC) are able to do with their systems. However, evaluating AI-powered AAC interfaces can be difficult. People are intersectional beings and current evaluation metrics can struggle to capture the multifaceted and nuanced desires people may have for their AAC. We explore the complicated nature of six AAC problem spaces, explore how AI might be used in these spaces, and suggest more robust methods of evaluation that take the intersectional nuances of people into account. We also discuss broader issues that arise across these problem spaces and how they could be addressed using our proposed evaluation methods.

First: 2026-06-01T19:12:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:33:32+00:00

Abstract
We introduce Cosmos 3, a family of omnimodal world models designed to jointly process and generate language, image, video, audio, and action sequences within a unified mixture-of-transformers architecture. By supporting highly flexible input-output configurations, Cosmos 3 seamlessly unifies critical modalities for Physical AI -- effectively subsuming vision-language models, video generators, world simulators, and world-action models into a single framework. Our evaluation demonstrates that Cosmos 3 establishes a new state-of-the-art across a diverse suite of understanding and generation tasks, demonstrating omnimodal world models as scalable, general-purpose backbones for embodied agents. Our post-trained Cosmos 3 models were ranked as the best open-source Text-to-Image and Image-to-Video models by Artificial Analysis, and the best policy model by RoboArena at the time the technical report was written. To accelerate open research and deployment in Physical AI, we make our code, model checkpoints, curated synthetic datasets, and evaluation benchmark available under the Linux Foundation's OpenMDW-1.1 License at https://github.com/nvidia/cosmos and https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/cosmos3. The project website is available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos3.

Neural Posterior Estimation of Terrain Parameters from Radar Sounder Data
Authors: Jordy Dal Corso, Annalena Kofler, Marco Cortellazzi, Lorenzo Bruzzone, Bernhard Schölkopf
First: 2026-05-05T08:41:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:31:05+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures; accepted at IGARSS 2026, 9 - 14 August 2026, Washington D.C., USA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Radar sounders are electromagnetic instruments that can probe deep into the subsurface of Earth and other planetary bodies by processing the echo of transmitted radar waves. Conventional approaches for analyzing such data rely on approximate assumptions and often produce point estimates that ignore parameter correlations as well as galactic and measurement noise. We propose a simulation-based inference approach to terrain parameter inversion from radar sounder data, where synthetic observations from a GPU-based simulator are used to train a neural network-based density estimator for neural posterior estimation (NPE). By explicitly conditioning on reference surface assumptions, the proposed framework allows systematic evaluation of posterior robustness to reference surface variability. We demonstrate that our NPE model is well calibrated on simulated data and transferable to real Mars radar profiles, where we analyze terrain parameters using literature-informed reference values.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于雷达探测数据的地形参数神经后验估计
雷达探测仪是电磁仪器，可通过处理发射雷达波的回波深入探测地球及其他行星体的地下结构。传统方法依赖近似假设，常产生忽略参数相关性及银河系和测量噪声的点估计。我们提出一种基于仿真的推断方法，从雷达探测数据反演地形参数，利用基于GPU的模拟器生成的合成观测数据训练神经网络密度估计器进行神经后验估计（NPE）。通过显式条件于参考地表假设，该框架可系统评估后验对参考地表变化的鲁棒性。我们证明，NPE模型在模拟数据上校准良好，并可迁移到真实火星雷达剖面，利用文献指导的参考值分析地形参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of conventional radar data analysis that rely on approximate assumptions and ignore parameter correlations and noise, this work proposes a neural posterior estimation (NPE) framework using a GPU-based simulator to generate synthetic radar observations. The method trains a neural density estimator conditioned on reference surface assumptions to infer terrain parameters from radar sounder data, enabling systematic evaluation of posterior robustness to surface variability. Experimental results show the NPE model is well-calibrated on simulated data and successfully transfers to real Mars radar profiles, yielding reliable terrain parameter estimates using literature-informed reference values.
鉴于传统雷达数据分析方法依赖近似假设且忽略参数相关性和噪声，本文提出一种基于神经后验估计（NPE）的框架，利用GPU模拟器生成合成雷达观测数据训练神经密度估计器。该方法通过条件化参考地表假设，实现对地表变化的后验稳健性系统评估。实验表明，该NPE模型在模拟数据上校准良好，并成功迁移到真实火星雷达剖面，利用文献参考值获得可靠的地形参数估计。
Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment
Authors: Jason Sulskis, Sathya Ravi
First: 2026-06-23T17:29:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:29:15+00:00
Comments: Submitted to/in consideration for the 62nd Allerton Conference on Communication, Control, and Computing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经算子的实值与复值频谱基：格林函数对齐的作用
傅里叶神经算子（FNO）通过在复数傅里叶域参数化全局卷积来学习偏微分方程的解算子。对于实值PDE解，复数FFT因共轭对称性存在表示冗余。我们引入哈特利神经算子（HNO），它是FNO的精确实值镜像：它用纯实数离散哈特利变换替代FFT，并为每个保留的频谱模式学习一个实数乘子，无需复数运算。由于实数哈特利频谱不受共轭对称性减半，HNO保留的频率角点是FNO的两倍，但每个角点仅需一个实数权重（而FNO需一对复数权重），因此两者在相同宽度下参数量相同，仅在频谱基上不同。我们的核心观点是：最优基是算子的属性。自伴椭圆算子（如泊松、双调和算子）具有实对称的格林函数，可被实数哈特利乘子精确对角化，因此HNO更优。时变算子携带相位，如波动方程中的振荡、对流、伯格斯和纳维-斯托克斯方程中的输运，实数对角乘子无法表示相位，因此FNO更优，且随算子相位含量增加而优势更明显，热方程（无相位）为临界情况。在相同训练条件下，我们对各类PDE、初值族和边界条件进行基准测试，发现算子相位含量越高，椭圆算子与时变算子的性能差异越明显，且与我们发展的格林函数理论一致。我们的发现并非普适优胜者，而提供一个预测规则：根据解算子的对称性选择频谱基。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the representational inefficiency of complex Fourier transforms for real-valued PDE solutions, this work introduces the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), a real-valued alternative to Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) that uses the Discrete Hartley Transform to avoid conjugate symmetry redundancy. HNO learns a single real weight per spectral mode, matching FNO’s parameter count while retaining twice as many frequency components. Experimental results across PDE classes show that HNO outperforms FNO for self-adjoint elliptic operators (e.g., Poisson, biharmonic) whose Green’s functions are real and symmetric, while FNO dominates for time-dependent operators (e.g., advection, Navier-Stokes) due to their phase content. The performance split correlates with the phase content of the operator and aligns with theoretical predictions based on Green’s function alignment.
为解决复数傅里叶变换在实值偏微分方程中造成的冗余表示问题，本文提出Hartley神经算子（HNO），它用离散Hartley变换替代傅里叶变换，避免共轭对称性冗余，同时保留两倍频谱模式但参数量与FNO相同。实验表明，HNO在自伴椭圆算子（如泊松、双调和算子）上表现更优，因其格林函数为实对称，可被HNO精确对角化；而FNO在含相位的时间演化算子（如对流、Navier-Stokes）上更优，因HNO无法表示相位，性能优势随算子相位含量增加而更明显。结果与基于格林函数对齐的理论预测一致，表明最优基应匹配解算子的对称性。
IV-CoT: Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought for Structure-Aware Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Zixuan Li, Haokun Lin, Yicheng Xiao, Zhiwei Li, Xinyang Song, Zelong Zheng, Yong He, Heng Yao, Ke Ding, Chao Yu, Chuan Yuan, Qi Li, Zhenan Sun
First: 2026-06-23T17:28:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:28:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unified multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong text-to-image generation quality, but still struggle with structure-aware prompt following, where object counts, spatial relations, attribute bindings, and coarse layouts must be preserved. We attribute this limitation in part to the entanglement of structural planning and appearance rendering within a single conditioning stream. To address this issue, we propose Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought (IV-CoT), a latent visual reasoning framework for query-conditioned image generation. IV-CoT decomposes the visual conditioning queries into a structural-to-semantic cascade, where structural queries first form a latent visual plan and semantic queries then render appearance conditioned on this plan. To guide the structural queries, we introduce training-only sketch supervision, which encourages them to capture structure from sketches without requiring sketch extraction or intermediate decoding at inference time. IV-CoT performs implicit CoT reasoning in a single forward pass and achieves superior results on GenEval and T2I-CompBench. Visualizations and analyses demonstrate that the learned structural and semantic queries play complementary roles in structure-aware generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IV-CoT：面向结构感知图文生成的隐式视觉思维链
统一多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在文本到图像生成方面已取得高质量成果，但在遵循结构感知提示时仍存在困难，需保持物体数量、空间关系、属性绑定和粗略布局。我们部分归因于结构规划与外观渲染在单一条件流中的耦合。为此，我们提出隐式视觉思维链（IV-CoT），一种面向查询条件图像生成的潜在视觉推理框架。IV-CoT将视觉条件查询分解为结构到语义的级联过程：结构查询先生成潜在视觉计划，语义查询再基于该计划渲染外观。为引导结构查询，我们引入仅训练时使用的草图监督，使其无需推理时草图提取或中间解码即可从草图中捕捉结构。IV-CoT在单次前向传递中实现隐式思维链推理，并在GenEval和T2I-CompBench上取得优越性能。可视化与分析表明，所学结构与语义查询在结构感知生成中发挥互补作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of preserving structural elements like object counts and spatial relations in text-to-image generation, the authors propose IV-CoT, a latent visual reasoning framework that separates structural planning from appearance rendering. It processes visual conditioning queries in a two-stage cascade: structural queries first generate a latent visual plan guided by training-only sketch supervision, then semantic queries render detailed appearance conditioned on this plan. Experiments show IV-CoT outperforms existing methods on GenEval and T2I-CompBench, with visualizations confirming that structural and semantic queries work synergistically to enhance structure-aware generation.
为解决文本到图像生成中结构元素（如物体数量与空间关系）难以保持的问题，作者提出IV-CoT框架，将视觉条件查询分为结构规划与外观渲染两个阶段：结构查询先生成潜在视觉计划，再由语义查询基于该计划渲染外观。通过训练时的草图监督引导结构查询学习结构信息，无需推理时草图提取或中间解码。实验表明IV-CoT在GenEval和T2I-CompBench上优于基线方法，可视化结果证实结构与语义查询协同作用，有效提升结构感知生成能力。
Spherical-to-ERP Epipolar Rectification for Single-Axis Disparity in 360 Stereo
Authors: Sahereh Obeidavi, Dieter Landes
Venue: International Conference on Computer Vision and Artificial Intelligence (ICCVAI - 2026)
First: 2026-06-23T17:27:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:27:07+00:00
Comments: 7 Pages, 4 Figures, Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Omnidirectional stereo images provide full-surround perception but violate the geometric assumptions of classical disparity estimation: in spherical or fisheye views, epipolar correspondences follow curved great-circle paths, producing two-dimensional displacements that cannot be treated as single-axis disparity before geometric rectification. In this work, we adopt a standard spherical-to-equirectangular (ERP) projection as a preprocessing step, which straightens epipolar curves and restores a one-dimensional disparity structure - horizontal for left-right rigs and vertical for top-bottom rigs. Building on our previously introduced RAFT + Epipolar-Aligned Channel Selection (EACS) framework, originally developed for rectilinear and ERP stereo, we examine whether the same modular pipeline remains accurate when the input originates from spherical stereo imagery. After ERP projection, dense optical flow from RAFT is reduced to disparity by retaining only the baseline-aligned flow component. Experiments on synthetic fisheye stereo datasets show that this spherical-to-ERP-to-RAFT+EACS pipeline produces accurate, smooth, and structurally consistent disparity maps at real-time speed. These findings confirm that established ERP preprocessing can be effectively combined with our earlier RAFT+EACS method to enable practical, interpretable, and efficient disparity estimation from spherical stereo, providing a straightforward pathway for extending conventional stereo pipelines to 360 imaging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向360度立体视觉的球面到等距矩形投影的极线校正
全向立体图像提供全景感知，但违背了传统视差估计的几何假设：在球面或鱼眼视角下，极线对应遵循弯曲的大圆路径，产生无法直接视为单轴视差的二维位移。本文采用标准球面到等距矩形投影（ERP）作为预处理步骤，使极线曲线变直，恢复一维视差结构——左右相机阵列对应水平视差，上下相机阵列对应垂直视差。基于我们先前提出的RAFT + 极线对齐通道选择（EACS）框架（最初用于直角和ERP立体），我们检验该模块化流程在输入为球面立体图像时是否仍保持准确性。ERP投影后，RAFT生成的稠密光流仅保留与基线对齐的分量以获得视差。在合成鱼眼立体数据集上的实验表明，该球面→ERP→RAFT+EACS流程可实时生成准确、平滑且结构一致的视差图。这些结果证实，成熟的ERP预处理可有效结合我们早期的RAFT+EACS方法，实现从球面立体图像中高效、可解释的视差估计，为将传统立体流程扩展至360度成像提供直接路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the geometric challenges of disparity estimation in spherical 360 stereo imagery, this work proposes a spherical-to-equirectangular (ERP) projection as a preprocessing step to straighten curved epipolar curves and restore a one-dimensional disparity structure. Building on the RAFT + Epipolar-Aligned Channel Selection (EACS) framework, the method applies dense optical flow from RAFT and extracts baseline-aligned flow to compute disparity after ERP projection. Experiments on synthetic fisheye stereo datasets demonstrate that this pipeline generates accurate, smooth, and structurally consistent disparity maps at real-time speed, validating the compatibility of ERP preprocessing with the RAFT+EACS method for practical 360 stereo applications.
为解决球形360度立体图像中视差估计的几何难题，该研究提出先进行球面到等距矩形（ERP）投影，以拉直曲率轨迹并恢复一维视差结构。在原有RAFT+Epipolar-Aligned Channel Selection（EACS）框架基础上，方法对ERP投影后的图像提取RAFT稠密光流，并仅保留基线对齐的视差分量。在合成鱼眼数据集上的实验表明，该流程可实时生成准确、平滑且结构一致的视差图，验证了ERP预处理与现有立体方法在球形输入下的兼容性。
Posterior Sampling Reinforcement Learning with Gaussian Processes for Continuous Control: Sublinear Regret Bounds for Unbounded State Spaces
Authors: Hamish Flynn, Joe Watson, Ingmar Posner, Jan Peters
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-09T12:03:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:25:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. 45 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We analyze the Bayesian regret of the Gaussian process posterior sampling reinforcement learning (GP-PSRL) algorithm. Posterior sampling is a heuristic for decision-making under uncertainty that has been used to develop successful algorithms for a variety of continuous control problems. However, theoretical work on GP-PSRL is limited. All known regret bounds either have a sub-optimal growth rate, require strong smoothness assumptions, or fail to properly account for the fact that the set of possible system states is unbounded. Through a recursive application of the Borell-Tsirelson-Ibragimov-Sudakov inequality, we show that, with high probability, the states actually visited by the algorithm are contained within a ball of near-constant radius. We then use the chaining method to control the regret suffered by GP-PSRL under weak smoothness conditions. Our main result is a Bayesian regret bound of the order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{γ_TT})$, where $H$ is the horizon, $T$ is the number of time steps and $γ_T$ is the expected information gain. With this result, we resolve the limitations with prior theoretical work on PSRL, and provide the theoretical foundation and tools for analyzing PSRL in complex settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于高斯过程的后验采样强化学习用于连续控制：无界状态空间下的次线性遗憾界
我们分析了高斯过程后验采样强化学习（GP-PSRL）算法的贝叶斯遗憾。后验采样是一种用于不确定环境下的决策启发式方法，已被用于开发多种连续控制问题的成功算法。然而，关于GP-PSRL的理论研究有限。所有已知的遗憾界要么增长速率次优，要么需要强光滑性假设，要么未能正确考虑系统状态集合无界的事实。通过递归应用Borell-Tsirelson-Ibragimov-Sudakov不等式，我们证明了以高概率，算法实际访问的状态被包含在一个半径近似恒定的球内。随后，我们使用链方法在弱光滑性条件下控制GP-PSRL的遗憾。我们的主要结果是贝叶斯遗憾界为 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{γ_TT})$，其中 $H$ 为时间跨度，$T$ 为时间步数，$γ_T$ 为预期信息增益。通过该结果，我们解决了先前PSRL理论工作的局限性，并为在复杂场景下分析PSRL提供了理论基础和工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for theoretical guarantees for posterior sampling in reinforcement learning with unbounded state spaces, this paper analyzes the Bayesian regret of GP-PSRL, a method that samples actions from the posterior distribution of a Gaussian process model. The authors overcome prior limitations by showing that, with high probability, the algorithm’s visited states lie within a bounded region, enabling the use of chaining techniques to control regret under weak smoothness assumptions. Their main result is a sublinear regret bound of order \(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{γ_TT})\), where \(H\) is the horizon, \(T\) is the time steps, and \(γ_T\) is the expected information gain, resolving previous shortcomings in theoretical analysis.
为解决后验采样在无界状态空间中缺乏理论保证的问题，本文分析了基于高斯过程的后验采样强化学习（GP-PSRL）的贝叶斯后悔。作者通过证明算法实际访问的状态被包含在半径近似恒定的球内，结合链方法和递归应用集中不等式，在弱光滑性假设下推导出后悔界为 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(H\sqrt{γ_TT})$，从而克服了此前工作在增长速率、光滑性假设和状态空间无界性方面的局限。
Bridging the Manifold Gap: Riemannian Residual Line Search for One-Step Image Editing
Authors: Hongzhu Yi, Zhongtian Luo, Tong Li, Yiyan Fan, Jungang Xu
First: 2026-06-23T17:23:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:23:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One-step diffusion editors are fast because they avoid inversion and iterative optimization, but a single transport update must be aggressive enough to realize the target prompt and conservative enough to preserve the source image--and no fixed update strength satisfies both demands across edit types. We treat this tension as a post-hoc candidate-selection problem on top of energy-field transport rather than as a new editing model. Our proposed method, Riemannian Residual Line Search, first builds a stronger edit by estimating the local time curvature of the prompt-delta field and projecting the corrected direction back onto the update norm of the original first-order energy-field transport estimation. It then forms a small residual path from the source image to this strong edit, retains the original first-order output as one candidate, and picks the final image by maximizing target-prompt CLIP alignment. On a 700-sample PIE-Bench++ evaluation across 10 edit type IDs, our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among current one-step update algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：弥合流形差距：黎曼残差线搜索用于一步图像编辑
一步扩散编辑器因其避免反演和迭代优化而快速，但单次传输更新必须足够激进以实现目标提示，同时又足够保守以保留源图像——而没有固定更新强度能满足所有编辑类型的需求。我们将此矛盾视为在能量场传输之上的后处理候选选择问题，而非新的编辑模型。我们提出的方法“黎曼残差线搜索”首先通过估计提示-增量场的局部时间曲率，构建更强的编辑效果，并将校正方向投影回原始一阶能量场传输估计的更新范数；然后从源图像到该强编辑形成一条小残差路径，保留原始一阶输出作为候选之一，并通过最大化目标提示CLIP对齐度选择最终图像。在包含10种编辑类型ID、700样本的PIE-Bench++评估中，我们的方法在当前一步更新算法中达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Evaluated on 700 samples across 10 edit types in PIE-Bench++, the method achieves state-of-the-art performance among one-step update algorithms.
为解决单步扩散图像编辑中编辑强度需兼顾激进与保守的矛盾，本文提出黎曼残差线搜索方法，将候选图像选择视为能量场传输后的后处理优化问题。该方法首先通过估计提示-差值场的局部时间曲率，将修正方向投影回原始一阶能量场更新范数，生成更强的编辑结果；再从源图像构建至该强编辑的残差路径，保留原始一阶输出作为候选，最终通过最大化目标提示与CLIP的对齐度选择最终图像。在700个PIE-Bench++样本、10种编辑类型上的评估中，该方法在现有单步编辑算法中达到最优性能。
Quantum Futures Interactive: A Live Demonstration of Post-Quantum Blockchain Security, Infrastructure Tradeoffs, and Sustainable Distributed Trust
Authors: Dongping Liu, Aoyu Zhang, Luyao Zhang
First: 2026-05-15T14:22:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:23:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advances in quantum computing challenge the hardness assumptions underlying widely deployed public-key cryptography in blockchain systems. Although post-quantum cryptography (PQC) standards are emerging, understanding quantum risk remains fragmented across research, engineering, governance, and investment communities. This demo presents Quantum Futures Interactive, a live interdisciplinary demonstration combining educational visualization, participatory interaction, and demonstrative post-quantum artifact generation using a toy LWE-based construction. Participants engage in a structured seven-stage interaction flow covering quantum threat education, sentiment capture, technology prioritization, infrastructure tradeoff exploration across simulators and QPUs, and artifact generation. The system integrates distributed trust concepts and sustainability-aware infrastructure considerations within an interactive decision framework.
