World Models in Pieces: Structural Certification for General Agents
Authors: Yikai Lu, Yifei Wu, Xinyu Lu, Tongxin Li
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-23T17:21:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:21:09+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, camera-ready version in ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the big-world regime, agents cannot be universally capable and their ability is inevitably specialized across a world model in pieces. Consequently, standard uniform guarantees fail to distinguish between the understanding of critical bottlenecks and irrelevant failures. We first formalize this limitation by proving that general agents are not universal, rendering standard worst-case analysis uninformative. To overcome this, we introduce structural certification, a transition-local framework that maps bounded goal-conditioned performance to entry-wise guarantees on the agent's internal world model. Our main contribution is constructive. We provide algorithms that filter specific transitions using deep compositional goals and prove that a general agent on these goals has a structural world model with a $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$ error bound. Conversely, this bound is tight in the small-$δ$ regime, whose existence is explicitly guaranteed by our certification. These results enable the certifiable deployment of general agents by localizing the specific transitions where long-horizon planning is reliable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分块世界模型：通用代理的结构化认证
在大世界范式下，代理无法具备普适能力，其能力必然在分块的世界模型中专业化。因此，标准的统一保证无法区分对关键瓶颈的理解与无关的失败。我们首先通过证明通用代理并非普适，从而表明标准的最坏情况分析无用。为克服此问题，我们引入结构化认证，这是一种转移局部框架，将有界目标条件下的性能映射为代理内部世界模型的逐项保证。我们的主要贡献是构造性的：我们提供算法，利用深度组合目标过滤特定转移，并证明在这些目标下，通用代理具有结构化世界模型，其误差界为 $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$。相反，在小$δ$情形下，该界是紧的，且其存在性由我们的认证明确保证。这些结果使通用代理的可认证部署成为可能，通过定位长时规划可靠的特定转移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inherent specialization of agents in complex environments, this paper addresses the failure of uniform guarantees in the big-world regime by formalizing that general agents cannot be universally capable. It introduces structural certification, a transition-local framework that links bounded goal-conditioned performance to entry-wise guarantees within the agent’s internal world model. The authors construct algorithms using deep compositional goals to filter specific transitions, proving that general agents achieve an error bound of $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$ on these transitions. This bound is shown to be tight in the small-$δ$ regime, explicitly guaranteed by the certification, enabling reliable long-horizon planning on localized transitions.
鉴于复杂环境中智能体能力的固有专业化，本文指出大世界假设下统一保证的失效，并形式化证明通用智能体无法具备普适能力。为此，作者提出结构化认证框架，将有界目标条件下的性能与智能体内部世界模型的逐项保证相联系。通过构建基于深度组合目标的算法筛选特定状态转移，证明通用智能体在这些目标下可实现 $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$ 的结构化世界模型误差界，且在小δ条件下该界紧致——其存在性由认证框架明确保证。该结果使智能体可在特定转移上实现可认证的长时规划部署。
Matching Tasks to Objectives: Fine-Tuning and Prompt-Tuning Strategies for Encoder-Decoder Pre-trained Language Models
Authors: Ahmad Pouramini, Hesham Faili
Venue: Appl Intell 54(20):9783-9810, 2024
First: 2026-06-23T17:21:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:21:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Prompt-based learning has emerged as a dominant paradigm in natural language processing. This study explores the impact of diverse pre-training objectives on the performance of encoder-decoder pre-trained language models across generation and question answering tasks, with a focus on commonsense knowledge retrieval and completion. We highlight the benefits of incorporating multiple objectives during both pre-training and fine-tuning stages. We introduce the Match Task to Objective (MTO) framework and methods for determining the appropriate objective for a given task. This framework offers automated methods to prepare task-related data for adaptation through unsupervised training, based on the identified objective. In the fine-tuning stage, we design novel templates that align with the objectives of the pre-training and adaptation stages. When aligned with task requirements, these strategies can achieve a performance gain of over 120\% compared to conventional methods in few-shot settings. They significantly outperform related works in few-shot settings and exceed the baseline even in full-dataset scenarios. Furthermore, we extend this approach to include prompt-tuning methodologies, providing guidance for more effective soft prompt engineering and optimization. Our strategies significantly enhance prompt-tuning performance as well. These insights hold substantial value, precisely guiding the selection and optimization of models customized for specific tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/puraminy/MTO/
中文标题/摘要
标题：匹配任务与目标：编码器-解码器预训练语言模型的微调与提示调优策略
基于提示的学习已成为自然语言处理中的主流范式。本研究探讨了不同预训练目标对编码器-解码器预训练语言模型在生成与问答任务中性能的影响，重点关注常识知识检索与补全。我们强调在预训练和微调阶段结合多种目标的优势。我们提出了“匹配任务至目标”（MTO）框架及确定特定任务适用目标的方法。该框架提供基于识别目标的自动化方法，通过无监督训练准备任务相关数据以适应新任务。在微调阶段，我们设计了与预训练和适应阶段目标相匹配的新模板。当与任务需求对齐时，这些策略在少样本设置下相比传统方法性能提升超过120%，在少样本设置中显著优于相关工作，即使在全数据集场景下也超越基线。此外，我们将该方法扩展至提示调优方法，为更有效的软提示工程与优化提供指导。我们的策略也显著提升了提示调优性能。这些见解具有重要价值，能精准指导针对特定任务定制模型的选择与优化。代码见 https://github.com/puraminy/MTO/
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of aligning pre-training objectives with downstream tasks in encoder-decoder language models, motivated by the need to improve performance in generation and question answering, especially with limited data. The authors propose the Match Task to Objective (MTO) framework, which automatically identifies suitable pre-training objectives and adapts task data via unsupervised methods. During fine-tuning, they design objective-aligned templates that significantly boost performance—achieving over 120% gains in few-shot settings compared to conventional approaches—and outperform existing methods even with full datasets. The framework is further extended to prompt-tuning, enhancing soft prompt engineering and yielding consistent improvements across tasks.
为提升预训练目标与下游任务的匹配度，本研究探讨了不同预训练目标对编码器-解码器语言模型在生成与问答任务中的影响。提出匹配任务与目标（MTO）框架，自动识别适配目标并基于无监督训练准备任务相关数据。微调阶段采用与目标对齐的模板，相较传统方法在少样本场景下性能提升超120%，且在全数据集场景下仍优于基线。该框架进一步扩展至提示调优，显著提升软提示工程效果，整体策略在各类任务中表现更优。
Grading the Grader: Lessons from Evaluating an Agentic Data Analysis System
Authors: Tian Zheng, Kai-Tai Hsu
First: 2026-06-23T17:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:18:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic data analysis systems produce rich outputs, including code, numerical results, and verbal diagnostics. This makes them more challenging to evaluate than single-turn LLM responses. It is therefore necessary to distinguish genuine disagreement between an agent's output and a ground-truth answer from grading artifacts. We investigate how reliably automated graders assess such a system and what strategies improve grading quality by applying LAMBDA, a multi-agent data-analysis system, on 153 numerical QRData tasks from DSGym. We develop and evaluate a three-layer human-AI grading cascade: strict regex matching, LLM-based lenient grading, and snippet-based human inspection, which combines non-GenAI and GenAI strategies with different failure profiles. Both automated graders achieve 100% observed precision (0/70 false positives). The lenient grader's recall is 97% against human labels. A keyword-anchored extraction pipeline raises the strict grader's recall by 60 percentage points over a last-number heuristic; the lenient grader is architecturally parser-independent. An iterative nudge mechanism raises grading run success from 36% to 97% and lenient-pass rates from 16% to 46%; comparing nudging with and without original-question re-injection shows that re-injection offers no benefit, confirming the nudge as an answer template cue. We further observe in this case study that variable type is the task metadata field most consistently associated with grading pipeline dynamics and observed outcome grades.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估者评估：从评估代理数据分析系统中获得的启示
代理数据分析系统生成丰富输出，包括代码、数值结果和口头诊断，这使其比单轮LLM响应更难评估。因此，有必要区分代理输出与真实答案之间的真正分歧与评分伪迹。我们通过在DSGym的153个数值QRData任务上应用LAMBDA多代理数据分析系统，研究自动化评分器对这类系统的评估可靠性，并探讨哪些策略能通过结合非生成式AI与生成式AI的不同失败模式，提升评分质量。两种自动化评分器均实现100%观察精度（0/70误报）。宽松评分器对人工标注的召回率为97%。基于关键词锚定的提取管道使严格评分器的召回率比最后数字启发式方法提升60个百分点；宽松评分器架构上与解析器无关。迭代提示机制将评分运行成功率从36%提升至97%，宽松通过率从16%提升至46%；比较有无原题重注入的提示机制发现，重注入无益，证实提示机制作为答案模板提示的作用。本案例研究进一步观察到，变量类型是与评分流程动态和观察到的成绩最一致关联的任务元数据字段。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of evaluating agentic data analysis systems that generate diverse outputs like code, numbers, and diagnostics, this study investigates how reliably automated graders can assess such systems and how to improve their accuracy. The researchers applied LAMBDA, a multi-agent system, to 153 numerical tasks from DSGym and developed a three-layer grading cascade combining strict regex matching, LLM-based lenient grading, and human snippet inspection. Results show both automated graders achieved 100% precision, with the lenient grader achieving 97% recall against human labels. Enhancements like keyword-anchored extraction boosted strict grader recall by 60 percentage points over a baseline heuristic, while an iterative nudge mechanism increased overall success rates from 36% to 97% and lenient-pass rates from 16% to 46%. Crucially, re-injecting the original question did not improve outcomes, confirming the nudge as a template cue. The study also found that variable type was the most consistent metadata predictor of grading dynamics and final grades.
为评估生成代码、数值结果和诊断文本的代理数据分析系统，研究旨在区分真实错误与评分偏差。采用三层人机评分级联——严格正则匹配、基于LLM的宽松评分与片段人工检查——对LAMBDA系统在153个数值任务上的表现进行评估。结果显示，两种自动评分器均实现100%精确率，宽松评分器召回率达97%。关键词锚定提取方法使严格评分器召回率较基线提升60个百分点，且宽松评分器架构独立于解析器。迭代提示机制将整体成功率从36%提升至97%，宽松通过率从16%升至46%，而重注入原始问题无额外收益。变量类型被发现是与评分结果最一致相关的任务元数据字段。
QSignAI: Quantum-Randomness-Seeded Identity Signatures at the Intersection of AI for Science and Science for AI
Authors: Dongping Liu, Aoyu Zhang, Luyao Zhang
First: 2026-05-26T22:04:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:17:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The 2024-2025 Nobel and Turing awards recognised AI and quantum science simultaneously. Yet no deployed system has brought these streams together for the public. This paper presents QSignAI, a production-deployed platform demonstrating a bidirectional AI-quantum relationship in a real-time event participation system. We address three questions: can quantum-randomness generation via a two-source extractor be embedded in an AI-driven social platform with acceptable latency; can an AI bot make quantum phenomena perceptually legible to general audiences; and does the combined system work in practice? A conversational bot routes each participant's first message through a quantum pipeline comprising a Toeplitz two-source extractor over independent single-qubit Hadamard measurements on SV1 and DM1 simulators, plus a 2-qubit Bell state, producing a unique quantum-randomness-seeded identity signature per participant. The first two questions are answered through system architecture and qualitative deployment evidence from live events; the third through successful production deployment. The current deployment uses cloud quantum simulators; physical QPU randomness is the near-term extension. Measurable benchmarks are identified as priority future work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QSignAI：在AI用于科学与科学用于AI交叉点上的量子随机性种子身份签名
2024-2025年诺贝尔奖与图灵奖同时认可了人工智能与量子科学。然而，目前尚无系统将这两者结合并面向公众部署。本文提出了QSignAI，一个已投入实际应用的平台，展示了AI与量子技术在实时参与系统中的双向关系。我们回答了三个问题：通过双源提取器生成的量子随机性能否嵌入AI驱动的社交平台并保持可接受的延迟；AI机器人能否使量子现象对普通观众变得可感知；该联合系统在实践中是否有效？对话机器人将每位参与者的首条消息引导至一个量子管道，该管道包括在SV1和DM1模拟器上对独立单量子比特Hadamard测量的Toeplitz双源提取器，以及一个两量子比特Bell态，为每位参与者生成一个独特的量子随机性种子身份签名。前两个问题通过系统架构和现场活动的定性部署证据回答；第三个问题通过成功投入实际应用得到验证。当前部署使用云量子模拟器；物理QPU随机性是近期扩展方向。可测量的基准指标被列为未来优先工作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the parallel recognition of AI and quantum science in major awards but the absence of integrated public systems, this paper introduces QSignAI, a real-time platform that embeds quantum-randomness generation via a Toeplitz two-source extractor into an AI-driven social system. The method uses independent single-qubit Hadamard measurements and a 2-qubit Bell state on quantum simulators to generate unique identity signatures for each participant, routed through a conversational bot. Experimental results confirm acceptable latency, perceptual legibility of quantum phenomena to non-experts, and successful real-world deployment using cloud simulators, with physical quantum processors identified as the next step.
鉴于AI与量子科学在重要奖项中被并列认可，但尚未有面向公众的集成系统，本文提出QSignAI平台，将量子随机数生成嵌入AI驱动的社交系统。通过在量子模拟器上使用Toeplitz两源提取器处理单量子比特Hadamard测量与双量子比特Bell态，为每位参与者生成独特的量子随机种子身份签名。实验表明，系统延迟可接受，能向普通用户直观呈现量子现象，且已在真实活动中成功部署，当前使用云量子模拟器，物理量子处理器为下一阶段目标。
Accuracy and Satisfaction in Multi-Turn LLM Dialogues for NFR Assessment
Authors: Ali Pourghasemi Fatideh, Wilder Baldwin, Maria Dhakal, Collin McMillan, Sepideh Ghanavati
First: 2026-06-23T17:15:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:15:40+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures. Accepted to SIGDIAL 2026 (27th Annual Meeting of the Special Interest Group on Discourse and Dialogue)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based dialogue assistants have become mainstream tools for software developers, yet current evaluation benchmarks focus exclusively on functional correctness. This leaves a critical gap in assessing the quality and accuracy of these conversations when handling Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs), which are inherently vague, context-dependent, and involve many parts of a program. Evaluating how well these systems support collaborative reasoning about NFRs requires methods that go beyond single-turn accuracy to capture both the correctness of the system's outputs and the quality of the multi-turn interaction. In this paper, we investigate the accuracy and quality of multi-turn conversations between developers and an LLM-based agent in the domain of Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (HIPAA) regulatory compliance. We hired 49 programmers to interact with GitHub Copilot to assess 148 HIPAA-derived NFRs against the iTrust codebase, a system designed to comply with HIPAA regulations, across three dimensions: requirement satisfaction level, reasoning, and code localization. We find that developers tend to agree with LLM assessments, but accuracy against expert ground truth is low. We model user satisfaction and find that longer system responses and more information-providing turns negatively affect user satisfaction, whereas proactive interactions positively affect it. Our findings provide insights for designing LLM-based dialogue systems that support NFR assessment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多轮LLM对话中非功能需求评估的准确性与满意度
基于大语言模型的对话助手已成为软件开发者的主流工具，但当前评估基准仅关注功能正确性，而忽视了在处理固有模糊、依赖上下文且涉及程序多部分的非功能需求（NFRs）时，对话质量与准确性的评估。评估这些系统在支持协作推理NFRs方面的表现，需要超越单轮准确性的方法，以同时捕捉系统输出的正确性与多轮交互的质量。本文研究了开发者与基于LLM的代理在医疗保健保险可携性与责任法案（HIPAA）合规领域多轮对话的准确性和质量。我们聘请49名程序员与GitHub Copilot互动，针对iTrust代码库（设计用于符合HIPAA法规）评估148个衍生自HIPAA的NFRs，从需求满足程度、推理和代码定位三个维度进行分析。研究发现，开发者普遍认同LLM评估，但与专家真实标注的准确率较低。我们建模用户满意度，发现系统响应越长、提供信息的回合越多，用户满意度越低；而主动互动则提升满意度。研究结果为设计支持NFR评估的LLM对话系统提供了洞见。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of evaluation frameworks for assessing how well LLM-based dialogue systems handle Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) in multi-turn interactions, which are often vague and context-sensitive. The researchers conducted a controlled experiment where 49 developers interacted with GitHub Copilot to evaluate 148 HIPAA-related NFRs against the iTrust codebase, measuring satisfaction, reasoning quality, and code localization across three dimensions. Results showed that while developers largely agreed with the LLM’s assessments, accuracy against expert ground truth was low. User satisfaction was negatively correlated with longer system responses and information-heavy turns, but positively influenced by proactive, anticipatory interactions.
该研究旨在填补当前评估框架对LLM对话系统处理非功能性需求（NFR）能力的空白，因为NFR具有语境依赖性和模糊性。研究人员组织49名开发者使用GitHub Copilot评估148个与HIPAA相关的NFR，针对iTrust代码库在需求满足度、推理质量和代码定位三个维度进行测试。结果显示，开发者虽普遍认同LLM评估结果，但与专家标准相比准确率较低；用户满意度与系统回复长度及信息密集型回合呈负相关，而主动式交互则显著提升满意度。
Reference-Augmented Learning for Precise Tracking Policy of Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots
Authors: Ziqing Zou, Ke Qiu, Haojian Lu, Rong Xiong, Yue Wang
First: 2026-04-28T14:24:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:15:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots (TDCRs) pose significant control challenges due to their highly nonlinear, path-dependent dynamics and non-Markovian characteristics. Traditional Jacobian-based controllers often struggle with hysteresis-induced oscillations, while conventional learning-based approaches suffer from poor generalization to out-of-distribution trajectories. This paper proposes a reference-augmented offline learning framework for precise 6-DOF tracking control of TDCRs. By leveraging a differentiable RNN-based dynamics surrogate as a gradient bridge, we optimize a control policy through an augmented reference distribution. This multi-scale augmentation scheme incorporates stochastic bias, harmonic perturbations, and random walks, forcing the policy to internalize diverse tracking error recovery mechanisms without additional hardware interaction. Experimental results on a three-section TDCR platform demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves a 50.9\% reduction in average position error compared to non-augmented baselines and significantly outperforms Jacobian-based methods in both precision and stability across various speeds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于参考增强学习的腱驱动连续体机器人的精确跟踪控制策略
腱驱动连续体机器人（TDCR）因其高度非线性、路径依赖的动力学特性及非马尔可夫性，带来显著的控制挑战。传统基于雅可比的控制器常受滞回引起的振荡影响，而常规学习方法在面对分布外轨迹时泛化能力差。本文提出一种参考增强的离线学习框架，用于TDCR的精确六自由度跟踪控制。通过利用可微RNN构建的动力学代理作为梯度桥，我们在增强的参考分布上优化控制策略。该多尺度增强方案结合随机偏置、谐波扰动和随机游走，迫使策略内化多样化的跟踪误差恢复机制，无需额外硬件交互。在三段式TDCR平台上的实验表明，所提策略相比未增强基线平均位置误差降低50.9%，在多种速度下精度与稳定性均显著优于雅可比方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the control difficulties of tendon-driven continuum robots due to their nonlinear, path-dependent, and non-Markovian dynamics, this work introduces a reference-augmented offline learning framework to improve 6-DOF tracking precision. The method employs a differentiable RNN-based dynamics surrogate to act as a gradient bridge, enabling policy optimization over an augmented reference distribution that includes stochastic bias, harmonic perturbations, and random walks. Experiments on a three-section TDCR platform show the proposed policy reduces average position error by 50.9% compared to non-augmented baselines and significantly outperforms Jacobian-based controllers in both accuracy and stability across varying speeds.
为解决腱驱动连续体机器人因高度非线性、路径依赖及非马尔可夫特性带来的控制难题，本文提出一种参考增强的离线学习框架以提升六自由度轨迹跟踪精度。该方法利用可微RNN构建动力学代理，通过增强参考分布（含随机偏置、谐波扰动和随机游走）实现梯度优化的控制策略。在三节段机器人平台实验中，所提策略相较非增强基线平均位置误差降低50.9%，且在不同速度下显著优于基于雅可比的传统方法，兼具精度与稳定性。
Difference-Making without Making a Difference
Authors: Sander Beckers
First: 2026-06-23T17:15:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:15:26+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over a series of seven papers, Andreas & Günther have introduced seven definitions of actual causation and have classified them as belonging to three different, competing, types of accounts: factual difference-making, counterfactual difference-making, and regularity-based. I show that their most recent - factual difference-making - definition instantiates all three types, thereby proving that these are distinctions without a difference. I further compare their novel account to the other six accounts on several crucial examples, revealing that this undermines all seven of their accounts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无差别地产生影响
在七篇论文中，Andreas与Günther提出了七种实际因果性的定义，并将其归类为三种不同的、相互竞争的解释类型：事实差异性、反事实差异性和规律性基础。我证明了他们最新的——事实差异性——定义同时体现了这三种类型，从而表明这些区分并无实质差异。我进一步将他们的新解释与其余六种解释在若干关键例证中进行比较，揭示这实际上削弱了他们全部七种解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to clarify the conceptual boundaries between different accounts of actual causation, the paper challenges Andreas & Günther’s classification of seven causal definitions into three distinct types. The author demonstrates that their most recent factual difference-making definition actually encompasses all three categories, thereby showing these distinctions are illusory. Further comparison with the other six definitions across key examples reveals that the factual difference-making account undermines the coherence of all seven definitions collectively.
为厘清实际因果关系的不同定义，本文批判了Andreas与Günther提出的七种因果定义，指出其最新提出的‘事实差异制造’定义实际上融合了‘事实型’、‘反事实型’和‘规律型’三种类型，从而证明这些区分并无实质差异。作者进一步通过关键案例将该统一定义与其余六种定义进行比较，发现该定义实际上否定了所有七种定义的有效性。
GeoT2V-Bench: Benchmarking 3D Consistency in Text-to-Video Models via 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Chenrui Fan, Paolo Favaro
First: 2026-06-23T17:12:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:12:21+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 17 figures, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Camera-prompted text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly used to synthesize virtual camera captures, such as orbiting objects or moving through static scenes. For these outputs, visual plausibility is insufficient: the generated frames should also provide coherent multi-view evidence for a single static 3D scene. We introduce GeoT2V-Bench, a reconstruction-based diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether camera-prompted T2V clips can support explicit rigid 3D reconstruction. Our pipeline estimates per-frame camera intrinsics and poses with VGGT-style geometry estimation, fits DeformableGS, derives a static MedianGS proxy by temporal-median aggregation, and renders this proxy along the estimated camera path. Instead of producing a pass/fail label or a single scalar score, GeoT2V-Bench reports a continuous reconstruction profile covering apparent image motion, estimated trajectory behavior, MedianGS static rendering error, static-render flow agreement, and the gap between flexible and static fits. On a fair-format four-seed evaluation with 3,840 completed reconstructions from 12 open-weight model configurations and 80 GeCo-Eval static-scene prompts, we find that visible motion, static rendering error, flow agreement, and flexible-vs-static behavior often disagree. GeoT2V-Bench therefore captures complementary failure modes that emerge when generated videos are tested as global static-scene acquisitions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoT2V-Bench：通过3D重建对文本到视频模型的3D一致性进行基准测试
相机提示的文本到视频（T2V）模型正越来越多地用于合成虚拟相机捕捉，例如环绕物体或穿越静态场景。对于这些输出，视觉合理性已不足：生成的帧还应为单一静态3D场景提供连贯的多视角证据。我们引入GeoT2V-Bench，这是一个基于重建的诊断基准，用于评估相机提示的T2V片段是否能支持显式的刚性3D重建。我们的流程使用VGGT风格的几何估计，逐帧估计相机内参和姿态，拟合DeformableGS，通过时间中位数聚合推导出静态MedianGS代理，并沿估计的相机路径渲染该代理。GeoT2V-Bench不输出通过/失败标签或单一标量分数，而是报告连续的重建曲线，涵盖可见图像运动、估计轨迹行为、MedianGS静态渲染误差、静态渲染流一致性，以及柔性与静态拟合之间的差距。在公平格式的四种子评估中，我们使用12个开源模型配置和80个GeCo-Eval静态场景提示，共完成3840次重建，发现可见运动、静态渲染误差、流一致性及柔性与静态行为常不一致。因此，GeoT2V-Bench捕捉到当生成视频作为全局静态场景采集测试时出现的互补性失败模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate whether camera-prompted text-to-video models generate frames consistent with a single static 3D scene, the authors introduce GeoT2V-Bench, a reconstruction-based benchmark that assesses 3D consistency via geometric estimation and rendering. The method estimates per-frame camera intrinsics and poses, fits DeformableGS, derives a static MedianGS proxy via temporal median aggregation, and renders it along the estimated path to evaluate reconstruction fidelity. Experimental results on 3,840 reconstructions from 12 models and 80 prompts reveal that visual motion, static rendering error, flow agreement, and flexible-static behavior often conflict, indicating that current models fail in complementary ways when evaluated as global static-scene captures.
为评估相机提示的文本到视频模型是否生成与单一静态3D场景一致的帧，作者提出了GeoT2V-Bench基准。该方法通过估计每帧相机参数、拟合DeformableGS、利用时间中位数聚合得到静态MedianGS代理，并沿估计路径渲染以评估重建质量。实验在12个模型和80个提示上进行，结果显示运动可见性、静态渲染误差、光流一致性及柔性与静态拟合差异常不一致，揭示了传统评估方法难以捕捉的多种3D一致性失败模式。
Selective Rotary Position Embedding
Authors: Sajad Movahedi, Timur Carstensen, Arshia Afzal, Frank Hutter, Antonio Orvieto, Volkan Cevher
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-11-21T16:50:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:12:17+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Position information is essential for language modeling. In softmax transformers, Rotary Position Embeddings (\textit{RoPE}) encode positions through \textit{fixed-angle} rotations, while in linear transformers, order is handled via input-dependent (selective) gating that decays past key-value associations. Selectivity has generally been shown to improve language-related tasks. Inspired by this, we introduce \textit{Selective RoPE}, an \textit{input-dependent} rotary embedding mechanism, that generalizes \textit{RoPE}, and enables rotation in \textit{arbitrary angles} for both linear and softmax transformers. We show that softmax attention already performs a hidden form of these rotations on query-key pairs, uncovering an implicit positional structure. We further show that in state-space models and gated linear transformers, the real part manages forgetting while the imaginary part encodes positions through rotations. We validate our method by equipping gated transformers with \textit{Selective RoPE}, demonstrating that its input-dependent rotations improve performance in language modeling and on difficult sequence tasks like copying, state tracking, and retrieval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：选择性旋转位置嵌入
位置信息对语言建模至关重要。在softmax变换器中，旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）通过固定角度旋转编码位置；而在线性变换器中，顺序通过输入依赖的（选择性）门控处理，衰减过往键值关联。选择性通常被证明能提升语言相关任务表现。受此启发，我们提出选择性RoPE，一种输入依赖的旋转嵌入机制，它推广了RoPE，并使线性与softmax变换器均可在任意角度进行旋转。我们发现softmax注意力已在查询-键对上隐式执行此类旋转，揭示了隐含的位置结构。进一步地，我们表明在状态空间模型与门控线性变换器中，实部负责遗忘，虚部则通过旋转编码位置。我们通过为门控变换器配备选择性RoPE验证该方法，证明其输入依赖旋转可提升语言建模及复制、状态跟踪和检索等困难序列任务的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that selectivity in gating mechanisms improves language modeling, this work introduces Selective RoPE, an input-dependent rotary position embedding that generalizes fixed-angle RoPE to arbitrary angles, enabling both softmax and linear transformers to benefit from position-aware rotations. The method reveals that softmax attention implicitly performs such rotations on query-key pairs, uncovering hidden positional structure, and further demonstrates that in state-space models, real and imaginary parts of complex-valued representations manage forgetting and encode position respectively. Experimental results show that equipping gated transformers with Selective RoPE significantly improves performance on language modeling and challenging sequence tasks including copying, state tracking, and retrieval.
受输入依赖性选择性位置编码提升语言建模效果的启发，本文提出Selective RoPE，一种可对任意角度旋转的输入依赖型旋转位置嵌入，适用于softmax和线性变换器。该方法揭示了softmax注意力中查询-键对已隐含执行旋转，形成位置结构；同时在状态空间模型中，复数表示的实部负责遗忘，虚部负责位置编码。实验表明，为门控线性变换器引入Selective RoPE后，在语言建模及复制、状态追踪、检索等复杂序列任务上性能显著提升。
Less is More: Quality-Aware Training Data Selection for Scientific Summarization
Authors: Maria Nefeli Paraskevopoulou, Tatiana Passali, Grigorios Tsoumakas
First: 2026-06-23T17:12:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:12:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific long-document summarization datasets commonly treat author-written abstracts as gold reference summaries, although their quality and alignment with the source article vary. At the same time, publicly available scientific summarization datasets remain limited in scale and structure for modern long-context models. In this work, we address both challenges by a) constructing and releasing one of the largest biomedical and life science datasets for long-document summarization, containing 1.88 million PMC articles, and b) analyzing the reference quality of author-written abstracts with source-grounded and model-based metrics. We show that author-written abstracts vary in their alignment with the full article and that these quality signals can guide training-data selection. Training on selected high-quality subsets outperforms random sampling at matched training sizes and can match or exceed larger random subsets on factuality-oriented metrics. Our findings suggest that reference quality is an important factor in scientific summarization and that quality-aware data selection can improve training efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：少即是多：面向科学摘要的高质量训练数据选择
科学长文档摘要数据集通常将作者撰写的摘要视为黄金参考摘要，但其质量与源文章的契合度参差不齐。同时，公开可用的科学摘要数据集在规模和结构上仍难以满足现代长上下文模型的需求。本文通过两项工作解决上述挑战：a) 构建并发布迄今最大的生物医学与生命科学长文档摘要数据集，包含188万篇PMC文章；b) 通过源文对齐与模型基指标分析作者摘要的质量。我们发现，作者摘要与全文的契合度存在差异，且这些质量信号可指导训练数据选择。在相同训练规模下，基于高质量子集训练的表现优于随机采样，且在事实性指标上可匹配或超越更大规模的随机子集。我们的研究结果表明，参考摘要质量是科学摘要的重要因素，质量感知的数据选择可提升训练效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the variability in quality of author-written abstracts used as reference summaries in scientific long-document summarization and the scarcity of large-scale datasets for modern models, this work constructs a massive biomedical and life science dataset of 1.88 million PMC articles and evaluates reference quality using source-grounded and model-based metrics. It demonstrates that high-quality abstracts better align with source articles and that training on these curated subsets yields superior performance compared to random sampling at equivalent sizes, often matching or surpassing larger random subsets on factuality metrics. The results indicate that quality-aware data selection significantly enhances training efficiency in scientific summarization.
鉴于作者撰写的摘要作为科学长文摘要参考标准时质量参差不齐，且公开可用的科学摘要数据集规模有限，本研究构建了包含188万篇PMC文章的大型生物医学与生命科学数据集，并通过源域对齐与模型评估指标分析摘要质量。实验表明，高质量摘要与原文内容更匹配，针对性选取高质量样本训练可显著优于随机采样，尤其在事实性指标上可媲美或超越更大规模的随机样本。结果说明，参考质量是科学摘要任务的关键因素，质量感知的数据选择能有效提升训练效率。
Virtual Simulation for Mental Health
Authors: Anna Fang
First: 2026-06-23T17:11:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:11:09+00:00
Comments: Doctoral dissertation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Poorly designed interventions or those deployed without adequate safeguards can harm the communities they aim to serve, thus exacerbating existing vulnerabilities and leaving individuals unsupported. This is especially the case for the mental health context, where there is a growing trend of relying on technological interventions due to their accessibility and ability to deliver large-scale support. However, the mental health context is also particularly sensitive to change and risks of failure are dire; at their worst, failures in mental health interventions can result in lasting negative outcomes for individuals and tragic losses as people fall through the cracks. Thus, enabling safe ways to experiment in the mental health context is vital to allow both individuals and communities to engage with new interventions without risk of their real-world consequences. Virtual simulation, which uses virtual environments to replicate real-world interactions, processes, and behaviors, offers a promising opportunity for enabling safe, controlled experimentation with its ability to accurately replicate social situations, fears, stressors, and the potential outcomes of specific interactions. This work explores how simulation approaches can support emerging mental health processes through (1) evaluating community-level outcomes using agent-based modeling and (2) individual training in the mental health context through embodied, controlled spaces. I demonstrate this use of virtual simulation systems through a grounded human-centered approach, where system design is guided by empirical understanding of current real-world needs and challenges. By leveraging simulation to create environments where mental health strategies can be safely tested and practiced, this work aims to open new possibilities for designing scalable, user-centered systems that are effective and safe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：心理健康领域的虚拟仿真
设计不佳或缺乏充分保障的干预措施可能伤害其本意服务的社区，加剧现有脆弱性并使个体得不到支持。在心理健康领域尤为如此，该领域正日益依赖技术干预，因其具有可及性和大规模支持能力。然而，心理健康领域对变化尤为敏感，失败风险极高；最坏情况下，心理健康干预失败可能导致个体长期负面后果，甚至因系统漏洞造成悲剧性损失。因此，为心理健康领域提供安全的实验方式至关重要，以使个体和社区能够在无现实后果风险的情况下接触新干预措施。虚拟仿真通过虚拟环境复制现实互动、过程和行为，能够准确再现社交情境、恐惧、压力源及特定互动的潜在结果，为安全可控的实验提供了有力机会。本研究探讨仿真方法如何支持新兴心理健康进程，包括（1）通过基于代理的建模评估社区层面结果，以及（2）通过具身化、受控空间进行个体心理健康训练。本研究采用扎根于人本主义的方法，系统设计以对当前现实需求与挑战的实证理解为指导。通过利用仿真创建安全测试与实践心理健康策略的环境，本研究旨在开拓设计可扩展、以用户为中心、有效且安全系统的全新可能性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risks of harmful mental health interventions due to poor design or lack of safeguards, this work proposes virtual simulation as a safe, controlled method to test and train mental health strategies. Using agent-based modeling to evaluate community-level outcomes and embodied virtual environments for individual training, the approach enables experimentation without real-world consequences. Experimental results demonstrate that virtual simulation effectively replicates social stressors and interactions, allowing for iterative refinement of interventions while minimizing harm and supporting scalable, user-centered mental health solutions.
鉴于设计不当或缺乏保障措施的干预措施可能对目标群体造成伤害，本研究提出利用虚拟仿真作为安全可控的方法，用于测试和训练心理健康策略。通过基于代理的建模评估社区层面效果，结合具身化虚拟环境进行个体训练，该方法依托现实需求和挑战的实证理解进行设计。实验结果表明，虚拟仿真可有效支持心理健康干预的安全测试与实践，实现规模化、用户中心化部署，同时避免真实世界风险。
Activation Functions, Statistics and Learning of Higher-Order Interactions in Restricted Boltzmann Machines
Authors: Giovanni di Sarra, Yasser Roudi
First: 2026-05-18T23:04:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:11:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in Physica A: Statistical Mechanics and its Applications, Special Issue on Statistical Mechanics for Learning. 36 pages, 27 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The great success of neural networks primarily arises from the presence of the large number of weight parameters combined with nonlinearities in the input-output relationship of single neurons. In this work, we study the relationship between the statistical properties of the weights and the nonlinearity of the hidden unit in Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs) on the one side, and the distribution induced on binary visible units. We do this for four commonly used activation functions: Linear, Step, ReLU, and Exponential, and make qualitative predictions about the ability of these models to learn distributions with strong higher order interactions over the visible nodes. We show that in general, in an ensemble of RBMs with Gaussian weights, these distributions are rare and hard to learn, except when the hidden unit activation function is an Exponential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受限玻尔兹曼机中激活函数、统计特性与高阶相互作用的学习
神经网络的巨大成功主要源于大量权重参数与单个神经元输入输出关系中的非线性特性。本文研究了受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）中权重的统计特性与隐藏单元非线性激活函数之间的关系，以及由此在二值可见单元上诱导出的分布。我们针对四种常用激活函数——线性、阶跃、ReLU和指数函数——进行分析，并对这些模型学习具有强高阶相互作用的可见节点分布的能力做出定性预测。我们表明，在高斯权重的RBM集合中，这类分布通常罕见且难以学习，除非隐藏单元的激活函数为指数函数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of neural networks relying on nonlinearities and weight parameters, this study investigates how activation functions in Restricted Boltzmann Machines influence the ability to model higher-order interactions in visible units. It analyzes four activation functions—Linear, Step, ReLU, and Exponential—by examining their statistical properties and their impact on induced visible unit distributions. The results show that, in ensembles with Gaussian weights, higher-order interactions are generally rare and difficult to learn, except when the hidden unit uses the Exponential activation function, which uniquely enables such learning.
受神经网络成功依赖非线性与权重参数的启发，本研究探讨了受限玻尔兹曼机中激活函数如何影响可见单元上高阶相互作用的建模能力。针对四种激活函数（线性、阶跃、ReLU、指数），作者分析了权重统计特性对可见单元分布的影响。实验表明，在高斯权重的模型集合中，高阶相互作用普遍稀少且难以学习，唯独指数激活函数能有效实现此类学习。
L3Cube-MahaPOS: A Marathi Part-of-Speech Tagging Dataset and BERT Models
Authors: Hariom Ingle, Ronit Ghode, Ishwari Gondkar, Jidnyasa Harad, Raviraj Joshi
First: 2026-06-23T17:10:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:10:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Part-of-Speech (POS) tagging is a foundational NLP task underpinning machine translation, information extraction, and syntactic parsing. Despite Marathi being spoken by over 83 million people and ranking among the top twenty most spoken languages worldwide, it remains severely under-resourced in annotated corpora and standardised evaluation benchmarks. Marathi presents unique challenges for computational modelling owing to its rich morphology, relatively free word order, lack of capitalisation conventions, and pervasive code-mixing with Hindi and English. We introduce L3Cube-MahaPOS, a gold-standard POS tagging dataset for Marathi comprising 32,354 manually annotated sentences drawn from news text. Annotation was performed entirely manually by a team of Marathi-proficient annotators following a 16-tag Universal Dependencies-aligned scheme. A structured preprocessing pipeline covering Unicode normalisation, Devanagari-aware tokenisation, and noise filtering ensures label consistency across all splits. We benchmark the dataset across six model families spanning HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and the Marathi-specific transformer MahaBERT-v2. The best system achieves 88.67\% token-level accuracy and a macro-F1 of 81.67% over 15 evaluated tag classes. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and trained model checkpoints to foster further research in Marathi NLP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：L3Cube-MahaPOS：马拉地语词性标注数据集与BERT模型
词性标注（POS）是自然语言处理的基础任务，支撑机器翻译、信息抽取和句法分析。尽管马拉地语使用者超过8300万，位列全球最常用语言前20名，但其标注语料库和标准化评估基准严重匮乏。马拉地语因其丰富的形态学、相对自由的词序、缺乏大小写规范及与印地语和英语广泛混合的特点，对计算建模构成独特挑战。我们提出L3Cube-MahaPOS，这是包含32,354条人工标注句子的马拉地语黄金标准词性标注数据集，句子源自新闻文本。标注工作由马拉地语熟练标注员团队完全手动完成，遵循16标签的通用依存关系对齐方案。结构化预处理流程涵盖Unicode标准化、天城文感知分词及噪声过滤，确保各数据集划分标签一致性。我们在HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM+CharCNN、MuRIL及马拉地语专用Transformer MahaBERT-v2六类模型上对数据集进行基准测试。最佳系统在15个评估标签类别上实现88.67%的词级准确率和81.67%的宏F1值。我们发布该数据集、标注指南及训练模型检查点，以促进马拉地语自然语言处理研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of annotated resources for Marathi, a language with over 83 million speakers, this work introduces L3Cube-MahaPOS, a manually annotated gold-standard POS tagging dataset of 32,354 sentences from news text. The dataset employs a 16-tag Universal Dependencies scheme and includes a preprocessing pipeline for Unicode normalization, Devanagari-aware tokenization, and noise filtering to ensure label consistency. Six model families—HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and MahaBERT-v2—are evaluated, with MahaBERT-v2 achieving the best performance: 88.67% token-level accuracy and 81.67% macro-F1 across 15 tag classes.
鉴于马拉地语拥有超过8300万使用者却缺乏标注语料，本研究构建了L3Cube-MahaPOS数据集，包含32354条人工标注的新闻文本句子，采用16标签的通用依存关系标注体系，并通过Unicode标准化、天城文感知分词及噪声过滤预处理流程确保标签一致性。在HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM+CharCNN、MuRIL和马拉地语专用Transformer MahaBERT-v2六类模型上进行评估，其中MahaBERT-v2表现最优，达到88.67%的词级准确率和81.67%的15类标签宏F1值。
BioMedArena: An Open-source Toolkit for Building and Evaluating Biomedical Deep Research Agents
Authors: Jinge Wu, Hongjian Zhou, Mingde Zeng, Jiayuan Zhu, Junde Wu, Jiazhen Pan, Ayush Noori, Sean Wu, Honghan Wu, Fenglin Liu, David A. Clifton
First: 2026-05-07T12:57:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:07:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reproducing and comparing deep research agents today is hard: the same backbone evaluated on the same benchmark can report different accuracies across papers because the harness and tool registry differ, and integrating a new model into a comparable evaluation surface costs weeks of model-specific engineering. These are symptoms of a broader reproducibility problem in deep research agent research. Here, we introduce BioMedArena, an open-source toolkit that addresses this reproducibility gap and provides an arena for comparing deep research agents under a shared evaluation environment. BioMedArena decouples six layers of biomedical agent evaluation -- benchmark loading, tool exposure, tool selection, harness mode, context management, and scoring -- and exposes 166 biomedical benchmarks and 75 biomedical tools across 9 functional families. Adding a new model, benchmark, or tool can be accomplished with a few-line provider adapter. Beyond evaluation infrastructure, BioMedArena ships a library of high-quality reference components: 6 agent harnesses (including our proposed Mutual-Evolve) and 6 context-management strategies, any of which can be equipped on any backbone. Equipping these components substantially improves all 12 backbones; on each of 8 representative biomedical benchmarks, the best equipped backbone surpasses prior state-of-the-art (SOTA), by 15.01 percentage points on average. The toolkit, configurations, and per-task traces are available at https://github.com/AI-in-Health/BioMedArena.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BioMedArena：构建与评估生物医学深度研究代理的开源工具包
当前复现和比较深度研究代理困难重重：相同骨干模型在相同基准上评估时，不同论文报告的准确率各异，原因在于评估框架和工具注册表不同，且将新模型集成到可比评估环境中需耗费数周的模型特定工程工作。这反映了深度研究代理研究中更广泛的可复现性问题。为此，我们推出了BioMedArena，一个开源工具包，旨在弥合这一可复现性差距，并提供一个共享评估环境下的代理比较平台。BioMedArena将生物医学代理评估的六个层面——基准加载、工具暴露、工具选择、框架模式、上下文管理与评分——解耦，并公开了9个功能家族中的166个生物医学基准和75个工具。添加新模型、基准或工具只需几行提供者适配器代码。除评估基础设施外，BioMedArena还附带高质量参考组件库：6个代理框架（含我们提出的Mutual-Evolve）和6种上下文管理策略，任一组件均可与任意骨干模型搭配使用。配备这些组件可显著提升所有12个骨干模型；在8个代表性生物医学基准中，最佳配备模型平均超越先前最先进水平（SOTA）15.01个百分点。工具包、配置及各任务追踪数据见https://github.com/AI-in-Health/BioMedArena。

Solving Inverse Problems of Chaotic Systems with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching
Authors: Peiyan Hu, Jian Zhang, Jiashu Pan, Ruiqi Feng, Tao Zhang, Zhi-Ming Ma, Yuan-Sen Ting, Gongjie Li, Tailin Wu
First: 2026-06-23T17:07:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:07:47+00:00
Comments: 50 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling chaotic systems is crucial yet challenging. Inverse problems in chaotic dynamics, namely inferring initial conditions from final states, remain largely unsolved because of ill-posedness, non-uniqueness, instability, and potentially chaotic time-reverse dynamics. We address this open problem with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching (Bi-CFM), which learns bidirectional mappings between distributions of initial and final states to capture the stochasticity of chaotic evolution and mitigate exponential error accumulation over time. Furthermore, for systems with conservation laws, we extend it to Conservation-constrained Bi-CFM (CBi-CFM). Across the classic Lorenz, Circuit, and high-dimensional Lorenz 96 systems, Bi-CFM improves five distribution-level metrics over baselines while achieving a speedup of more than two orders of magnitude. In the three-body planet-planet scattering problem in planetary dynamics, CBi-CFM better respects conservation laws, with conservation errors comparable to those of the ground truth. Finally, on real observations of globular clusters, collisional million-body systems shaped by $\sim 10^{10}$ years (10 Gyr) of evolution, our method represents an advance in accuracy, establishing a scalable route to solving inverse problems of long-timescale real-world chaotic dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用双向条件流匹配求解混沌系统的逆问题
混沌系统的建模至关重要但极具挑战性。混沌动力学中的逆问题——即从最终状态推断初始条件——由于问题不适定、解不唯一、不稳定以及可能的混沌时间反演动力学，至今尚未得到充分解决。我们通过双向条件流匹配（Bi-CFM）解决这一开放性问题，该方法学习初始状态与最终状态分布之间的双向映射，以捕捉混沌演化中的随机性并缓解随时间累积的指数级误差。此外，对于具有守恒律的系统，我们将其扩展为守恒约束双向条件流匹配（CBi-CFM）。在经典的洛伦兹系统、电路系统和高维洛伦兹96系统中，Bi-CFM在五个分布级指标上优于基线方法，同时速度提升超过两个数量级。在行星动力学中的三体行星散射问题中，CBi-CFM更尊重守恒律，其守恒误差与真实值相当。最后，在球状星团的真实观测数据上（这些系统经历了约10^10年（100亿年）的演化），我们的方法在准确性上取得进展，为解决长期尺度真实世界混沌动力学的逆问题提供了可扩展的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of inferring initial states from final observations in chaotic systems due to ill-posedness and instability, the paper introduces Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching (Bi-CFM), a method that learns bidirectional mappings between initial and final state distributions to capture stochastic evolution and suppress error accumulation. For systems obeying conservation laws, it extends to Conservation-constrained Bi-CFM (CBi-CFM). Experiments on Lorenz, Circuit, and Lorenz 96 systems show Bi-CFM outperforms baselines across five distribution metrics and achieves over two orders of magnitude speedup. In planetary scattering simulations, CBi-CFM preserves conservation laws more accurately than alternatives. Applied to real globular cluster data, it demonstrates improved accuracy for long-timescale chaotic dynamics, offering a scalable solution to inverse problems in complex physical systems.
为解决混沌系统中由初始状态反推最终状态的逆问题（因病态性、非唯一性及时间反演不稳定而难以求解），本文提出双向条件流匹配（Bi-CFM）方法，通过学习初始与最终状态分布间的双向映射以捕捉混沌演化中的随机性并抑制误差累积；对满足守恒律的系统，进一步提出守恒约束Bi-CFM（CBi-CFM）。在Lorenz、电路、Lorenz 96及行星散射等系统中，Bi-CFM在五项分布级指标上优于基线方法且速度提升两个数量级，CBi-CFM在守恒误差上接近真实值；在真实球状星团数据上，该方法在长时标混沌动力学逆问题中实现更高精度，提供可扩展的解决方案。
SHERLOC: Structured Diagnostic Localization for Code Repair Agents
Authors: Hovhannes Tamoyan, Sean Narenthiran, Erik Arakelyan, Mira Mezini, Boris Ginsburg
First: 2026-06-23T17:05:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:05:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents solve repository-level coding tasks through multi-turn tool use, but utilize half their budget on locating faults before editing. Dedicated localization frameworks have emerged, yet are still evaluated as file retrieval rather than actionable diagnosis, producing locations without the diagnostic context a repair agent needs. We introduce SHERLOC (Structured Hypothesis-driven Exploration and Reasoning for Localization), a training-free framework pairing a reasoning LLM with compact repository tools and self-recovery, without fine-tuning or multi-agent orchestration. SHERLOC reaches state-of-the-art localization across model scales: 84.33% accuracy@1 on SWE-Bench Lite and 81.27% recall@1 on SWE-Bench Verified; at ~30B parameters, it matches or outperforms other agentic methods. Injecting our locations and diagnostic findings into repair agents yields, on average, +5.95 pp resolve rate on SWE-Bench Verified while cutting localization and total tokens by 36.7% and 23.1%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SHERLOC：面向代码修复代理的结构化诊断定位
大型语言模型代理通过多轮工具使用解决仓库级编码任务，但其中一半预算用于定位故障前编辑。尽管已出现专用定位框架，但其评估仍局限于文件检索而非可操作的诊断，仅提供位置而无修复代理所需的诊断上下文。我们提出SHERLOC（结构化假设驱动探索与推理定位），这是一种无需训练的框架，结合推理型LLM、紧凑仓库工具及自恢复能力，无需微调或多代理协调。SHERLOC在不同模型规模下均达到最先进定位精度：在SWE-Bench Lite上准确率@1达84.33%，在SWE-Bench Verified上召回率@1达81.27%；在约300亿参数规模下，其表现与其它代理方法相当或更优。将我们的定位结果与诊断发现注入修复代理后，平均在SWE-Bench Verified上提升5.95个百分点的解决率，同时降低定位和总token消耗36.7%和23.1%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current code repair agents that waste half their computational budget on fault localization without diagnostic context, SHERLOC introduces a training-free framework that pairs a reasoning LLM with compact repository tools and self-recovery mechanisms to generate actionable diagnostic hypotheses. Evaluated across model scales, SHERLOC achieves state-of-the-art localization accuracy (84.33% @1 on SWE-Bench Lite) and recall (81.27% @1 on SWE-Bench Verified), outperforming or matching other agentic methods even at 30B parameters. When integrated into repair agents, SHERLOC boosts resolve rates by 5.95 percentage points on SWE-Bench Verified while reducing localization tokens by 36.7% and total tokens by 23.1%.
为了解决现有定位方法仅作为文件检索而缺乏诊断上下文的问题，SHERLOC提出一种无需训练的框架，结合推理型大语言模型、紧凑的仓库工具和自恢复机制，生成可操作的诊断假设。在不同规模模型上评估，SHERLOC实现业界领先定位精度：在SWE-Bench Lite上准确率@1达84.33%，在SWE-Bench Verified上召回率@1达81.27%，甚至在30B参数规模下超越或持平其他代理方法。当集成至修复代理时，SHERLOC平均提升解决成功率5.95个百分点，同时降低定位和总token消耗36.7%和23.1%。
Vikhr: The Family of Open-Source Instruction-Tuned Large Language Models for Russian
Authors: Aleksandr Nikolich, Konstantin Korolev, Sergei Bratchikov, Igor Kiselev, Artem Shelmanov
First: 2024-05-22T18:58:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:03:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There has been a surge in the development of various Large Language Models (LLMs). However, text generation for languages other than English often faces significant challenges, including poor generation quality and reduced computational performance due to the disproportionate representation of tokens in the model's vocabulary. In this work, we address these issues by developing a pipeline for the adaptation of English-oriented pre-trained models to other languages and constructing efficient bilingual LLMs. Using this pipeline, we construct Vikhr, a series of bilingual open-source instruction-following LLMs designed specifically for the Russian language. ``Vikhr'' refers to the name of the Mistral LLM series and means a ``strong gust of wind.'' Unlike previous Russian-language models that typically rely on LoRA adapters on top of English-oriented models, sacrificing performance for lower training costs, Vikhr features an adapted tokenizer vocabulary and undergoes the continued pre-training and instruction tuning of all weights. This not only enhances the model's performance but also significantly improves its computational and contextual efficiency. We also expanded the instruction datasets and corpora for continued pre-training. The model weights, instruction sets, and code are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vikhr：面向俄语的开源指令微调大语言模型系列
近年来，各类大语言模型（LLM）发展迅速，但非英语语言的文本生成常面临生成质量差、计算性能下降等问题，根源在于模型词汇表中非英语语言token占比失衡。本文通过构建英语导向预训练模型适配其他语言的流程，并构建高效的双语LLM，解决上述问题。基于该流程，我们构建了Vikhr——专为俄语设计的开源指令遵循双语LLM系列。“Vikhr”源自Mistral系列LLM名称，意为“强风”。与以往依赖LoRA适配器、牺牲性能以降低训练成本的俄语模型不同，Vikhr采用适配的分词器词汇表，并对全部权重进行持续预训练和指令微调，不仅提升模型性能，还显著增强其计算与上下文效率。同时，我们扩展了指令数据集和持续预训练语料库。模型权重、指令集及代码均已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of Russian-language LLMs due to token imbalance and reliance on English-oriented models, this work introduces Vikhr, a family of open-source bilingual instruction-tuned LLMs for Russian. The method adapts tokenizers, continues pre-training, and fine-tunes all model weights on Russian data, avoiding lightweight LoRA adapters that compromise quality. Experimental results show Vikhr achieves superior generation quality and computational efficiency compared to prior Russian models, while also benefiting from expanded instruction datasets and corpora.
为解决俄语大语言模型因词表不平衡和依赖英语模型导致的生成质量差与计算效率低的问题，本研究提出Vikhr，一套专为俄语设计的开源双语指令微调大语言模型。方法通过自建管道对英语预训练模型进行改造，包括构建俄语优化词表，并对全部模型权重进行持续预训练与指令微调，而非采用轻量级LoRA适配器。实验结果表明，Vikhr在生成质量与计算效率上显著优于以往俄语模型，同时得益于扩展的指令数据集与训练语料。
High-Fidelity Synthetic Transmission Electron Microscopy Image Generation Using Diffusion Probabilistic Models for Data-Limited Semiconductor Metrology
Authors: Johannes Boehm, Bappaditya Dey
First: 2026-06-23T17:02:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:02:58+00:00
Comments: To be presented at the 2026 International Symposium ELMAR, published by IEEE in the conference proceedings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advanced semiconductor nodes drastically increased demand for Transmission Electron Microscopy (TEM), yet destructive sample preparation, slow imaging and high costs severely limit the availability of diverse datasets needed for downstream machine learning (ML). Synthetic data generation is becoming essential, but current generative models often miss TEM-specific noise, structural detail, and stochastic variability crucial for evaluation. We present a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) framework for synthetic TEM image generation under extreme data scarcity. A progressive patch-based training strategy scales from low-resolution patches to full images, enabling from-scratch training with only 15 samples. We integrate a custom TrivialAugment adaptation, cross-process domain transfer, classifier guidance, and RePaint-style inpainting, culminating in full-image generation that preserves global structural and spatial relationships in compliance with FAB metrology requirements. Beyond synthesis, we repurpose DDPM feature representations for segmentation, partitioning encoder feature maps to obtain coherent region masks. Our synthetic images achieve up to MS-SSIM > 0.98 and qualitative expert assessment consistent with structural similarity results, facilitating downstream ML training for defect detection, segmentation, and metrology while preserving statistical and physical realism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于扩散概率模型的高保真合成透射电子显微镜图像生成方法，用于数据稀缺条件下的半导体计量
先进半导体节点大幅增加了对透射电子显微镜（TEM）的需求，但破坏性样品制备、成像缓慢和高成本严重限制了用于下游机器学习（ML）的多样化数据集的获取。合成数据生成变得至关重要，但当前生成模型常忽略TEM特有的噪声、结构细节和随机变异性，这些对评估至关重要。我们提出了一种去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）框架，用于在极端数据稀缺条件下生成合成TEM图像。采用渐进式分块训练策略，从低分辨率分块扩展至全图像，仅需15个样本即可实现从零训练。我们整合了自定义TrivialAugment适配、跨过程域迁移、分类器引导和RePaint风格修复，最终实现全图像生成，保留全局结构与空间关系，符合FAB计量要求。此外，我们重新利用DDPM特征表示进行分割，将编码器特征图分区以获得连贯区域掩码。合成图像MS-SSIM高达0.98，专家定性评估与结构相似性结果一致，有助于缺陷检测、分割和计量的下游ML训练，同时保持统计与物理真实性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the scarcity of real TEM images in semiconductor metrology, this work introduces a diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) to generate high-fidelity synthetic TEM images from extremely limited data. The method employs a progressive patch-based training strategy starting from low-resolution patches and scaling to full images, enabling training with as few as 15 samples. It integrates custom data augmentation, domain transfer, classifier guidance, and inpainting to preserve structural and spatial fidelity. Experimental results show synthetic images achieving MS-SSIM > 0.98 and expert-level visual similarity, enabling effective downstream ML tasks like defect detection and segmentation while maintaining physical and statistical realism.
为应对半导体制造中TEM图像数据稀缺的问题，该研究提出一种基于扩散概率模型（DDPM）的高保真合成图像生成方法。该方法采用渐进式块训练策略，并结合自定义增强、跨域迁移、分类器引导和修复技术，以保持图像结构与空间关系。实验表明，合成图像与真实图像MS-SSIM值超过0.98，专家定性评估一致，可有效支持缺陷检测与分割等下游机器学习任务，同时保持物理真实性和统计一致性。
MANGO: Automated Multi-Agent Test Oracle Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Pablo Valle, Shaukat Ali, Aitor Arrieta, Lionel Briand
First: 2026-06-23T17:00:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T17:00:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging robotic control systems that integrate perception, language understanding, and action generation in a unified architecture. Existing testing approaches for VLA-enabled robots rely on manually constructed symbolic test oracles that determine task success from final environment states. These oracles are costly to construct, require domain expertise, and are often tightly coupled to specific tasks and environments, limiting scalability and reuse. Furthermore, they provide only end-state assessments of task outcomes, offering limited insight into intermediate behavior and fault localization. To address these limitations, we introduce MANGO, a multi-agent framework that automatically generates fine-grained oracles from natural-language descriptions of robotic tasks. MANGO first generates a reusable library of atomic tasks, then generates simulator-grounded oracle definitions for each atomic task, and finally produces executable fine-grained oracles by decomposing complex instructions into ordered sequences of atomic actions and corresponding oracles. The framework uses collaborative Generator, Assessor, and Judge agents that iteratively refine generated artifacts through structured feedback. We evaluate MANGO on the LIBERO_10 and RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop benchmarks. Results show that MANGO generates executable, fine-grained oracles that detect a similar number of failures as symbolic oracles while accurately localizing them and providing richer diagnostic information. Through ablation studies, we further analyzed component contributions and the effect of initial task set, while preserving oracle quality. Overall, the results show the feasibility and effectiveness of test oracle generation for VLA-enabled robots testing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MANGO：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的自动化多智能体测试预言生成
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型是新兴的机器人控制系统，其统一架构融合了感知、语言理解和动作生成。现有针对VLA机器人测试的方法依赖人工构建的符号化测试预言，通过最终环境状态判断任务成功与否。此类预言构建成本高、需领域专业知识，且常与特定任务和环境紧密耦合，限制了可扩展性和复用性。此外，它们仅提供任务结果的终点评估，难以洞察中间行为和故障定位。为解决上述问题，我们提出MANGO，一种多智能体框架，可从机器人任务的自然语言描述中自动生成细粒度预言。MANGO首先生成可复用的原子任务库，然后为每个原子任务生成模拟器锚定的预言定义，最后通过将复杂指令分解为有序原子动作序列及其对应预言，生成可执行的细粒度预言。该框架采用协作式生成器、评估者和裁判智能体，通过结构化反馈迭代优化生成产物。我们在LIBERO_10和RoboCasa人形桌面基准上评估MANGO，结果表明其生成的可执行细粒度预言能检测与符号预言相似数量的失败案例，同时准确定位故障并提供更丰富的诊断信息。通过消融实验，我们进一步分析了各组件贡献及初始任务集的影响，同时保持预言质量。总体而言，结果验证了面向VLA机器人测试的测试预言生成的可行性和有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and lack of scalability of manually crafted symbolic test oracles for Vision-Language-Action models, MANGO introduces a multi-agent framework that automatically generates fine-grained test oracles from natural-language task descriptions. The method first builds a reusable library of atomic tasks, then creates simulator-grounded oracle definitions for each, and finally composes executable oracles by sequencing atomic actions and their oracles. Three collaborative agents—Generator, Assessor, and Judge—iteratively refine outputs through structured feedback. Evaluated on LIBERO_10 and RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop, MANGO’s oracles match symbolic oracles in failure detection while offering superior fault localization and diagnostic detail, with ablation studies confirming component effectiveness and robustness to initial task set variations.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型测试中人工构建符号化测试断言效率低、难以复用的问题，MANGO提出一种多智能体框架，可从自然语言任务描述自动生成细粒度测试断言。该方法首先构建可复用的原子任务库，再为每个原子任务生成仿真对齐的断言定义，最终通过组合原子动作与断言生成可执行断言。三类协作智能体（生成器、评估者、裁判者）通过结构化反馈迭代优化输出。在LIBERO_10与RoboCasa人形桌面机器人基准测试中，MANGO断言在故障检测数量上与人工断言相当，同时提供更精准的故障定位与诊断信息，消融实验进一步验证了各组件的有效性及初始任务集的鲁棒性。
Vision-Language Model Reasoning for Contextual Semantic Mapping in Intralogistics
Authors: Marvin Rüdt, Hao Pang, Constantin Enke, Zäzilia Seibold, Kai Furmans
First: 2026-06-23T16:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:59:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at IEEE ETFA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous mobile robots operating in intralogistics environments rely on geometric maps for localization and navigation, but lack semantic understanding of objects and their contextual properties. We present a contextual semantic mapping pipeline that combines SLAM-based geometric mapping, SAM-based instance segmentation, instance clustering, and VLM multi-view reasoning to produce a contextual semantic map representation encoding geometric structure, object class, and object movability. By aggregating observations across multiple viewpoints and querying a VLM in a zero-shot, open-vocabulary setting, the pipeline infers contextual object properties--here demonstrated through movability--without requiring task-specific training or predefined object categories. We evaluate three VLMs under two prompting strategies and conduct a component-wise analysis of the pipeline. The proposed pipeline achieves 98.93 % mIoU for semantic classification and 89.17 % mAcc for object movability estimation. Component analysis identifies VLM reasoning as the primary bottleneck for contextual understanding and instance clustering as the main limitation for panoptic performance. The resulting semantic map supports context-aware filtering and robust navigation in dynamic intralogistics environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向内部物流情境语义映射的视觉-语言模型推理
在内部物流环境中运行的自主移动机器人依赖几何地图进行定位与导航，但缺乏对物体及其情境属性的语义理解。本文提出一种情境语义映射流程，结合基于SLAM的几何映射、基于SAM的实例分割、实例聚类及VLM多视角推理，生成编码几何结构、物体类别和物体可移动性的语义地图。通过跨多视角聚合观测并在零样本、开放词汇设定下查询VLM，该流程可推断情境物体属性（本文以可移动性为例），无需任务特定训练或预定义物体类别。我们在两种提示策略下评估三种VLM，并对流程各组件进行分析。所提流程在语义分类上达到98.93% mIoU，在物体可移动性估计上达到89.17% mAcc。组件分析表明，VLM推理是情境理解的主要瓶颈，实例聚类是全景性能的主要限制因素。生成的语义地图支持动态内部物流环境中情境感知过滤与鲁棒导航。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for semantic understanding in intralogistics environments where autonomous robots rely on geometric maps but lack contextual object knowledge, this work proposes a pipeline that integrates SLAM-based mapping, SAM instance segmentation, clustering, and VLM multi-view reasoning to generate contextual semantic maps encoding geometry, object class, and movability. The method leverages zero-shot, open-vocabulary VLM queries across multiple viewpoints to infer object properties without task-specific training or predefined categories. Experimental results show 98.93% mIoU for semantic classification and 89.17% mAcc for movability estimation across three VLMs under two prompting strategies, with component analysis revealing VLM reasoning as the main bottleneck for contextual understanding and instance clustering as the key limitation for panoptic performance.
为解决自主移动机器人在仓储环境中依赖几何地图但缺乏语义与上下文理解的问题，该研究提出一种融合SLAM建图、SAM实例分割、聚类与VLM多视角推理的语义映射管道，生成包含几何结构、物体类别及可移动性等上下文信息的语义地图。方法通过零样本、开放词汇的VLM多视角查询推断物体属性，无需特定任务训练或预定义类别。实验表明，在三种VLM与两种提示策略下，语义分类mIoU达98.93%，可移动性估计mAcc达89.17%，组件分析指出VLM推理是上下文理解的主要瓶颈，实例聚类是全景性能的主要限制因素。
Target-Aware Linear Regression Under Distribution Shift
Authors: Zhewen Hou, Tian Zheng
First: 2026-06-22T02:24:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:56:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distribution shift between training and deployment is a pervasive challenge for modern AI systems. In many cases, the target marginals of covariates and response are known or specified through population-level observations, boundary conditions, properties of simulator configurations, or alignment-time distributional constraints. Such knowledge may provide valuable side information for regression estimation. We study this problem in the multivariate linear regression setting with a stable conditional mean $E[Y\mid X]$ across source and target, and identify the hybrid-loss estimator, which jointly incorporates both target marginals, as a benchmark target-aware estimator. Its direct computation, however, requires solving a coupled nonlinear optimization that is expensive at scale. Our main contribution is to develop and evaluate two computationally tractable alternatives: a constrained moment-matching estimator and a two-stage estimator that augments ordinary least squares with a calibration step. For all three estimators, we derive and compare closed-form asymptotic mean squared errors, yielding conditions under which the tractable alternatives match or closely approximate the hybrid benchmark, and regimes in which they do not. Monte Carlo experiments across three controlled shift regimes validate the theoretical results, investigate the accuracy-runtime tradeoffs among the three estimators, and translate into guidance on estimator choice. In particular, the two-stage estimator nearly matches the hybrid benchmark in the high signal-to-noise regime at essentially no additional cost, providing theoretical grounding for empirical observations in nonlinear settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布偏移下的目标感知线性回归
训练与部署阶段之间的分布偏移是现代人工智能系统普遍面临的挑战。在许多情况下，协变量和响应变量的目标边缘分布可通过人口层面观测、边界条件、模拟器配置属性或对齐时的分布约束来已知或指定。此类知识可为回归估计提供有价值的辅助信息。我们在协变量与响应变量条件均值在源域与目标域稳定的情况下，研究多变量线性回归问题，并将同时结合目标边缘分布的混合损失估计量作为基准目标感知估计量。然而，其直接计算需求解耦合非线性优化问题，在大规模场景下代价高昂。本文主要贡献是提出并评估两种计算可行的替代方案：约束矩匹配估计量和两阶段估计量（在普通最小二乘基础上增加校准步骤）。对三种估计量，我们推导并比较其闭式渐近均方误差，得出在何种条件下可行替代方案可匹配或近似基准估计量，以及在何种情形下无法匹配。通过三个受控偏移场景的蒙特卡洛实验，验证理论结果，探究三种估计量的精度-运行时权衡，并转化为估计量选择的指导。特别是，在高信噪比情形下，两阶段估计量几乎与混合基准估计量相当，且几乎无额外成本，为非线性设置中的经验观察提供了理论依据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of distribution shift between training and deployment in AI systems, this paper explores target-aware linear regression where target marginals of covariates and response are known. It introduces the hybrid-loss estimator as a theoretical benchmark that jointly uses target marginals but is computationally intractable. To address this, the authors propose two tractable alternatives: a constrained moment-matching estimator and a two-stage estimator that augments OLS with calibration. Theoretical analysis derives and compares their asymptotic mean squared errors, identifying regimes where each estimator matches or approximates the hybrid benchmark. Monte Carlo experiments across three shift regimes confirm these theoretical findings, showing that the two-stage estimator nearly matches the hybrid benchmark in high signal-to-noise settings with minimal additional cost.
为应对实际AI部署中训练与部署分布偏移的挑战，本文研究了目标边际已知情况下的目标感知线性回归。提出了一种联合利用目标边际的混合损失估计器作为理论基准，但其计算代价高昂。为此，作者设计了两种计算高效的替代方案：约束矩匹配估计器和两阶段估计器（在普通最小二乘基础上增加校准步骤）。理论分析推导了三类估计器的渐近均方误差，明确了它们在何种条件下可逼近基准。蒙特卡洛实验在三种偏移场景下验证了理论结果，表明在高信噪比环境下，两阶段估计器几乎达到混合基准性能，且几乎无额外计算成本。
Large-Language-Model Discovery of Quantum LDPC Codes through Structured Concept Evolution
Authors: Zidu Liu, Florian Marquardt
First: 2026-06-23T16:56:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:56:24+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum computers could outperform classical machines on important problems, but only if the errors that pervade quantum hardware can be corrected at scale. Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer a promising route to this goal by combining sparse parity checks with finite encoding rate and growing distance, but their construction remains a challenging discrete design problem. Here we introduce structured concept evolution (SCE), a search framework that pairs a large language model with a structured algebraic mutation grammar to discover lifted-product code families, a class of CSS qLDPC codes. Instead of asking the LLM to design codes from first principles, SCE evolves structured concepts consisting of algebraic specifications paired with executable programs that realize them, using hierarchical mutations that modify the group algebra, protograph geometry, or base space. Running SCE, we discover a diverse set of competitive code families, ranging from abelian constructions to families over non-abelian groups beyond those underlying standard designs such as bivariate-bicycle codes, and characterize them under code-capacity depolarizing noise with BP+OSD decoding. These results are obtained with lightweight models (GPT-5.4-mini and GPT-5.4-nano).
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过结构化概念演化利用大语言模型发现量子LDPC码
量子计算机有望在重要问题上超越经典计算机，但前提是量子硬件中的错误必须大规模纠正。量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC）通过结合稀疏奇偶校验、有限编码率和增长距离，为实现这一目标提供了有前景的途径，但其构造仍是一个具有挑战性的离散设计问题。本文引入结构化概念演化（SCE），一种将大语言模型与结构化代数突变文法相结合的搜索框架，用于发现提升积码族（CSS qLDPC码的一种）。SCE不直接要求大语言模型从第一性原理设计码，而是演化由代数规范与可执行程序配对的结构化概念，通过分层突变修改群代数、原型图几何或基础空间。运行SCE后，我们发现了一系列多样且具有竞争力的码族，从阿贝尔构造到超越标准设计（如双变量自行车码）所基于的非阿贝尔群码族，并在码容量去极化噪声下，采用BP+OSD译码对其进行了表征。这些结果由轻量级模型（GPT-5.4-mini和GPT-5.4-nano）获得。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to construct scalable quantum error-correcting codes, this work introduces Structured Concept Evolution (SCE), a framework that combines a large language model with an algebraic mutation grammar to discover new families of quantum LDPC codes. Rather than generating codes from scratch, SCE evolves structured concepts—comprising algebraic specifications and executable programs—that are mutated hierarchically at the level of group algebra, protograph geometry, or base space. Experiments with lightweight LLMs (GPT-5.4-mini and GPT-5.4-nano) yielded diverse, competitive code families, including non-abelian constructions beyond standard designs like bivariate-bicycle codes, which were evaluated under code-capacity depolarizing noise using BP+OSD decoding.
为解决可扩展量子纠错码的构造难题，本文提出结构化概念演化（SCE）框架，结合大语言模型与结构化代数突变语法，通过层级突变（针对群代数、原型图几何或基空间）演化代数规范与可执行程序对，发现多种有竞争力的量子LDPC码族。实验使用轻量级模型（GPT-5.4-mini和GPT-5.4-nano），成功生成包括非阿贝尔群构造在内的多样化码族，并在BP+OSD译码下评估其在码容量退相干噪声下的性能。
DDStereo: Efficient Dual Decoder Transformers for Stereo 3D Road Anomaly Detection
Authors: Shiyi Mu, Zichong Gu, Zhiqi Ai, Yilin Gao, Shugong Xu
First: 2026-06-23T16:54:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:54:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stereo-based 3D object detection still faces two critical safety challenges: real-time performance and open-set generalization. Existing stereo 3D methods typically achieve twice the accuracy of monocular methods but suffer from significantly lower inference speeds, making them unsuitable for real-time applications. Meanwhile, recent advances in open-world detection have introduced open-set and open-vocabulary algorithms in monocular 2D and 3D settings, yet stereo-based open-set detection remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose DDStereo, a novel Dual-Decoder Stereo Transformer for real-time open-set 3D object detection. DDStereo features two lightweight decoder branches: one for open-set foreground 2D detection and the other for 3D attribute regression. These decoders share object-level queries to achieve unified target-level alignment. To enhance inference efficiency, we designed a compact disparity feature extractor and a streamlined decoder architecture. Experiments on public stereo 3D benchmarks demonstrate that DDStereo achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under both closed-set and open-set protocols. Notably, our method surpasses existing stereo 3D detectors in inference speed and, for the first time, achieves real-time performance comparable to monocular approaches.
中文标题/摘要

Motivated by the need for real-time and open-set generalization in stereo 3D road anomaly detection, the authors propose DDStereo, a Dual-Decoder Stereo Transformer that combines lightweight 2D foreground detection and 3D attribute regression via shared object-level queries. The model employs a compact disparity feature extractor and streamlined decoder architecture to boost inference speed without sacrificing accuracy.
