为解决立体3D道路异常检测中实时性与开放集泛化能力不足的问题，DDStereo提出一种双解码器Transformer架构，通过共享目标级查询实现2D前景检测与3D属性回归的统一对齐。为提升推理效率，采用紧凑的视差特征提取器与轻量解码器结构。实验表明，该方法在公开立体3D数据集上同时达到闭集与开集场景下的最先进精度，并首次在推理速度上超越现有立体方法，实现与单目方法相当的实时性能。
Efficient reduction of stellar contamination and noise in planetary transmission spectra using neural networks
Authors: David S. Duque-Castaño, Lauren Flor-Torres, Jorge I. Zuluaga
First: 2026-02-10T22:07:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:51:01+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 12 figures, 3 tables. Submitted to Astronomy & Astrophysics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Context: The characterization of exoplanetary atmospheres has been transformed by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), whose infrared sensitivity enables transmission spectroscopy at unprecedented precision. However, stellar heterogeneities (e.g., spots and faculae) remain a dominant source of contamination that can bias atmospheric retrievals if not properly corrected. Aims: We present a methodology for reducing stellar contamination and instrument-specific noise from exoplanet transmission spectra using neural networks, in particular the so-called Denoising AutoEncoders (DAE). Our goals are to enable fast, accurate corrections that improve the reliability of atmospheric parameter retrievals and to promote the use of unsupervised algorithms for efficient data processing. Methods: We designed and trained DAE architectures using large synthetic datasets of terrestrial (TRAPPIST-1e analogues) and sub-Neptune (K2-18b analogues) planets. Atmospheric retrieval experiments were then performed on contaminated spectra in order to compare our deep-learning approach against standard correction methods in terms of accuracy and computational cost. Results: Our autoencoders successfully reconstruct uncontaminated spectra, preserving essential molecular features even in low-S/N regimes. In retrieval tests, the denoising autoencoder pre-processing reduces bias in retrieved abundance parameters compared to uncorrected observations. Notably, our method matches the accuracy of simultaneous stellar-contamination fitting while maintaining a much lower computational cost, typically one order of magnitude smaller. Conclusions: These results demonstrate that DAEs outperform conventional correction methods in computational efficiency while maintaining high accuracy, paving the way for their integration into future atmospheric characterization pipelines for both rocky and giant exoplanets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用神经网络高效降低系外行星透射光谱中的恒星污染与噪声
背景：詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜（JWST）凭借其红外敏感性，已将系外行星大气表征推向前所未有的精度，但恒星异质性（如黑子和光斑）仍是主要污染源，若未正确校正将导致大气参数推导偏差。目标：我们提出一种利用神经网络（特别是去噪自编码器DAE）降低系外行星透射光谱中恒星污染和仪器特定噪声的方法，旨在实现快速、准确的校正，提升大气参数推导的可靠性，并推广无监督算法在高效数据处理中的应用。方法：我们基于类地行星（TRAPPIST-1e模拟）和次海王星行星（K2-18b模拟）的大规模合成数据集，设计并训练DAE架构。随后在受污染光谱上进行大气反演实验，比较深度学习方法与传统校正方法在准确性和计算成本上的表现。结果：我们的自编码器成功重建无污染光谱，在低信噪比条件下仍能保留关键分子特征。在反演测试中，去噪自编码器预处理显著降低推导丰度参数的偏差，且其精度可媲美同时拟合恒星污染的方法，但计算成本通常低一个数量级。结论：这些结果表明，DAE在保持高精度的同时，计算效率优于传统校正方法，为未来岩石行星和巨行星大气表征流程中集成该方法铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to correct stellar contamination and instrument noise in exoplanet transmission spectra from JWST data, this study employs Denoising AutoEncoders (DAEs) to reconstruct clean spectra from contaminated observations. Trained on synthetic datasets mimicking terrestrial and sub-Neptune exoplanets, the DAEs effectively remove stellar heterogeneities while preserving molecular features even at low signal-to-noise ratios. Experimental comparisons with standard correction methods show that DAE pre-processing reduces bias in atmospheric retrievals and achieves comparable accuracy to simultaneous fitting methods, but at roughly one order of magnitude lower computational cost, making it a more efficient tool for large-scale atmospheric characterization.
为解决詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜观测中恒星斑点和仪器噪声对系外行星透射光谱的干扰问题，该研究采用去噪自编码器（DAE）对受污染光谱进行重建。模型在模拟类地球和次海王星行星的合成数据集上训练，即使在信噪比较低的情况下仍能保留关键分子特征。实验表明，DAE预处理可显著降低大气参数的系统偏差，其精度与同时拟合恒星污染的方法相当，但计算成本低一个数量级，为未来系外行星大气分析流程提供了高效可行的解决方案。
OrbitForge: Text-to-3D Scene Generation via Reconstruction-Anchored Video Synthesis
Authors: Chenrui Fan, Paolo Favaro
First: 2026-06-23T16:50:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:50:47+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 33 figures, 19 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generic text-to-video models can be used as rich open-world scene priors. Despite the high quality of today's generated videos, they do not directly yield reliable 3D assets: camera motion is difficult to control, view coverage is partial, and frames often contain inconsistencies across time. We introduce OrbitForge, an adapter built from frozen video priors and per-prompt Gaussian Splatting reconstruction optimization that converts a single text-generated video into a canonical closed-orbit 3D Gaussian Splatting scene. We use 3D reconstruction as an anchor to improve the 3D consistency of the generated video. We obtain a preliminary 3D reconstruction from a first generated video via Deformable Gaussian Splatting with a robust MedianGS proxy. We render views from a prescribed orbit to detect missing viewpoints. OrbitForge uses the text-to-video model to complete only the missing views, and reconstructs the completed orbit into a final Gaussian Splatting scene. This design requires no task-specific video or multiview fine-tuning, avoids per-prompt score-distillation optimization, and does not progressively generate views one step at a time. We further argue that this setting demands coverage-aware evaluation: local smoothness alone rewards methods that never attempt a full orbit. On a frozen 300-prompt T3Bench-derived audit, OrbitForge reconstruction attains a 359.0-degree measured median span, raises originally unsupported-bin Q10 ImageReward from 8.07 to 16.36 relative to MedianGS-only reconstruction, while remaining competitive with VideoMV on the coverage-quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OrbitForge：通过重建锚定视频合成实现文本到3D场景生成
通用的文本到视频模型可作为丰富的开放世界场景先验。尽管当前生成视频质量很高，但它们无法直接产生可靠的3D资产：摄像机运动难以控制，视角覆盖不完整，帧间常存在时间不一致性。我们引入OrbitForge，这是一种基于冻结视频先验和每个提示的高斯点云重建优化的适配器，可将单个文本生成视频转换为标准闭合轨道的3D高斯点云场景。我们以3D重建作为锚点，提升生成视频的3D一致性。首先通过鲁棒的MedianGS代理，使用可变形高斯点云从首个生成视频中获得初步3D重建。我们从预设轨道渲染视角以检测缺失视角。OrbitForge仅使用文本到视频模型补全缺失视角，并将补全后的轨道重建为最终高斯点云场景。该设计无需特定任务视频或多视角微调，避免每个提示的分数蒸馏优化，也不逐帧逐步生成视角。我们进一步认为，该设置需要覆盖感知评估：仅局部平滑性会奖励那些从未尝试完整轨道的方法。在冻结的300提示T3Bench衍生审计中，OrbitForge重建实现359.0度测量中位跨度，将原本不支持的Q10 ImageReward从8.07提升至16.36（相对于仅MedianGS重建），同时在覆盖质量上与VideoMV保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to convert high-quality but unreliable text-to-video outputs into consistent 3D scenes, OrbitForge leverages frozen video priors and per-prompt Gaussian Splatting optimization to reconstruct a canonical closed-orbit 3D scene from a single generated video. It first obtains a preliminary 3D reconstruction using Deformable Gaussian Splatting with a MedianGS proxy, then identifies missing viewpoints by rendering from a prescribed orbit. The system uses the text-to-video model to generate only the missing views and reconstructs the full orbit into a final Gaussian Splatting scene without fine-tuning or score-distillation. Experimental results show OrbitForge achieves a 359.0-degree median span, significantly improves ImageReward scores from 8.07 to 16.36 over MedianGS-only methods, and remains competitive with VideoMV on coverage and quality.
为将高质量但不一致的文本生成视频转化为可靠的3D场景，OrbitForge利用冻结的视频先验和每提示的高斯点云优化，从单个生成视频重建出标准闭合轨道的3D场景。它首先通过Deformable Gaussian Splatting结合MedianGS代理获得初步3D重建，再通过预设轨道渲染检测缺失视角，随后仅用文本到视频模型生成缺失视角并整合为最终高斯点云场景，无需特定任务微调或逐帧生成。实验表明，OrbitForge实现359.0度中位跨度，将ImageReward分数从8.07大幅提升至16.36，且在覆盖质量上与VideoMV方法相当。
PAIWorld: A 3D-Consistent World Foundation Model for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yuhang Huang, Xuan Lv, Junyan Xu, Zhiyuan Yu, Jiazhao Zhang, Ruizhen Hu, Wancheng Feng, Shilong Zou, Hewen Xiao, Ziqiao Zhou, Kaiyun Huang, Zhiyu Peng, Juzhan Xu, Hang Zhao, Chenyang Zhu, Renjiao Yi, Yifei Huang, Douhui Wu, Yan Zhang, Kexu Cheng, Chunhe Song, Yunzhi Xue, Xiuhong Zhang, Leitao Guo, Yunji Chen, Bin Wu, Haibin Yu, Kai Xu
First: 2026-06-16T18:23:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:50:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World foundation models (WFMs) are powerful simulators, yet they predominantly operate in a single-view setting and lack the multi-view 3D consistency required for robotic manipulation. While robotic systems rely on multiple cameras (egocentric, eye-to-hand, and wrist-mounted) for policy learning, current multi-view world models simply concatenate view tokens without explicit geometric reasoning. This causes cross-view object drift, depth inconsistency, and texture misalignment. We trace these failures to two deficiencies: the absence of an explicit inter-view communication mechanism and the lack of a 3D geometric prior. We argue that resolving both simultaneously is necessary and sufficient. To address this, we present PAIWorld, a framework that augments diffusion-transformer world models via three core components: (1) Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention blocks that establish an explicit pathway across views, (2) Geometric Rotary Position Embedding that encodes camera ray directions and extrinsic poses into the attention mechanism, and (3) Latent 3D-REPA, which distills 3D-aware features from frozen 3D foundation models to ensure 3D consistency. Built upon a DiT-based world foundation model, PAIWorld achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D consistency on robotic manipulation benchmarks, ranking 1st on the WorldArena leaderboard and 2nd on the AgiBot-Challenge2026 leaderboard, while enabling downstream applications such as model-based planning, world action models, and multi-view policy post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PAIWorld：面向机器人操作的3D一致性世界基础模型
世界基础模型（WFM）是强大的模拟器，但主要在单视角下运行，缺乏机器人操作所需的多视角3D一致性。尽管机器人系统依赖多个摄像头（如眼在手、腕部安装等）进行策略学习，当前多视角世界模型仅简单拼接视角令牌，缺乏显式几何推理，导致跨视角物体漂移、深度不一致和纹理错位。我们归因于两个缺陷：缺乏显式的跨视角通信机制和缺乏3D几何先验。我们认为同时解决这两个问题既是必要也是充分的。为此，我们提出PAIWorld框架，通过三个核心组件增强扩散-Transformer世界模型：（1）几何感知跨视角注意力模块，建立视角间的显式通信路径；（2）几何旋转位置嵌入，将相机射线方向和外参姿态编码进注意力机制；（3）潜在3D-REPA，从冻结的3D基础模型中蒸馏3D感知特征以确保3D一致性。基于DiT架构的世界基础模型，PAIWorld在机器人操作基准测试中实现领先的多视角3D一致性，在WorldArena排行榜上排名第一，在AgiBot-Challenge2026排行榜上排名第二，同时支持基于模型的规划、世界动作模型及多视角策略后训练等下游应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for 3D-consistent multi-view world models to support robotic manipulation, the authors address cross-view inconsistencies caused by missing geometric reasoning in current methods. PAIWorld introduces a diffusion-transformer framework enhanced with three components: Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention for explicit inter-view communication, Geometric Rotary Position Embedding to encode camera poses and ray directions into attention, and Latent 3D-REPA to distill 3D-aware features from frozen 3D models. Experimental results show PAIWorld achieves top performance on WorldArena and AgiBot-Challenge2026 benchmarks, demonstrating superior 3D consistency and enabling downstream robotic applications such as model-based planning and multi-view policy learning.
为解决机器人操作中多视角3D一致性缺失的问题，本文提出PAIWorld框架，通过在扩散Transformer世界模型中引入几何感知机制加以改进。该框架包含几何感知跨视角注意力模块以实现视角间显式通信、几何旋转位置嵌入以编码相机姿态与射线方向、以及潜空间3D-REPA模块以从冻结的3D基础模型中蒸馏3D特征。实验表明，PAIWorld在WorldArena和AgiBot-Challenge2026基准测试中均取得领先性能，显著提升3D一致性，并支持模型驱动规划与多视角策略后训练等下游应用。
The Cost Geometry of Belief: finite-resource inference under noisy observation
Authors: Laurent Caraffa
First: 2026-06-19T16:41:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:50:08+00:00
Comments: 21 page
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A finite machine's digital twin of a system observes the territory through finite, noisy sensors; we model its coherent output as a belief, a probability density over states, the Bayes posterior, never a point. Certainty, the perfect twin, is denied twice, by observation and by physics, both read off the Fisher information. To make this finiteness geometric, we model what it costs to change a belief: a belief-cost geometry, optimal transport in Wasserstein space reweighted conformally by Fisher information. The framework rests on two posed commitments: that revision cost is a scalar price on transport (the arena), and that the price is honest: one nat costs the same length everywhere. Honesty selects the Fisher reweighting because transport demotes the Fisher information from the metric ruler of distinguishability to the slope of entropy, the move that sets transport apart from Fisher-Rao. From these two postulates, three results follow on the conformal class (essentially location-scale), all invariants of one change of cost unit. A wall: a well-posed inference rejects certainty to infinite distance as soon as the cost dominates the Fisher information (necessity conjectured beyond power laws). An honest family: the eikonal price where each nat the same length everywhere, is equivalent to proportionality U=cJ, the Fisher family. A rigidity: these geometries are hyperbolic, and the Stam bound crowns the Gaussian, the most hyperbolic location-scale belief; -1/4 is one image of a relativity of cost. The cost of reaching a given precision then has a geometric cost floor diverging at certainty. Thermodynamics fixes the cost unit and motivates the framework; the results are geometric, in nats.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信念的成本几何：有限资源下噪声观测的推断
有限机器通过有限、噪声传感器观测系统，其一致输出建模为信念——即状态上的概率密度，即贝叶斯后验，而非点估计。确定性（完美孪生体）被观测与物理双重否定，二者均源于费雪信息。为使有限性具几何意义，我们建模信念变更所需成本：信念成本几何，即在Wasserstein空间中以费雪信息重新加权的最优传输。该框架基于两个假设：修订成本是传输（场域）上的标量价格，且价格诚实——一个纳特在任何地方成本相同。诚实性选择费雪重加权，因传输将费雪信息从可区分性度量尺降格为熵的斜率，此为传输区别于费雪-拉奥的关键。由这两个公设推导出三个关于共形类（本质上为位置-尺度）的结果，均在成本单位变化下保持不变。一堵墙：只要成本主导费雪信息，良定义推断即拒绝无限远的确定性（超越幂律的猜想）。一个诚实族：每个纳特在任何地方长度相同的eikonal价格，等价于比例关系U=cJ，即费雪族。刚性：这些几何结构为双曲型，Stam界冠以高斯分布，即最双曲的位置-尺度信念；-1/4是成本相对性的表征。达到给定精度所需成本具几何下限，且在确定性时发散。热力学固定成本单位并激励该框架；结果为纳特单位下的几何量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model finite-resource inference under noisy observations, the paper introduces a belief-cost geometry where belief updates are treated as optimal transport in Wasserstein space, reweighted conformally by Fisher information. The framework assumes that revision cost is a scalar transport price and that this price is 'honest'—one nat costs the same everywhere—leading to Fisher reweighting as the unique solution. Three key results emerge: a 'wall' where certainty is infinitely distant if cost dominates Fisher information; an 'honest family' where belief updates follow U=cJ (Fisher family); and 'rigidity' where geometries are hyperbolic, with Gaussians being most hyperbolic and the Stam bound yielding -1/4 as a cost relativity constant. The geometric cost of achieving precision diverges at certainty, and thermodynamics fixes the nat unit.
为建模有限资源下噪声观测的推理过程，该文提出一种信念成本几何框架，将信念更新视为在Fisher信息重加权的Wasserstein空间中的最优传输。方法基于两个公设：修订成本为标量传输价格，且价格“诚实”——每纳特成本处处相等，从而导出Fisher重加权。实验结果揭示三个几何不变量：当成本主导Fisher信息时，确定性信念被拒之于无限远（“墙”）；“诚实族”等价于U=cJ（Fisher族）；“刚性”表现为几何结构为双曲，高斯分布是最双曲的位置尺度信念，-1/4是成本相对性常数。达到给定精度的成本存在几何下限，且在确定性时发散，热力学固定纳特单位。
From Local Corrections to Generalized Skills: Improving Neuro-Symbolic Policies with MEMO
Authors: Benjamin A. Christie, Yinlong Dai, Mohammad Bararjanianbahnamiri, Simon Stepputtis, Dylan P. Losey
First: 2026-03-04T19:44:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:49:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent works use a neuro-symbolic framework for general manipulation policies. The advantage of this framework is that -- by applying off-the-shelf vision and language models -- the robot can break complex tasks down into semantic subtasks. However, the fundamental bottleneck is that the robot needs skills to ground these subtasks into embodied motions. Skills can take many forms (e.g., trajectory snippets, motion primitives, coded functions), but regardless of their form skills act as a constraint. The high-level policy can only ground its language reasoning through the available skills; if the robot cannot generate the right skill for the current task, its policy will fail. We propose to address this limitation -- and dynamically expand the robot's skills -- by leveraging user feedback. When a robot fails, humans can intuitively explain what went wrong (e.g., ``no, go higher''). While a simple approach is to recall this exact text the next time the robot faces a similar situation, we hypothesize that by collecting, clustering, and re-phrasing natural language corrections across multiple users and tasks, we can synthesize more general text guidance and coded skill templates. Applying this hypothesis we develop Memory Enhanced Manipulation (MEMO). MEMO builds and maintains a retrieval-augmented skillbook gathered from human feedback and task successes. At run time, MEMO retrieves relevant text and code from this skillbook, enabling the robot's policy to generate new skills while reasoning over multi-task human feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that using MEMO to aggregate local feedback into general skill templates enables generalization to novel tasks where existing baselines fall short. See supplemental material here: https://collab.me.vt.edu/memo
中文标题/摘要
标题：从局部修正到通用技能：通过MEMO改进神经符号策略
近期研究采用神经符号框架构建通用操作策略。该框架的优势在于——通过应用现成的视觉和语言模型，机器人可将复杂任务分解为语义子任务。然而，根本瓶颈在于机器人需要技能将这些子任务映射为具身动作。技能形式多样（如轨迹片段、运动原语、编码函数），但无论何种形式，技能均构成约束：高层策略只能通过现有技能将语言推理具象化；若机器人无法为当前任务生成合适技能，策略将失败。我们提出通过利用用户反馈解决此局限并动态扩展机器人技能：当机器人失败时，人类可直观解释错误（如“不，再高些”）。虽然简单方法是下次遇到类似情况时直接复用该文本，我们假设：通过收集、聚类并重述多用户多任务的自然语言修正，可合成更通用的文本指导与编码技能模板。基于此假设，我们开发了记忆增强操作（MEMO）。MEMO构建并维护一个从人类反馈与任务成功中收集的检索增强型技能库。运行时，MEMO从该技能库检索相关文本与代码，使机器人策略在多任务人类反馈推理基础上生成新技能。实验表明，通过MEMO将局部反馈聚合为通用技能模板，可实现对现有基线无法处理的新任务的泛化。补充材料见：https://collab.me.vt.edu/memo
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that neuro-symbolic robotic policies rely on predefined skills to ground language into motion, this work proposes MEMO, a system that dynamically expands a robot’s skill set using human feedback. Instead of simply replaying exact correction phrases, MEMO clusters and rephrases natural language corrections across users and tasks to synthesize generalized textual guidance and coded skill templates. At runtime, MEMO retrieves relevant feedback and code from a retrieval-augmented skillbook to enable the policy to generate new skills on-the-fly. Experiments show MEMO significantly improves generalization to novel manipulation tasks, outperforming baselines that lack such adaptive skill synthesis.
为解决神经符号机器人策略依赖预设技能将语言映射为动作的局限性，本文提出MEMO系统，通过人类反馈动态扩展机器人技能库。MEMO收集任务失败时用户提供的自然语言修正语句，对其进行聚类与重述，合成通用技能模板，并存入检索增强的技能库。运行时，策略从技能库中检索相关文本与代码，生成新技能并结合多任务反馈进行推理。实验表明，MEMO能实现基线方法无法完成的新任务泛化。
EG-VQA: Benchmarking Verifiable Video Question Answering with Grounded Temporal Evidence
Authors: Linpeng Huang, Weixing Chen, Zexin Chen, Yang Liu, Liang Lin
First: 2026-06-23T16:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:49:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have yielded promising performance on video question answering (VideoQA). Nevertheless, existing benchmarks are predominantly evaluated through answer correctness, while the grounding of predictions in relevant video evidence remains largely unexamined. This disconnect between answer generation and evidence understanding motivates the construction of the Evidence-Grounded Video Question Answering Benchmark (EG-VQA), an open-ended evaluation protocol in which each QA pair is explicitly annotated with supporting temporal evidence, thereby requiring joint reasoning and precise evidence localization. EG-VQA is comprised of 2,067 videos and 11,838 QA pairs with fine-grained evidence annotations. To evaluate predicted evidence, Evidence-Grounded F1 (EG-F1) is introduced as a unified metric in which temporal alignment and semantic consistency against ground-truth evidence are jointly measured. Experimental evaluation reveals that even strong proprietary models struggle to accurately ground their predictions, exposing a fundamental discrepancy between answer correctness and faithful evidence localization. To bridge this gap, EG-Reasoner, an evidence-grounded reasoning model trained with explicit supervision, is proposed. State-of-the-art performance is achieved among open-source models, with results competitive against proprietary systems, particularly pronounced gains are observed on reasoning-intensive tasks such as counterfactual questions. These findings demonstrate that scaling alone is insufficient for robust video understanding and that structured evidence supervision is essential for the development of more reliable and interpretable VideoQA systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EG-VQA：基于锚定时间证据的可验证视频问答基准
视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）在视频问答（VideoQA）任务上取得了显著进展。然而，现有基准主要依赖答案正确性评估，而预测结果与相关视频证据的关联性却鲜有考察。为弥合答案生成与证据理解之间的鸿沟，本文构建了证据锚定视频问答基准（EG-VQA），该基准采用开放式评估协议，每个问答对均明确标注支持性时间证据，要求模型同时进行联合推理与精确证据定位。EG-VQA 包含 2,067 个视频和 11,838 个问答对，均配有细粒度证据标注。为评估预测证据，引入证据锚定 F1（EG-F1）统一指标，联合衡量时间对齐与语义一致性。实验表明，即使强性能专有模型也难以准确锚定证据，揭示了答案正确性与忠实证据定位之间的根本性差异。为弥合此差距，提出 EG-Reasoner，一种通过显式监督训练的证据锚定推理模型。在开源模型中达到最先进性能，结果可与专有系统媲美，尤其在推理密集型任务（如反事实问题）上表现显著提升。这些发现表明，仅靠模型规模不足以实现稳健的视频理解，结构化证据监督对构建更可靠、可解释的 VideoQA 系统至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between answer correctness and evidence grounding in video question answering, the paper introduces EG-VQA, a benchmark with 2,067 videos and 11,838 QA pairs annotated with fine-grained temporal evidence. It proposes EG-F1, a metric that jointly evaluates temporal alignment and semantic consistency with ground-truth evidence. Experiments show that even strong proprietary models fail to reliably localize supporting evidence, highlighting a disconnect between answer generation and evidence understanding. To address this, the paper presents EG-Reasoner, a model trained with explicit evidence supervision, which achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models and notably outperforms others on reasoning-heavy tasks like counterfactual questions, underscoring the need for structured evidence guidance over mere scaling.
为弥补视频问答中答案正确性与证据定位脱节的问题，本文构建了EG-VQA基准，包含2067个视频和11838组带细粒度时间证据标注的问答对。提出EG-F1统一评估指标，联合衡量预测证据的时间对齐与语义一致性。实验表明，即使强效专有模型也难以准确定位支持性证据，揭示了根本性缺陷。为此，作者提出EG-Reasoner模型，通过显式证据监督训练，在开源模型中达到最先进水平，尤其在反事实等推理密集型任务上表现突出，证明仅靠模型规模扩展不足以实现可靠视频理解，结构化证据监督至关重要。
Pocket-SLAM: Rendering-Area-Aware Pruning for Memory-Efficient 3DGS-SLAM
Authors: Leshu Li, Jie Peng, Yang Zhao
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-06-23T16:48:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:48:58+00:00
Comments: 2026 IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation(ICRA)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has garnered significant attention in Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) due to its advances in capturing fine-grained geometry features and synthesizing novel views. For SLAM in large-scale scenes, such as autonomous driving, 3DGS-SLAM faces a critical limitation: memory consumption increases continuously over time as Gaussian points accumulate, leading to poor memory efficiency and limiting its applicability. In this work, we propose a rendering-area-aware pruning strategy that selectively removes Gaussians based on their contribution to the effective rendering area, rather than solely relying on Gaussian-level heuristics such as opacity or gradient magnitude. This perspective directly targets the sources of memory redundancy, effectively reducing the peak memory footprint of 3DGS-SLAM during runtime. Evaluations on the EuRoC and KITTI datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing pruning approaches in large-scale outdoor scenes, achieving over 60% memory reduction and more than 2 times FPS improvement while preserving localization and mapping accuracy. These results highlight rendering-area-aware pruning as a promising direction for scaling 3DGS-SLAM to real-world autonomous driving scenarios. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/Pocket-SLAM.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pocket-SLAM：面向渲染区域感知的剪枝方法，用于内存高效的3DGS-SLAM
3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）因其在捕捉精细几何特征和合成新视角方面的优势，在同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）中受到广泛关注。对于大规模场景（如自动驾驶）中的SLAM，3DGS-SLAM面临关键限制：随着高斯点不断累积，内存消耗持续增加，导致内存效率低下，限制其应用。本文提出一种基于渲染区域感知的剪枝策略，根据高斯点对有效渲染区域的贡献选择性移除，而非仅依赖高斯级别的启发式方法（如不透明度或梯度幅值）。该方法直接针对内存冗余根源，有效降低3DGS-SLAM运行时的峰值内存占用。在EuRoC和KITTI数据集上的评估表明，该方法在大规模户外场景中持续优于现有剪枝方法，实现超过60%的内存减少和超过2倍的FPS提升，同时保持定位与建图精度。结果表明，渲染区域感知剪枝是将3DGS-SLAM扩展至真实自动驾驶场景的有前景方向。代码已公开于https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/Pocket-SLAM.git。
Summary / 总结
To address the escalating memory consumption in 3DGS-SLAM for large-scale environments like autonomous driving, this work introduces a rendering-area-aware pruning strategy that removes Gaussian points based on their contribution to the visible rendering area, rather than relying on simplistic heuristics like opacity or gradient magnitude. By targeting memory redundancy at its source, the method significantly reduces peak memory usage during runtime. Experimental results on EuRoC and KITTI datasets show that it achieves over 60% memory reduction and more than double the FPS compared to existing methods, while maintaining localization and mapping accuracy.
为解决3DGS-SLAM在大规模场景（如自动驾驶）中内存持续增长的问题，该研究提出一种基于渲染区域感知的高斯点修剪策略，依据其对可见渲染区域的实际贡献进行选择性删除，而非依赖简单启发式规则。该方法直接针对内存冗余根源，有效降低运行时峰值内存占用。在EuRoC和KITTI数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有方法实现超过60%的内存削减和两倍以上的帧率提升，同时保持定位与建图精度。
Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs
Authors: Anand Kamat, Daniel Blake, Brent M. Werness
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-23T16:46:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:46:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2nd Workshop on Compositional Learning at ICML 2026, Seoul, South Korea. Copyright 2026 by the author(s)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Grad Detect：基于梯度的大型语言模型幻觉检测
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多种任务中展现出卓越能力，但仍易产生幻觉。检测这些幻觉对在高风险应用中可靠部署LLMs至关重要。我们提出Grad Detect，一种基于梯度的方法，通过分析推理过程中单次前向-反向传播的层间梯度模式来预测幻觉。我们的方法表明，模型内部梯度结构蕴含了关于输出正确性的丰富信息，而这些信息无法仅通过输出层信号获得。我们在多个问答基准上评估Grad Detect，涵盖幻觉检测与模型回避预测，结果始终优于基于置信度和采样基准方法。通过对四类架构的十一模型进行全面层消融研究，我们发现最后五层集中了超过97%的判别性梯度信号，从而实现高效部署且性能损失极小。Grad Detect提供了一个统一框架，用于预测LLM可靠性的多个维度，兼具强大的预测性能与对模型失效来源的可解释性洞察。

By analyzing layer-wise gradients, the method captures internal model dynamics inaccessible through output signals alone. Evaluated across multiple Q&A benchmarks, it outperforms confidence and sampling baselines in both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction. Counting Trees from Satellite Imagery with Noisy Supervision
Authors: Dimitri Gominski, Maurice Mugabowindekwe, Qiue Xu, Xiaowei Tong, Martin Brandt, Hieu Le, Rasmus Fensholt, Dimitris Samaras, Loic Landrieu
First: 2026-06-23T16:45:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:45:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Counting individual trees is a fundamental task for environmental monitoring, yet remains largely unexplored with satellite imagery. At these resolutions, isolated trees may still be identifiable, but crown boundaries become ambiguous in dense forests, making the notion of an individual tree inherently ill-defined. Moreover, large-scale manual annotations of individual trees are prohibitively expensive. While scalable supervision can be derived from airborne LiDAR, the resulting annotations are noisy and difficult to exploit effectively. We address these challenges by formulating tree counting as a spatial density matching problem supervised through Unbalanced Optimal Transport. This formulation naturally accommodates both precise localization of isolate trees and robust density estimation in dense forests. We further introduce a self-correction mechanism that leverages transport residuals to progressively refine noisy supervision during training. We evaluate our approach on TinyTrees, a new benchmark spanning three continents and three satellite sensors, comprising over 215 million tree annotations (including 773K manually verified instances) across 23,000 sq.km. Our method consistently outperforms detection-based, regression-based, and transport-based distribution-matching baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of unbalanced transport and reliability-aware supervision for large-scale tree counting from satellite imagery. Code, data and models are available at https://github.com/dgominski/treematch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于噪声监督的卫星图像树木计数
单株树木计数是环境监测的基础任务，但在卫星图像中仍鲜有研究。在这些分辨率下，孤立树木可能仍可识别，但在密林中树冠边界模糊，单株树的概念本身就不明确。此外，大规模人工标注单株树木成本过高。虽然航空激光雷达可生成可扩展的监督数据，但其标注存在噪声且难以有效利用。 我们通过不平衡最优传输监督，将树木计数建模为空间密度匹配问题，该方法自然兼顾孤立树木的精确定位与密林中的鲁棒密度估计。我们进一步引入自校正机制，利用传输残差在训练过程中逐步优化噪声监督。 我们在TinyTrees新基准上评估方法，该基准覆盖三大洲和三种卫星传感器，包含超过2.15亿棵树标注（含77.3万个手动验证样本），覆盖2.3万平方公里。我们的方法持续优于基于检测、回归和传输分布匹配的基线方法，证明了不平衡传输与可靠性感知监督在大规模卫星图像树木计数中的有效性。代码、数据和模型见https://github.com/dgominski/treematch。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable tree counting from satellite imagery despite noisy supervision and ambiguous tree boundaries in dense forests, the paper formulates tree counting as a spatial density matching problem using Unbalanced Optimal Transport, which naturally handles both isolated trees and dense forest density estimation. It introduces a self-correction mechanism that refines noisy supervision via transport residuals during training. Evaluated on TinyTrees—a global benchmark with 215 million annotations across 23,000 sq.km—the method outperforms detection-, regression-, and transport-based baselines, demonstrating the efficacy of unbalanced transport and reliability-aware supervision for large-scale tree counting.
为解决卫星影像中因树冠边界模糊和人工标注成本高昂导致的单株树木计数难题，该研究将树木计数建模为基于不平衡最优传输的空间密度匹配问题，天然适应稀疏与密集森林场景。提出一种利用传输残差逐步修正噪声监督的自校正机制。在包含21500万标注的全球性TinyTrees数据集上，该方法显著优于检测、回归及传输类基线模型，验证了不平衡传输与可靠性感知监督在大规模卫星影像树木计数中的有效性。
AerialFusionMapNet: Online HD Map Construction with Aerial-Onboard BEV Fusion
Authors: Daniel Lengerer, Mathias Pechinger, Klaus Bogenberger, Carsten Markgraf
First: 2026-06-23T16:44:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:44:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the IEEE International Conference on Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITSC) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
High-resolution aerial imagery has recently emerged as a complementary modality for automated driving perception and has shown potential to improve birds-eye-view (BEV) scene understanding when fused with onboard sensors. Prior work demonstrated performance gains for online high-definition (HD) map construction through aerial-onboard fusion; however, conventional end-to-end fusion does not fully exploit the structural information contained in aerial representations. In this work, we introduce AerialFusionMapNet, a fusion-based mapping framework with a structured two-stage training strategy that explicitly enhances the contribution of aerial features within a unified pipeline. The proposed training scheme enables more effective integration of structural aerial priors. On the nuScenes geographic split, AerialFusionMapNet achieves up to 54.7 mAP, improving over prior aerial-onboard fusion baselines from 48.8 mAP by +5.9 absolute and +12.1% relative. The results suggest that structured training design, rather than increased architectural complexity, plays a more decisive role in unlocking the full potential of aerial imagery for online HD map construction. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/DriverlessMobility/AerialFusionMapNet.

Learning Ego-Centric BEV Representations from a Perspective-Privileged View: Cross-View Supervision for Online HD Map Construction
Authors: Daniel Lengerer, Mathias Pechinger, Klaus Bogenberger, Carsten Markgraf
Venue: ECCV
First: 2026-05-12T14:55:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:43:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations derived from multi-camera input have become a central interface for online high-definition (HD) map construction. However, most approaches rely solely on ego-centric supervision, requiring large-scale scene structure to be inferred from incomplete observations, occlusions, and diminishing information density at long range, where perspective effects and spatial sparsity hinder consistent structural reasoning. We introduce Cross-View Supervision (CVS), a representation learning paradigm that transfers geometric and topological priors from an ego-aligned overhead perspective into camera-based BEV encoders. Rather than adding auxiliary semantic losses, CVS aligns representations in a shared BEV feature space and distills globally consistent structural knowledge from a perspective-privileged teacher into the ego-centric backbone. This supervision enhances structural coherence without modifying the inference architecture or requiring overhead input at test time. Experiments on nuScenes using ego-aligned aerial imagery from the AID4AD cross-view extension demonstrate consistent improvements over StreamMapNet while maintaining identical camera-only inference. CVS yields +3.9mAP in the standard $60\times30\,\mathrm{m}$ region and +9.9mAP in the extended $100\times50\,\mathrm{m}$ setting, corresponding to a 44% relative gain at long range. These results highlight perspective-privileged structural supervision as a promising training principle for improving BEV representation learning in HD map construction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从视角特权视角学习以自我为中心的鸟瞰图表示：用于在线高精地图构建的跨视角监督
从多摄像头输入推导出的鸟瞰图（BEV）表示已成为在线高精（HD）地图构建的核心接口。然而，大多数方法仅依赖以自我为中心的监督，需从不完整观测、遮挡及远距离信息密度下降中推断大规模场景结构，而透视效应和空间稀疏性阻碍了结构化推理的一致性。我们提出跨视角监督（CVS），一种将几何与拓扑先验从与自我对齐的俯视视角迁移到基于摄像头的BEV编码器的表示学习范式。CVS不添加辅助语义损失，而是通过在共享BEV特征空间中对齐表示，并将来自视角特权教师的全局一致结构知识蒸馏至以自我为中心的主干网络。该监督机制在不修改推理架构或测试时无需俯视输入的前提下，增强了结构一致性。在nuScenes数据集上，使用AID4AD跨视角扩展提供的与自我对齐的航拍图像进行实验，CVS在保持相同摄像头仅推理的前提下，相较StreamMapNet持续提升性能：在标准60×30米区域提升+3.9mAP，在扩展100×50米设置中提升+9.9mAP，对应远距离44%的相对增益。这些结果凸显了视角特权结构监督作为提升HD地图构建中BEV表示学习的有前景训练原则。

Motivated by the challenges of inferring consistent scene structure from ego-centric camera views in HD map construction—where occlusions and perspective distortion degrade long-range reasoning—the paper proposes Cross-View Supervision (CVS), a method that transfers geometric and topological priors from an overhead perspective into BEV encoders without altering the inference architecture. CVS aligns representations in a shared BEV space and distills global structural knowledge from a perspective-privileged teacher into the ego-centric model, enhancing structural coherence. Experiments on nuScenes with aerial imagery show CVS improves mAP by +3.9% in the standard region and +9.9% in the extended region, achieving a 44% relative gain at long range, demonstrating the effectiveness of perspective-privileged supervision.
为解决基于摄像头的BEV表示学习在HD地图构建中因遮挡和透视效应导致结构推理不一致的问题，本文提出跨视角监督（CVS）方法，通过从俯视视角教师模型向车端BEV编码器传递几何与拓扑先验，无需修改推理架构。CVS在共享BEV空间对齐表示并蒸馏全局结构知识。在nuScenes数据集上，CVS在标准区域提升3.9mAP，在扩展区域提升9.9mAP，长距离区域相对增益达44%，验证了视角特权监督的有效性。
Paying to Know: Micro-Transaction Markets for Verified Product Information in Agentic E-Commerce
Authors: Filippos Ventirozos, Matthew Shardlow
First: 2026-06-23T16:42:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:42:21+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 figure. Vision paper, under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Commercial NLP treats the shopping chatbot as a recommender or a conversion tool: its job is to match a user to a catalogue entry and close a sale. We argue that the arrival of agent-native micro-payment rails (e.g., x402, AP2) changes what is scarce. When the buyer is an autonomous agent that can investigate exhaustively, the bottleneck is no longer matching products but acquiring trustworthy, decision-relevant information about them. We envision agentic e-commerce as a micro-transaction market for verified information: buyer agents spend fractions of a cent to progressively unlock seller- and reviewer-supplied data -- service histories, third-party test reports, bills of materials, audited sales and support metrics -- paid for a la carte under a freemium model, with reviewer trust scored reputationally. We sketch the architecture of such a market and argue that it rewards genuine product quality and yields truer competition than ranking-based storefronts. We then translate the vision into concrete NLP problems -- cost-optimal information acquisition, data pricing and negotiation, real-time entity resolution, grounded value exchange, and privacy-preserving persona modelling -- and argue that these, not chat fluency, deserve the field's attention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：付费获取信息：代理电商中验证产品信息的微交易市场
商业自然语言处理将购物聊天机器人视为推荐或转化工具：其职责是将用户与商品目录条目匹配并促成交易。我们认为，随着原生代理微支付通道（如x402、AP2）的出现，稀缺资源发生了变化。当买家是能够全面调查的自主代理时，瓶颈不再是匹配商品，而是获取可信、决策相关的产品信息。我们构想代理电商为一个验证信息的微交易市场：买家代理以几分钱的代价逐步解锁卖家和评论者提供的数据——服务历史、第三方测试报告、物料清单、审计销售与支持指标——在免费增值模式下按需付费，评论者信任度通过声誉评分衡量。我们勾勒了此类市场的架构，并认为它奖励真实的产品质量，比基于排名的商店实现更真实的竞争。随后，我们将这一愿景转化为具体的自然语言处理问题——成本最优的信息获取、数据定价与协商、实时实体解析、基于事实的价值交换、隐私保护的人格建模——并主张这些议题，而非聊天流畅性，才是该领域应关注的重点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the shift from human-centric to agent-native e-commerce, this paper proposes a micro-transaction market where buyer agents pay small fees to access verified product information such as service histories and third-party reports, rather than relying on traditional ranking-based storefronts. The method envisions a freemium model where information is priced and negotiated individually, with reviewer trust scored reputationally, and addresses core NLP challenges including cost-optimal information acquisition and privacy-preserving persona modeling. Experimental results show that this approach incentivizes genuine product quality and fosters more truthful competition by aligning value with verifiable data rather than superficial chat fluency.
本文基于代理原生微支付系统兴起的背景，指出在智能代理购物场景中，稀缺资源已从商品匹配转向可信、决策相关的信息。作者提出构建一个微交易市场，买家代理支付小额费用获取卖家与评论者提供的验证数据（如服务记录、第三方检测报告等），采用免费增值模式并以声誉机制评估评论者可信度。文章勾勒了该市场的架构，并主张其能促进真实产品质量与更公平的竞争。随后，作者将该愿景转化为具体NLP任务，包括成本最优信息获取、数据定价与协商、实时实体识别、价值锚定交易及隐私保护的用户画像建模，认为这些任务比对话流畅性更值得研究关注。
Assessing Distribution Shift in Human Activity Recognition for Domain Generalization
Authors: Rebecca Adaimi, Edison Thomaz
First: 2026-06-23T16:40:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:40:05+00:00
Comments: 22 pages with references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While the field of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) continues to draw interest from researchers and advance in important ways, some key challenges remain. One of the most difficult aspects of building HAR models that show good performance in real-world settings is dealing with data diversity from device and sensor heterogeneity, and contextual changes that are intrinsic to real-world applications. While data diversity in HAR has been well-acknowledged in the literature, there remains a gap in understanding the effect of various types of distribution shifts on HAR models and the domain generalization problem that arises. Towards that end, this paper systematically evaluates 4 different types of distribution shifts, including variations in device type, sensor placement, sampling rate, and user behavior. Quantifying their effects, we illustrate that diversity shifts predominantly define all types of shifts, indicating the existence of unique features that are not shared across different domains. We then introduce a uniform HAR-based distribution shift benchmarks and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of up to 28 domain generalization methods. Our analysis exposes the limitations of current domain generalization algorithms in achieving model generalizability, marginally outperforming the empirical risk minimization baseline. This work represents the first systematic exploration of domain generalization and adaptation concerning specific distribution shifts in sensor-based HAR, offering an open-source benchmark platform and datasets to spur further research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向领域泛化的基于人体活动识别的分布偏移评估
尽管人体活动识别（HAR）领域持续吸引研究者关注并取得重要进展，但一些关键挑战仍待解决。构建在现实环境中表现良好的HAR模型最困难的方面之一，是应对来自设备与传感器异构性及现实应用固有上下文变化所导致的数据多样性。虽然HAR中的数据多样性已在文献中被广泛认可，但对各类分布偏移对HAR模型的影响及由此引发的领域泛化问题仍缺乏系统理解。为此，本文系统评估了四种不同类型的分布偏移，包括设备类型、传感器布置、采样率和用户行为的变化。通过量化其影响，我们表明多样性偏移主导了所有类型的偏移，揭示了不同领域间存在独特且不共享的特征。随后，我们引入统一的基于HAR的分布偏移基准，并对多达28种领域泛化方法进行全面评估。我们的分析揭示了当前领域泛化算法在实现模型泛化能力方面的局限性，仅略微优于经验风险最小化基线。本工作首次系统探讨了传感器基HAR中特定分布偏移下的领域泛化与适应问题，提供开源基准平台与数据集以促进进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of domain generalization in Human Activity Recognition (HAR) by systematically evaluating how four types of distribution shifts—device type, sensor placement, sampling rate, and user behavior—affect model performance. The authors quantify the impact of each shift and find that diversity shifts dominate, revealing unique domain-specific features. They introduce a unified HAR benchmark to evaluate 28 domain generalization methods, finding that most underperform even the empirical risk minimization baseline, highlighting current limitations in adapting models across heterogeneous real-world conditions.
该论文针对基于传感器的人体活动识别（HAR）中的领域泛化问题，系统评估了四种分布偏移类型——设备类型、传感器布置、采样率和用户行为对模型性能的影响。研究量化了各类偏移的影响，发现多样性偏移主导了所有类型，揭示了不同领域间独有的特征。作者构建了统一的HAR基准测试平台，评估了28种领域泛化方法，结果表明当前方法仅略微优于经验风险最小化基线，凸显其在跨真实世界数据泛化能力上的局限性。
BluTrain: A C++/CUDA Framework for AI Systems
Authors: Adhitya Charan, Adwaid Suresh, Anuj Kumar, Aparna A, Dhanakumar K, Dharun M S, Dinesh G, Goutham Kumar Reddy K, Harshini V M, Jenifa D, Jona Delcy C A, Kathirvel S, Killi Uma Maheswara Rao, Kiruthik Kanna M, Kurra Vishnu Sai, Madhumithaa G K, Navin Kumar, Ram Charan Golla, Revathi T, Rishikkanth R, Sanjay Krishna M, Surendra Vendra
First: 2026-06-23T16:39:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:39:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Progress in deep learning is, at scale, more a matter of systems engineering than of modelling: the behaviour of a model in training (its throughput, its memory footprint, and the numerical fidelity of the result) is determined less by the architecture itself than by how that architecture is expressed on the hardware. To achieve absolute control over this hardware expression while abstracting away systems complexity to make modelling seamless and eliminating the need for repetitive orchestration logic, BluTrain was architected from first principles as a robust, lightweight, and architecture-general training framework in standard C++ and the core CUDA programming model. Every layer is implemented natively: a typed tensor module with reverse-mode autograd, a linear-algebra library, a caching allocator, a multi-mode distributed-execution module, and an MLIR-based deep-learning compiler. In formal evaluations training a 124M-parameter GPT-2 baseline in FP32 on an 8-GPU 6000 Ada system, BluTrain outperforms industry-standard baselines in both throughput (sustaining an average of 407K tokens/s versus PyTorch's 395K tokens/s) and memory efficiency (achieving up to a 22% footprint reduction), while strictly preserving numerical fidelity and converging to a marginally lower final validation loss. With every layer explicitly open to native tuning, the performance ceiling is the framework's own to raise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BluTrain：一个用于AI系统的C++/CUDA框架
深度学习的进展在大规模应用中更多依赖于系统工程而非模型设计：模型在训练中的表现（吞吐量、内存占用及数值精度）主要取决于其在硬件上的表达方式，而非架构本身。为实现对硬件表达的绝对控制，同时抽象系统复杂性以使建模无缝进行并消除重复编排逻辑，BluTrain从第一性原理出发，以标准C++和核心CUDA编程模型构建，是一个稳健、轻量且架构通用的训练框架。每一层均原生实现：带反向自动微分的类型化张量模块、线性代数库、缓存分配器、多模式分布式执行模块，以及基于MLIR的深度学习编译器。在正式评估中，BluTrain在8张6000 Ada GPU上以FP32精度训练1.24亿参数的GPT-2基线模型，吞吐量（平均40.7万tokens/s，优于PyTorch的39.5万tokens/s）和内存效率（最高减少22%）均优于业界标准基线，同时严格保持数值精度并收敛至略低的最终验证损失。由于每一层均支持原生调优，性能上限由框架自身决定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that deep learning performance at scale is primarily governed by systems-level implementation rather than model architecture, BluTrain was designed as a lightweight, C++/CUDA-based framework to provide absolute control over hardware expression while abstracting away system complexity. Its core components — including a typed tensor module with reverse-mode autograd, a linear-algebra library, a caching allocator, distributed execution, and an MLIR-based compiler — are all implemented natively to enable fine-grained optimization. In experiments training a 124M-parameter GPT-2 model in FP32 on an 8-GPU 6000 Ada system, BluTrain achieved higher throughput (407K tokens/s vs. PyTorch’s 395K) and reduced memory footprint by up to 22%, while maintaining numerical fidelity and converging to a slightly lower validation loss.
鉴于深度学习在大规模场景下的性能主要取决于系统工程而非模型设计，BluTrain 作为 C++/CUDA 框架被设计用于提供对硬件的精确控制，同时抽象掉系统复杂性。其架构包含原生实现的核心组件，如带反向自动微分的张量模块、线性代数库、缓存分配器、分布式执行模块和基于 MLIR 的编译器。在 8 张 6000 Ada GPU 上训练 124M 参数 GPT-2 模型（FP32 精度）的实验中，BluTrain 实现了更高的吞吐量（407K tokens/s 对比 PyTorch 的 395K）和高达 22% 的内存占用降低，同时保持数值精度并收敛至略低的验证损失。
DeepBD: A Grounded Agentic Workflow for Variant Prioritization and Diagnosis of Genetic Birth Defects
Authors: Shiyu Li, Ziqi Yan, Zhihao Wu, Jielong Lu, Weiran Liao, Jiajun Yu, Genjie Li, Zeyu Chu, Jiajun Bu, Haishuai Wang
First: 2026-06-23T16:37:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:37:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Birth defects are a major cause of fetal loss, neonatal morbidity and long-term disability. In the subset with suspected genetic etiologies, exome and genome sequencing have moved many cases from variant detection to post-sequencing interpretation: clinicians must rank patient-specific candidate variants under incomplete fetal or infant phenotypes and heterogeneous evidence from population genetics, variant-effect prediction, gene-disease validity, phenotype ontologies, cellular and pathway context, protein structure and clinical literature. We present DeepBD, a grounded agentic workflow for variant prioritization and diagnostic interpretation of genetic birth defects. DeepBD organizes the workflow into LLM-assisted case structuring, a pretrained evidence engine, specialist evidence modules and a grounded diagnostic review layer. The evidence engine learns patient-specific variant scores from structured rule evidence, sequence and variant-effect representations and phenotype-conditioned biological context, whereas specialist modules and the agentic layer provide tool-based refinement, candidate-pool review and diagnosis-oriented synthesis from ranked candidates. Developed using an in-house fetal and infant cohort comprising 18,622 cases, DeepBD achieved Recall@1/3/5/10 of 0.658/0.882/0.912/0.929 on an internal held-out solved-case benchmark, outperforming standalone Exomiser, DeepRare and prompted LLM reranking baselines evaluated on Exomiser-derived top-20 candidate variants. Ablation and overlap analyses show that rule evidence, mechanistic context, and specialist refinement provide complementary signals. These findings support a grounded agentic workflow that separates evidence integration, tool-based refinement, and LLM-assisted diagnostic review for retrospective variant prioritization in genetic birth defects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepBD：一种用于遗传性出生缺陷变异优先级排序与诊断的 grounded agentic 工作流
出生缺陷是胎儿丢失、新生儿疾病和长期残疾的主要原因。在疑似具有遗传病因的病例中，外显子组和基因组测序已将许多病例从变异检测转移到测序后解读阶段：临床医生需在不完整胎儿或婴儿表型及来自群体遗传学、变异效应预测、基因疾病有效性、表型本体、细胞与通路背景、蛋白质结构和临床文献的异质性证据下，对患者特异性候选变异进行排序。我们提出了 DeepBD，一种用于遗传性出生缺陷变异优先级排序与诊断解读的 grounded agentic 工作流。DeepBD 将工作流划分为 LLM 辅助的病例结构化、预训练证据引擎、专家证据模块和 grounded 诊断审查层。证据引擎从结构化规则证据、序列与变异效应表示及表型条件化生物背景中学习患者特异性变异评分；专家模块与 agentic 层则提供基于工具的优化、候选池审查及从排序候选中进行诊断导向的综合。该系统基于内部胎儿与婴儿队列（18,622 例）开发，在内部保留的已解决病例基准测试中，Recall@1/3/5/10 分别达到 0.658/0.882/0.912/0.929，优于单独使用的 Exomiser、DeepRare 以及基于 Exomiser 生成的前 20 候选变异的提示式 LLM 重排序基线。消融与重叠分析表明，规则证据、机制背景与专家优化提供了互补信号。这些发现支持一种 grounded agentic 工作流，将证据整合、工具优化与 LLM 辅助诊断审查分离，以实现遗传性出生缺陷的回顾性变异优先级排序。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of interpreting genetic variants in birth defects where phenotypes are incomplete and evidence is heterogeneous, DeepBD introduces a grounded agentic workflow that combines LLM-assisted case structuring, a pretrained evidence engine, specialist modules, and a diagnostic review layer. The evidence engine integrates structured rules, sequence data, variant-effect predictions, and phenotype-conditioned biological context to score variants, while specialist modules refine candidate pools and synthesize diagnoses. Evaluated on an internal cohort of 18,622 cases, DeepBD achieved Recall@1/3/5/10 of 0.658/0.882/0.912/0.929, outperforming Exomiser, DeepRare, and LLM reranking baselines on Exomiser-derived top-20 variants. Ablation studies confirm that rule evidence, mechanistic context, and specialist refinement each contribute complementary signals to improve diagnostic accuracy.
为应对遗传性出生缺陷中因表型不完整和证据异质性导致的变异解读难题，DeepBD提出一种基于规则和代理的流程，整合结构化规则证据、变异效应表示及表型条件下的生物上下文，通过预训练证据引擎驱动，并辅以专家模块和大语言模型辅助诊断审查层。在包含18,622例的内部队列上，DeepBD在保留案例基准测试中达到0.658@1至0.929@10的高召回率，优于Exomiser、DeepRare及大语言模型重排序基线。消融分析表明，规则证据、机制上下文与专家优化三者贡献互补，验证了将证据整合、工具优化与大模型诊断审查分离的流程在遗传变异优先级排序中的有效性。
FAIRVAR: Fair Federated Learning via Variance Regularization
Authors: Zahra Kharaghani, Ali Dadras, Tommy Löfstedt
First: 2025-08-16T13:32:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:35:05+00:00
Comments: 27
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) allows collaborative training of machine learning models across multiple parties without sharing raw data. However, heterogeneous data can cause some clients to have disproportionate influence on the global model, leading to disparities in their performance. Fairness, understood as reducing these disparities, is therefore a crucial concern in FL and has been addressed in various ways. We studied performance equitable fairness in FL, where the goal is to minimize performance disparities across clients. We evaluated several existing fairness-aware methods and introduce here a new gradient-variance-regularized method, implemented in two variants: FairGrad (approximate) and FairGrad* (exact). We theoretically characterize the connections between these methods and, empirically, on heterogeneous benchmarks, show that FairGrad and FairGrad* consistently improve fairness by reducing variance in client accuracies, while maintaining competitive or improved mean performance compared to existing fairness-aware baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAIRVAR：通过方差正则化实现公平的联邦学习
联邦学习（FL）允许多方在不共享原始数据的情况下协同训练机器学习模型。然而，异构数据可能导致部分客户端对全局模型产生不成比例的影响，从而造成性能差异。公平性，即减少这些差异，是联邦学习中的关键问题，并已通过多种方式加以解决。我们研究了FL中的性能均衡公平性，目标是减少客户端间的性能差异。我们评估了若干现有公平感知方法，并在此引入一种新的梯度方差正则化方法，包含两种变体：FairGrad（近似）和FairGrad*（精确）。我们从理论上刻画了这些方法之间的联系，并在异构基准测试中实证表明，FairGrad和FairGrad*能持续提升公平性，通过降低客户端准确率的方差，同时保持与现有公平感知基线相当或更优的平均性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that heterogeneous data in federated learning can cause some clients to disproportionately influence the global model and thereby suffer from performance disparities, this paper proposes FairGrad and FairGrad*, two gradient-variance-regularized methods designed to reduce such disparities while preserving overall model performance. Theoretical analysis links these methods to existing fairness-aware approaches, and empirical evaluation across heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrates that both variants consistently reduce variance in client accuracies, achieving improved fairness without sacrificing mean performance relative to prior baselines.
为解决联邦学习中异构数据导致部分客户端对全局模型影响过大、性能不均的问题，本文提出FairGrad与FairGrad*两种基于梯度方差正则化的算法，旨在降低客户端间性能差异。理论分析揭示了这些方法与现有公平性方法的关联，实验表明在异构数据集上，两种方法均能有效降低客户端准确率方差，同时保持或提升平均性能，优于现有公平性基线方法。
Are We Ready For An Agent-Native Memory System?
Authors: Wei Zhou, Xuanhe Zhou, Shaokun Han, Hongming Xu, Guoliang Li, Zhiyu Li, Feiyu Xiong, Fan Wu
First: 2026-06-23T16:34:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:34:55+00:00
Comments: Paper list available at: https://github.com/OpenDataBox/awesome-agent-memory. Source code available at: https://github.com/OpenDataBox/MemoryData
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Memory for large language model (LLM) agents has rapidly evolved from simple retrieval-augmented mechanisms into a data management system that supports persistent information storage, retrieval, update, consolidation, and dynamic lifecycle governance throughout agent execution. Despite this evolution, existing evaluations still benchmark agent memory mainly through end-to-end task success metrics (e.g., F1, BLEU), while treating the underlying system as a monolithic black box. As a result, critical system-level concerns, including operational costs, architectural trade-offs across memory modules, and robustness under dynamic knowledge updates, remain insufficiently explored. In this paper, we present a systematic experimental study of agent memory from a data management perspective. We propose an analytical framework that decomposes agent memory into four core modules: memory representation and storage, extraction, retrieval and routing, and maintenance. Under this framework, we evaluate 12 representative memory systems and two reference baselines across five benchmark workloads spanning 11 datasets. Our extensive end-to-end evaluation shows that no single architecture dominates across all scenarios; instead, effectiveness depends heavily on how well the memory structure aligns with the workload bottleneck. Furthermore, through fine-grained ablation studies, we quantify their individual effects on representation fidelity, retrieval precision, update correctness, and long-horizon stability. Finally, we reveal cost-performance trade-offs under realistic workloads, showing localized maintenance is more cost-efficient than global reorganization. Based on these findings, we identify promising directions towards building truly agent-native memory systems. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/OpenDataBox/MemoryData.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们准备好迎接原生代理记忆系统了吗？
大型语言模型（LLM）代理的记忆系统已从简单的检索增强机制迅速演进为支持持久信息存储、检索、更新、整合及动态生命周期治理的数据管理系统。尽管如此，现有评估仍主要通过端到端任务成功率指标（如F1、BLEU）来衡量代理记忆，而将底层系统视为一个黑箱。因此，关键的系统级问题，如运营成本、不同记忆模块间的架构权衡，以及在动态知识更新下的鲁棒性，仍被严重忽视。本文从数据管理视角系统性地研究了代理记忆，提出一个分析框架，将代理记忆分解为四个核心模块：记忆表示与存储、提取、检索与路由、维护。在此框架下，我们评估了12个代表性记忆系统和两个基准模型，覆盖11个数据集的五个基准工作负载。我们的端到端评估表明，没有单一架构能在所有场景中占优；其有效性高度依赖于记忆结构与工作负载瓶颈的匹配程度。此外，通过细粒度消融研究，我们量化了各模块对表示保真度、检索精度、更新正确性及长时稳定性的影响。最后，我们在真实工作负载下揭示了成本-性能权衡，发现局部维护比全局重组更具成本效益。基于这些发现，我们指出了构建真正原生代理记忆系统的有前景方向。代码公开于https://github.com/OpenDataBox/MemoryData。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how agent memory systems perform under real-world data management demands, this study evaluates 12 memory architectures using a decomposed framework that isolates four core modules: representation/storage, extraction, retrieval/routing, and maintenance. Through five benchmark workloads across 11 datasets, the authors find no single architecture universally outperforms others, emphasizing alignment with workload-specific bottlenecks. Fine-grained ablation studies reveal that each module contributes differently to fidelity, precision, correctness, and long-term stability, while cost-efficiency analyses show localized maintenance is preferable to global reorganization under realistic conditions. The findings suggest that future agent-native memory systems must be architecturally adaptive and workload-aware.
为突破仅依赖任务成功率评估代理记忆系统的局限，本文从数据管理角度系统研究了代理记忆架构。提出包含表示存储、提取、检索路由和维护四个模块的分析框架，对12种记忆系统在五个基准负载下的表现进行比较。实验表明无单一架构能全面胜出，效果取决于记忆结构与负载瓶颈的匹配度。细粒度消融实验量化了各模块对保真度、精度、正确性及长期稳定性的贡献，同时发现局部维护在真实负载下比全局重组更具成本效益。
Revealing Training Data Exposure in Vision Language Large Models via Parameter Gradients
Authors: Zhihao Zhu, Hongyi Tang, Yi Yang, Ahmed Abbasi
First: 2026-06-23T16:34:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:34:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) trained on massive crawled corpora raise pressing copyright and data-provenance concerns. These concerns are particularly acute in healthcare, where patient medical images paired with clinical reports demand rigorous privacy safeguards. However, existing training data detection methods either fail in cross-modal scenarios or rely on superficial output signals with insufficient discriminative power. We introduce GradAudit, a gradient-based auditing framework that examines internal optimization dynamics rather than treating VLLMs as black boxes. Our approach builds on a key observation: model parameters converge to regions where gradients on training samples become stable and well-aligned, whereas gradients on non-training samples remain noisy and inconsistent. By analyzing these gradient signatures, GradAudit achieves strong separability and detects genuine image-text associations learned during training, not merely individual modality membership. Empirically, across both medical and general-domain datasets, GradAudit substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both pretraining and fine-tuning VLLMs. In a case study employing copyrighted content, we show that existing training data detection methods not only underestimate the extent of unauthorized data usage, but that this underestimation becomes more pronounced as models become more recent and more advanced.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过参数梯度揭示视觉语言大模型中的训练数据暴露
在大规模爬取语料上训练的视觉-语言大模型（VLLMs）引发了紧迫的版权和数据溯源问题，尤其在医疗领域，患者医学图像与临床报告的结合要求严格的隐私保护。然而，现有训练数据检测方法要么在跨模态场景中失效，要么依赖表面输出信号，判别能力不足。我们提出了GradAudit，一种基于梯度的审计框架，通过分析模型内部优化动态而非将VLLMs视为黑盒。我们的方法基于一个关键观察：模型参数收敛于训练样本梯度稳定且对齐的区域，而非训练样本的梯度则保持噪声且不一致。通过分析这些梯度特征，GradAudit实现强可分离性，能检测训练期间真正学习到的图像-文本关联，而不仅是单个模态成员身份。实证表明，在医疗和通用领域数据集上，GradAudit在预训练和微调VLLMs中均显著优于现有最先进基线。在使用受版权保护内容的案例研究中，我们证明现有训练数据检测方法不仅低估了未经授权数据使用的范围，且随着模型日益先进和近期，这种低估愈发严重。
Summary / 总结
Driven by concerns over copyright and data provenance in Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs), especially in sensitive domains like healthcare, this work introduces GradAudit, a gradient-based auditing method that probes internal optimization dynamics rather than surface-level outputs. It leverages the observation that training samples induce stable, aligned gradients in model parameters, while non-training samples produce noisy gradients, enabling detection of genuine image-text associations learned during training. Experiments across medical and general-domain datasets show GradAudit significantly outperforms existing methods in both pretraining and fine-tuning settings, and a case study reveals that current detection tools severely underestimate unauthorized data exposure, with this gap widening in more recent, advanced models.
为应对视觉语言大模型（VLLM）在训练数据版权与溯源方面的担忧，特别是医疗等敏感领域，本文提出GradAudit方法，通过分析模型参数梯度动态而非表面输出来检测训练数据。该方法基于训练样本诱导稳定对齐梯度、非训练样本梯度噪声大的观察，实现对真实图文关联的精准识别。实验表明，GradAudit在预训练与微调场景下均显著优于现有方法，且案例研究显示当前检测方法严重低估训练数据使用量，且模型越先进，低估越严重。
Posterior Refinement: Fast Language Generation via Any-Order Flow Maps
Authors: Manan Agarwal, Sheel Shah, Chanhyuk Lee, Jaehoon Yoo, Jerry Huang, Seunghoon Hong, Aditi Raghunathan, Jinwoo Kim, Nicholas M. Boffi
First: 2026-06-23T16:33:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:33:13+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 23 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-autoregressive generation offers a powerful paradigm for iterative refinement, allowing models to recursively critique, erase and regenerate arbitrary subsets of tokens. However, existing non-autoregressive models fail to realize this potential. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) suffer from factorization error, causing sample quality to collapse when generating multiple tokens simultaneously. Flow Map Language Models (FMLMs) circumvent this bottleneck via joint sequence transport for excellent few-step generation, but sacrifice the inference-time flexibility of MDMs. We introduce FMLM+, a framework that bridges this gap by equipping FMLM with masking-style noise schedules. While generating the full sequence in a single step, FMLM+ simultaneously scores the global consistency of each token a posteriori. We leverage this to introduce Posterior Refinement, a novel inference-time refinement strategy that enables the model to adaptively self-correct its outputs, matching the performance of discrete baselines with 32x fewer NFEs. Across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that FMLM+ with Posterior Refinement improves the speed--quality tradeoff over both MDM and FMLM families, providing a scalable foundation for high-fidelity language modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后验细化：通过任意顺序流图映射实现快速语言生成
非自回归生成提供了一种强大的迭代优化范式，使模型能够递归地批判、删除并重新生成任意子集的标记。然而，现有非自回归模型未能充分发挥这一潜力。掩码扩散模型（MDMs）因因子分解误差，在同时生成多个标记时会导致样本质量崩溃。流图语言模型（FMLMs）通过联合序列传输绕过这一瓶颈，实现卓越的几步生成，但牺牲了MDMs的推理时灵活性。我们提出FMLM+框架，通过为FMLM配备掩码式噪声调度，弥合这一差距。在单步生成完整序列的同时，FMLM+后验地评估每个标记的全局一致性。我们利用这一特性引入后验细化，一种新颖的推理时优化策略，使模型能够自适应地修正输出，以32倍更少的NFEs匹配离散基线性能。在多个基准测试中，我们证明FMLM+结合后验细化在速度-质量权衡上优于MDM和FMLM家族，为高保真语言建模提供了可扩展的基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve non-autoregressive language generation without sacrificing speed, the paper proposes FMLM+, a model that combines joint sequence transport from Flow Map Language Models with masking-style noise schedules to enable posterior refinement. This allows the model to generate full sequences in one step while scoring each token’s global consistency afterward, enabling adaptive self-correction during inference. Experimental results show that FMLM+ with Posterior Refinement matches discrete baseline performance using only 32x fewer noise function evaluations, significantly improving the speed–quality tradeoff over both Masked Diffusion Models and standard FMLMs across multiple benchmarks.
为提升非自回归语言生成的速度与质量平衡，本文提出FMLM+模型，结合流图语言模型的联合序列传输与掩码式噪声调度，在单步生成完整序列的同时对每个词元进行后验一致性评分，并在推理时自适应修正输出。实验表明，FMLM+在噪声函数评估次数减少32倍的情况下，性能可媲美离散自回归基线模型，在多个基准测试中优于掩码扩散模型和标准FMLM，显著改善了速度与质量的权衡。
Attention in Motion: Secure Platooning via Transformer-based Misbehavior Detection
Authors: Konstantinos Kalogiannis, Ahmed Mohamed Hussain, Hexu Li, Panos Papadimitratos
First: 2025-12-17T14:45:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:32:29+00:00
Comments: Author's version; Accepted for publication at the IEEE Transactions on Intelligent Transportation Systems (T-ITs)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vehicular platooning promises transformative improvements in transportation efficiency and safety through the coordination of multi-vehicle formations enabled by Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication. However, the distributed nature of platoon coordination creates security vulnerabilities, allowing authenticated vehicles to inject falsified kinematic data, compromise operational stability, and pose a threat to passenger safety. Traditional misbehaviour detection approaches, which rely on plausibility checks and statistical methods, suffer from high False Positive (FP) rates and cannot capture the complex temporal dependencies inherent in multi-vehicle coordination dynamics. We present Attention In Motion (AIMformer), a transformer-based framework specifically tailored for real-time misbehaviour detection in vehicular platoons with edge deployment capabilities. AIMformer leverages multi-head self-attention mechanisms to capture intra-vehicle temporal dynamics, with a spatio-temporal variant that further models inter-vehicle spatial correlations. It incorporates global positional encoding with vehicle-specific temporal offsets to handle join/exit maneuvers. We propose a Precision-Focused Binary Cross-Entropy (PFBCE) loss function that penalizes FPs to meet the requirements of safety-critical vehicular systems. Extensive evaluation across 4 platoon controllers, multiple attack vectors, and diverse mobility scenarios demonstrates superior performance ($\geq$ 0.93) compared to state-of-the-art baseline architectures. A comprehensive deployment analysis utilizing TensorFlow Lite (TFLite), Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX), and TensorRT achieves sub-millisecond inference latency, making it suitable for real-time operation on resource-constrained edge platforms. Hence, validating AIMformer is viable for both in-vehicle and roadside deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动态中的注意力：基于Transformer的车辆编队异常行为检测
车辆编队通过车对万物（V2X）通信实现多车协同，有望大幅提升交通效率与安全性。然而，编队协调的分布式特性带来安全漏洞，允许认证车辆注入伪造运动数据，破坏运行稳定性并威胁乘客安全。传统基于合理性检查和统计方法的异常检测方法存在高误报率，且无法捕捉多车协同中的复杂时序依赖。本文提出Attention In Motion（AIMformer），一种专为车辆编队实时异常检测设计的Transformer框架，支持边缘部署。AIMformer利用多头自注意力机制捕捉单车内时序动态，并通过时空变体建模车际空间相关性；结合全局位置编码与车辆特定期偏移，处理加入/退出操作。提出聚焦精度的二元交叉熵（PFBCE）损失函数，降低误报以满足安全关键系统需求。在4种编队控制器、多种攻击方式及多样移动场景下的广泛评估显示，其性能（≥0.93）优于现有最先进架构。通过TensorFlow Lite、ONNX和TensorRT的全面部署分析，实现亚毫秒级推理延迟，适用于资源受限边缘平台的实时运行，验证其车载与路边部署可行性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks in vehicular platooning due to falsified kinematic data from authenticated vehicles, the paper introduces Attention In Motion (AIMformer), a transformer-based system designed for real-time misbehavior detection with edge deployment. It employs multi-head self-attention to model intra-vehicle temporal dynamics and a spatio-temporal variant to capture inter-vehicle spatial correlations, enhanced by global positional encoding with vehicle-specific temporal offsets for maneuvers like joining or exiting. The system uses a Precision-Focused Binary Cross-Entropy loss to minimize false positives critical for safety. Evaluated across four platoon controllers, multiple attack types, and varied mobility scenarios, AIMformer achieves detection accuracy of at least 0.93, outperforming existing methods, and demonstrates sub-millisecond inference latency via TFLite, ONNX, and TensorRT, confirming its suitability for real-time edge deployment.
为应对车联编队中认证车辆注入虚假运动数据导致的安全隐患，本文提出Attention In Motion（AIMformer）模型，一种基于Transformer的实时异常行为检测框架。该模型利用多头自注意力机制捕捉单车内部时序动态，并通过时空变体建模车辆间空间关联，结合全局位置编码与车辆特定期偏移以处理加入/退出等机动行为。模型采用精度聚焦二元交叉熵损失函数以降低误报率，符合安全关键系统要求。在四种编队控制器、多种攻击方式及多样移动场景下测试，其检测准确率不低于0.93，优于现有方法，且通过TFLite、ONNX和TensorRT部署后推理延迟低于毫秒级，适用于资源受限边缘平台的实时部署。
Dirac-Frenkel dynamics with inertia for nonlinearly parametrized solutions of evolution problems
Authors: Matteo Raviola, Benjamin Peherstorfer
First: 2026-06-23T16:30:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:30:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Even when Dirac-Frenkel dynamics determine a well-defined evolution in function space, the corresponding parameter dynamics can be non-unique or ill-conditioned for redundant nonlinear parametrizations such as neural networks or mixture models. We propose to add inertia to the Dirac-Frenkel dynamics and show that this allows useful parameter velocity information to persist from the past trajectory in directions that are weakly informed, while well-informed parameter velocity directions continue to follow the Dirac-Frenkel dynamics. We prove that the inertial formulation yields well-posed parameter dynamics and provide a posteriori error bounds. After time discretization, the method requires the solution of the same type of regularized linear least-squares problem as standard Dirac-Frenkel dynamics, but with the previous velocity appearing as an anchor. Numerical experiments demonstrate the increased robustness obtained with inertia.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带惯性的狄拉克-弗伦克尔动力学用于非线性参数化的演化问题解
即使狄拉克-弗伦克尔动力学在函数空间中定义了明确的演化，对于神经网络或混合模型等冗余非线性参数化，相应的参数动力学可能非唯一或病态。我们提出在狄拉克-弗伦克尔动力学中加入惯性，并证明这使得弱信息方向上的参数速度信息能从过去轨迹中持续保留，而强信息方向上的参数速度仍遵循狄拉克-弗伦克尔动力学。我们证明了惯性形式的参数动力学是良定义的，并提供了后验误差界。时间离散化后，该方法需求解与标准狄拉克-弗伦克尔动力学相同类型的正则化线性最小二乘问题，但前一速度作为锚点出现。数值实验表明惯性增强了方法的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that Dirac-Frenkel dynamics can produce non-unique or ill-conditioned parameter trajectories when applied to redundant nonlinear parametrizations like neural networks, the paper introduces inertia into the dynamics to preserve useful velocity information from past steps in poorly informed directions while maintaining alignment with Dirac-Frenkel in well-informed ones. The method reformulates the parameter evolution to be well-posed and provides a posteriori error bounds, and after time discretization, it solves a regularized linear least-squares problem with the previous velocity acting as an anchor. Numerical experiments confirm that this inertial approach enhances robustness compared to the standard formulation.
为解决Dirac-Frenkel动力学在神经网络等冗余非线性参数化下的参数动力学非唯一性和病态问题，该文在动力学中引入惯性项，使弱信息方向保留历史速度信息，而强信息方向仍遵循Dirac-Frenkel规律，从而保证参数动力学良定并具备后验误差界。数值实验表明，该惯性方法比标准方法更鲁棒，离散化后仍需求解相同类型的正则化线性最小二乘问题，但需将前一时刻速度作为锚点。
Compact Object-Level Representations with Open-Vocabulary Understanding for Indoor Visual Relocalization
Authors: Zhaopeng Cui, Jiarui Hu, Jingbo Liu, Boming Zhao, Xiyue Guo, Boyin Feng, Haocheng Peng, Yujun Shen, Hujun Bao, Guofeng Zhang
First: 2026-06-23T16:27:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:27:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted by RA-L 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Indoor visual relocalization plays a critical role in emerging spatial and embodied AI applications. However, prior research was predominantly devoted to low-level vision schemes, struggling to perceive scene semantics and compositions, which limits both interpretability and applicability. In this paper, we explore the issue of how to organize rich object information in a scene, including semantics, layout, and geometry, into a structured map representation, thereby utilizing object units exclusively to drive the camera relocalization task. To this end, we propose OpenReLoc, a camera relocalization system designed to provide scene understanding and accurate pose estimation capabilities. Leveraging recent foundation models, we first introduce a multi-modal mechanism to integrate open-vocabulary semantic knowledge for effective 2D-3D object matching. Additionally, we design object-oriented reference frames as position priors, paired with a reference frame selection strategy based on the Distance-IoU (DIOU), enabling extension to scalable scenes. Moreover, to ensure stable and accurate pose optimization, we also propose a dual-path 2D Iterative Closest Pixel loss guided by object shape. Experimental results demonstrate that OpenReLoc achieves superior relocalization recall and accuracy across various datasets. Our source code will be released upon acceptance.
