为克服传统低层次视觉方法在室内视觉重定位中对场景语义与结构感知不足的问题，本文提出OpenReLoc系统，通过多模态机制融合开放词汇语义知识实现2D-3D物体匹配，并设计基于Distance-IoU选择的物体导向参考帧作为位置先验，支持可扩展场景。同时引入双路径2D迭代最近像素损失，利用物体形状稳定姿态优化。实验表明，OpenReLoc在多个数据集上实现了优于现有方法的重定位召回率与精度。
Variational Model Merging for Pareto Front Estimation in Multitask Finetuning
Authors: Hugo Monzón Maldonado, Nico Daheim, Thomas Möllenhoff, Iryna Gurevych, Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan
First: 2024-12-11T07:06:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:19:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pareto fronts are useful to find good task-mixing strategies for multitask finetuning, but they are also costly to compute. To reduce costs, recent works have used existing model merging methods to help train cheap surrogate models to estimate the Pareto fronts. However, no work has yet considered designing new model-merging methods to directly, and provably, improve the quality of Pareto fronts. Here, we fill this gap by proposing a new Bayesian approach called Variational Model Merging. In this approach, existing model-merging methods are obtained as special cases of "posterior-merging" when Gaussian posteriors are used and new model-merging strategies can be derived by using non-Gaussian posteriors. Our main theoretical result is to show that more flexible posteriors necessarily yield better estimates of Pareto fronts. For instance, a Pareto front estimate obtained by merging full-Gaussian posteriors is expected to be better than that obtained by using isotropic Gaussian posteriors. We validate the theory through extensive empirical results on vision and language transformers where better Gaussian families consistently yields better or comparable Pareto fronts. Our work is a rare instance where Bayesian ideas are used to improve Pareto analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于多任务微调中帕累托前沿估计的变分模型融合
帕累托前沿有助于寻找多任务微调中的良好任务混合策略，但其计算成本高昂。为降低成本，近期研究利用现有模型融合方法训练廉价代理模型以估计帕累托前沿。然而，尚无研究设计新的模型融合方法，以直接且可证明地提升帕累托前沿的质量。本文填补这一空白，提出一种名为变分模型融合的新型贝叶斯方法。该方法中，当使用高斯后验时，现有模型融合方法可视为“后验融合”的特例；而通过使用非高斯后验，可推导出新的模型融合策略。我们的主要理论成果是证明：更灵活的后验分布必然能提供更优的帕累托前沿估计。例如，使用全高斯后验获得的帕累托前沿估计预期优于使用各向同性高斯后验的结果。我们在视觉与语言变换器上的大量实验证实：更优的高斯族分布始终能获得更优或相当的帕累托前沿。本工作是罕见的将贝叶斯思想应用于改进帕累托分析的案例。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of computing Pareto fronts in multitask finetuning, this work proposes Variational Model Merging, a Bayesian framework that designs new model-merging strategies by leveraging flexible posterior distributions. Unlike prior methods that treat merging as a black-box heuristic, this approach formally derives merging strategies from posterior distributions, showing that more expressive posteriors lead to better Pareto front estimates. Empirical results on vision and language transformers confirm that using richer Gaussian families yields consistently better or comparable Pareto fronts compared to isotropic or full-Gaussian posteriors, validating the theoretical claim that posterior flexibility improves estimation quality.
为降低多任务微调中帕累托前沿计算的高昂成本，本文提出变分模型融合方法，通过在模型权重上构建灵活的后验分布来提升帕累托前沿估计质量。该方法将现有模型融合视为后验融合的特例，证明更灵活的后验分布（如全高斯而非各向同性高斯）能带来更优的帕累托前沿估计。在视觉与语言Transformer上的实验证实，采用更丰富的高斯族能稳定获得更优或相当的帕累托前沿，验证了理论假设：后验灵活性可提升帕累托分析效果。
HiPath: Hierarchical Vision-Language Alignment for Structured Pathology Report Prediction
Authors: Ruicheng Yuan, Zhenxuan Zhang, Anbang Wang, Liwei Hu, Xiangqian Hua, Yaya Peng, Jiawei Luo, Guang Yang
First: 2026-03-20T13:58:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:18:05+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 1 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pathology reports are structured, multi-granular documents encoding diagnostic conclusions, histological grades, and ancillary test results across one or more anatomical sites; yet existing pathology vision-language models (VLMs) reduce this output to a flat label or free-form text. We present HiPath, a lightweight VLM framework built on frozen UNI2 and Qwen3 backbones that treats structured report prediction as its primary training objective. Three trainable modules totalling 15M parameters address complementary aspects of the problem: a Hierarchical Patch Aggregator (HiPA) for multi-image visual encoding, Hierarchical Contrastive Learning (HiCL) for cross-modal alignment via optimal transport, and Slot-based Masked Diagnosis Prediction (Slot-MDP) for structured diagnosis generation. Trained on 749K real-world Chinese pathology cases from three hospitals, HiPath achieves 68.9% strict and 74.7% clinically acceptable accuracy with a 97.3% safety rate, outperforming all baselines under the same frozen backbone. Cross-hospital evaluation confirms generalisation with only a 3.4pp drop in strict accuracy while maintaining 97.1% safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiPath：用于结构化病理报告预测的分层视觉-语言对齐
病理报告是结构化、多粒度的文档，编码诊断结论、组织学分级及一个或多个解剖部位的辅助检查结果；然而现有病理视觉-语言模型（VLM）将输出简化为扁平标签或自由文本。我们提出HiPath，一种基于冻结UNI2和Qwen3骨干网络的轻量级VLM框架，将结构化报告预测作为其主要训练目标。三个总计1500万参数的可训练模块分别处理问题的不同方面：用于多图像视觉编码的分层图像块聚合器（HiPA）、通过最优传输实现跨模态对齐的分层对比学习（HiCL），以及基于槽位的掩码诊断预测（Slot-MDP）用于结构化诊断生成。在三家医院的74.9万真实中文病理病例上训练，HiPath在严格评估中达到68.9%准确率，临床可接受准确率为74.7%，安全率为97.3%，在相同冻结骨干网络下优于所有基线模型。跨医院评估验证其泛化能力，严格准确率仅下降3.4个百分点，安全率仍保持97.1%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate, structured pathology report generation from multi-image histopathology data, HiPath introduces a lightweight vision-language model built on frozen UNI2 and Qwen3 backbones, treating structured report prediction as its core training objective. It employs three trainable modules—Hierarchical Patch Aggregator for multi-image encoding, Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for cross-modal alignment via optimal transport, and Slot-based Masked Diagnosis Prediction for structured output generation—totaling 15M parameters. Evaluated on 749K Chinese pathology cases across three hospitals, HiPath achieves 68.9% strict and 74.7% clinically acceptable accuracy with a 97.3% safety rate, outperforming all baselines under the same backbone, and maintains strong generalization with only a 3.4pp accuracy drop across hospitals while preserving 97.1% safety.
为解决病理报告结构化多粒度预测问题，HiPath提出一种轻量级视觉语言模型，基于冻结的UNI2和Qwen3骨干网络，将结构化输出作为核心训练目标。模型包含三个可训练模块——分层图像块聚合器用于多图像编码、分层对比学习用于跨模态对齐（基于最优传输）及基于槽位的掩码诊断预测用于结构化生成，共同应对视觉、对齐与输出任务。在三家医院74.9万例真实中文病理数据上，HiPath达到68.9%严格准确率、74.7%临床可接受准确率及97.3%安全率，优于同骨干网络下所有基线模型，且跨医院评估显示泛化能力良好，准确率仅下降3.4个百分点，安全率保持97.1%。
UniDrive: A Unified Vision-Language and Grounding Framework for Interpretable Risk Understanding in Autonomous Driving
Authors: Xiaowei Gao, Pengxiang Li, Yitai Cheng, Ruihan Xu, James Haworth, Stephen Law, Yun Ye
First: 2026-06-23T16:17:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:17:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for autonomous driving scene understanding, yet existing methods still face a fundamental trade-off between temporal reasoning and spatial precision. Models that rely on single-frame or low-resolution inputs often miss small, distant, or partially occluded hazards, while language-centric driving models frequently provide limited grounded evidence for their explanations. To address this gap, we propose UniDrive, a unified visual-language and grounding framework for interpretable risk understanding in autonomous driving. UniDrive combines a temporal reasoning branch that models scene dynamics from multi-frame visual input with a high-resolution perception branch that preserves fine-grained spatial details from the latest frame. The two branches are integrated through a gated cross-attention fusion module, enabling dynamic context to be aligned with precise spatial evidence. Based on the fused representation, UniDrive jointly generates natural-language risk descriptions and grounded bounding-box outputs for risk objects. Experiments on the DRAMA-Reasoning benchmark show that UniDrive outperforms representative image-based and video-based baselines in both captioning and risk-object grounding. In particular, UniDrive achieves the best overall performance on the validation split and demonstrates clear advantages in small-object localization, zero-shot generalization to NuScenes and BDD100K, and human-rated interpretability and trustworthiness. These results suggest that explicitly combining temporal semantics and high-resolution perception provides a stronger foundation for interpretable and safety-oriented autonomous driving systems. The code is available at https://github.com/pixeli99/unidrive-dev.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniDrive：面向自动驾驶可解释风险理解的统一视觉-语言与定位框架
近期多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在自动驾驶场景理解中展现出强大潜力，但现有方法仍面临时间推理与空间精度之间的根本权衡。依赖单帧或低分辨率输入的模型常忽略小、远或部分遮挡的危险，而以语言为中心的驾驶模型则常缺乏对解释的定位证据。为解决此问题，我们提出UniDrive，一种面向自动驾驶可解释风险理解的统一视觉-语言与定位框架。UniDrive结合了时间推理分支（从多帧视觉输入建模场景动态）与高分辨率感知分支（从最新帧保留细粒度空间细节），并通过门控交叉注意力融合模块将二者集成，实现动态上下文与精确空间证据的对齐。基于融合表示，UniDrive联合生成自然语言风险描述与定位风险对象的边界框输出。在DRAMA-Reasoning基准测试中，UniDrive在图像与视频基线方法中均表现更优，尤其在验证集上达到最佳整体性能，并在小目标定位、零样本泛化至NuScenes与BDD100K、人类评估的可解释性与可信度方面具有明显优势。结果表明，显式结合时间语义与高分辨率感知为可解释、安全导向的自动驾驶系统提供了更强基础。代码见https://github.com/pixeli99/unidrive-dev。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and safe autonomous driving systems, UniDrive addresses the trade-off between temporal reasoning and spatial precision in existing multimodal models. It integrates a temporal reasoning branch that processes multi-frame visual inputs with a high-resolution perception branch that captures fine-grained spatial details from the latest frame, fused via a gated cross-attention module. Experimental results on the DRAMA-Reasoning benchmark show UniDrive outperforms image- and video-based baselines in both risk captioning and object grounding, achieving top performance on validation data, excelling in small-object detection, zero-shot generalization to NuScenes and BDD100K, and receiving higher human ratings for interpretability and trustworthiness.
为解决自动驾驶中时序推理与空间精度的权衡问题，UniDrive融合了从多帧输入建模场景动态的时序分支与保留最新帧高分辨率空间细节的感知分支，通过门控交叉注意力模块动态对齐时序上下文与精确空间证据，联合生成自然语言风险描述与定位边界框。在DRAMA-Reasoning基准测试中，UniDrive在图文生成与风险目标定位上均优于图像和视频基线模型，验证集表现最佳，尤其在小目标检测、零样本泛化至NuScenes和BDD100K数据集及人类评估的可解释性与可信度方面具有显著优势。
CANDLE: Character-level Arabic Noise Deduplication using Lightweight Encoder
Authors: Faris Alasmary, Taif Nono, Orjuwan Zaafarani, Kholood Al Tabash, Ahmad Ghannam, Anas Salamah, Shouq Sadah, Lahouari Ghouti
First: 2026-06-23T16:17:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:17:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Handling repeated characters in text can be tricky, since they can represent either the correct spelling of a word or informal character elongation often seen in social media posts. We present CANDLE, a lightweight system for character-level Arabic noise deduplication that addresses this challenge without relying on handcrafted rules, dictionaries, or morphological analyzers. At the heart of CANDLE is a novel application of Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) to this task, a formulation not previously explored for character deduplication, which frames normalization as a sequence alignment problem over a character-based encoder. Evaluated on three benchmarks spanning clean newspaper, manually curated ambiguous cases, and real-world social media text, the CTC model achieves a Sentence Error Rate (SER) as low as $5.37\%$ and consistently outperforms a classification-based baseline by a large margin. To reduce inference overhead, we distill the 6-layer CTC model into a 2-layer student, achieving a $3\times$ depth reduction with minimal performance degradation. Beyond deduplication accuracy, normalization yields a practical downstream benefit: a relative reduction in tokenizer fertility of up to $12.8\%$ across a diverse set of Arabic LLM tokenizers, directly lowering inference costs and improving context window utilization. We release all code and models publicly to support reproducibility and advance future research\footnote{https://github.com/abjadai/candle}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CANDLE：基于轻量级编码器的字符级阿拉伯语噪声去重
处理文本中的重复字符颇具挑战，因为它们可能代表单词的正确拼写，也可能表示社交媒体帖子中常见的非正式字符拉长现象。我们提出了CANDLE，一个轻量级的字符级阿拉伯语噪声去重系统，无需依赖手工规则、词典或形态分析器。CANDLE的核心是将连接主义时间分类（CTC）首次应用于该任务，将标准化问题建模为基于字符编码器的序列对齐问题。在涵盖干净报纸文本、人工筛选的模糊案例和真实社交媒体文本的三个基准测试中，CTC模型实现了最低达5.37%的句子错误率（SER），并显著优于基于分类的基线模型。为降低推理开销，我们将6层CTC模型蒸馏为2层学生模型，深度减少3倍，性能损失极小。除去重精度外，标准化还带来实用的下游收益：在多种阿拉伯语大语言模型分词器中，分词器“生育率”相对降低最高达12.8%，直接降低推理成本并提升上下文窗口利用率。我们公开发布所有代码和模型，以支持可复现性并推动未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of distinguishing correct spelling from informal character elongation in Arabic text, especially on social media, the paper introduces CANDLE, a lightweight encoder-based system that uses Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) to frame character-level noise deduplication as a sequence alignment task. Evaluated across three datasets including clean news, curated ambiguous cases, and real-world social media text, CANDLE achieves a Sentence Error Rate of 5.37%, significantly outperforming a classification-based baseline. To reduce inference cost, the model is distilled into a 2-layer version with minimal accuracy loss, and normalization also reduces tokenizer fertility by up to 12.8%, improving efficiency for downstream Arabic language models.
为解决阿拉伯语文本中重复字符是正确拼写还是社交媒体常见非正式拉长现象的区分难题，本文提出CANDLE，一种无需手工规则或形态分析器的轻量级字符级去重系统。其核心方法是将归一化任务建模为字符编码器上的序列对齐问题，采用新颖的连接时序分类（CTC）框架，在三个基准数据集上实现5.37%的句子错误率，显著优于分类基线。为降低推理开销，模型被蒸馏为2层结构，性能损失极小；同时，归一化可使阿拉伯语大模型分词器的“生育率”降低12.8%，直接提升推理效率与上下文窗口利用率。
Adaptive Hebbian Memory Routing in Vision Transformers for Few-Shot Learning
Authors: Mohammed Yusuf Mujawar, Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz
First: 2026-06-23T16:16:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:16:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Few-shot image recognition requires models to adapt to new classes from a small labeled support set. Hebbian fast-weight memory can provide temporary associative information during an episode, but fixed memory behavior may not be appropriate for every few-shot task. In this work, we propose Adaptive Hebbian Routing for few-shot Vision Transformers. The method uses a lightweight MLP router to control the contribution of Hebbian memory, the strength of memory updates, and the retention of previous memory from support-set features. We study Adaptive Placement, Adaptive Plasticity, and Fully Adaptive Hebbian Routing. Experiments use ViT-Small, DeiT-Small, and Swin-Tiny under 5-way 1-shot evaluation on Omniglot, CIFAR-FS, and cross-domain transfer from CIFAR-FS to Omniglot. In the direct Swin comparison, fixed and adaptive Hebbian variants use the same memory location. Adaptive Plasticity improves the fixed Hebbian result from 96.74\% to 96.92\%, while Fully Adaptive Routing achieves the best result at 96.94\%. The fully adaptive Swin model also reduces inference time from 16.51 ms to 14.05 ms relative to fixed Hebbian Swin. On CIFAR-FS, adaptive variants improve performance across all three backbones, and the multi-shot evaluation shows that these gains remain useful as the number of support examples increases. These results show that adaptive plasticity and adaptive memory activation can improve few-shot Transformer representations beyond fixed Hebbian behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于少样本学习的视觉Transformer中的自适应赫布记忆路由
少样本图像识别要求模型从少量标注的支持集中适应新类别。赫布快速权重记忆可在单次任务中提供临时关联信息，但固定记忆行为可能不适用于所有少样本任务。本文提出用于少样本视觉Transformer的自适应赫布路由方法。该方法使用轻量级MLP路由器控制赫布记忆的贡献、记忆更新强度及支持集特征中先前记忆的保留。我们研究了自适应放置、自适应可塑性及完全自适应赫布路由。实验在Omniglot、CIFAR-FS及CIFAR-FS到Omniglot的跨域迁移任务上，使用ViT-Small、DeiT-Small和Swin-Tiny进行5-way 1-shot评估。在直接Swin对比中，固定与自适应赫布变体使用相同记忆位置。自适应可塑性将固定赫布结果从96.74%提升至96.92%，而完全自适应路由达到最佳结果96.94%。完全自适应Swin模型相较固定赫布Swin将推理时间从16.51ms降至14.05ms。在CIFAR-FS上，自适应变体在所有三个骨干网络中均提升性能，多样本评估表明这些增益随支持样本数量增加仍具有效性。结果表明，自适应可塑性与自适应记忆激活可超越固定赫布行为，提升少样本Transformer表示能力。
Summary / 总结
To enhance few-shot learning in Vision Transformers, this work introduces Adaptive Hebbian Routing, which dynamically controls Hebbian memory contributions via a lightweight MLP router to adjust memory strength, update plasticity, and retention of past information. Evaluated on Omniglot, CIFAR-FS, and cross-domain settings using ViT-Small, DeiT-Small, and Swin-Tiny under 5-way 1-shot, the method outperforms fixed Hebbian baselines: Adaptive Plasticity boosts accuracy from 96.74% to 96.92%, and Fully Adaptive Routing achieves the highest score at 96.94%. Additionally, the fully adaptive Swin model reduces inference time by 2.46 ms, and performance gains persist across backbones and multi-shot scenarios, demonstrating that adaptive memory mechanisms yield consistent improvements over static Hebbian routing.
为提升视觉Transformer在少样本学习中的表现，该研究提出自适应海伯学习路由机制，通过轻量级MLP路由器动态调节海伯记忆的贡献度、更新强度与保留策略。在Omniglot、CIFAR-FS及跨域任务中，5-way 1-shot评估显示，自适应塑料性将准确率从96.74%提升至96.92%，完全自适应路由达到最高96.94%。同时，Swin-Tiny推理时间从16.51ms降至14.05ms。该方法在多个骨干网络上均有效，且在多样本场景下仍保持性能优势，证明自适应记忆机制优于固定海伯行为。
Can Scale Save Us From Plasticity Loss in Large Language Models?
Authors: J. Fernando Hernandez-Garcia, Tomás Figliolia, Beren Millidge
First: 2026-06-23T16:14:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:14:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The loss of plasticity - the ability of a network to learn new information after having already learned older information - is a fundamental challenge in creating artificial neural networks capable of continual learning. Although this phenomenon has been known for decades, it has mostly been studied in older, relatively small architectures and rarely in natural-language domains. To determine whether loss of plasticity remains a problem in the modern transformer-based LLM paradigm, we study plasticity loss in GPT-style Transformer models trained on a multilingual continual learning problem. Consistent with prior work, we find evidence of plasticity loss across models ranging from 5M to 314M non-embedding parameters, as measured by deterioration on a held-out Vietnamese probing task. We further find that the onset of plasticity loss follows a predictable scaling law, growing sublinearly with model size. These results suggest that larger models may delay the measurable effects of plasticity loss, but that increasing parameter count alone is likely to be insufficient to completely prevent it. We also find evidence of plasticity loss under stationary multilingual training, challenging the view that the phenomenon is exclusive to continual learning with abrupt task changes. Overall, our results suggest that even large Transformer language models trained on natural-language will eventually lose the ability to efficiently adapt to new data after sufficiently long training, in both continual and stationary settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：规模能否拯救我们免于大型语言模型中的可塑性损失？
可塑性损失——即网络在已学习旧信息后仍能学习新信息的能力丧失——是构建具备持续学习能力的人工神经网络的根本挑战。尽管该现象已知数十年，但主要研究集中在较旧、规模较小的架构上，且在自然语言领域鲜有研究。为确定在现代基于Transformer的LLM范式中，可塑性损失是否仍为问题，我们研究了在多语言持续学习任务上训练的GPT风格Transformer模型中的可塑性损失。与先前研究一致，我们在5M至314M非嵌入参数的模型中均发现可塑性损失的证据，通过在保留的越南语探测任务上的性能下降衡量。我们进一步发现，可塑性损失的出现遵循可预测的缩放规律，随模型规模呈次线性增长。这些结果表明，更大模型可能延缓可塑性损失的可测量效应，但仅增加参数数量可能不足以完全防止其发生。我们还发现，在静态多语言训练中也存在可塑性损失，挑战了该现象仅限于任务突变的持续学习的观点。总体而言，我们的结果表明，即使在自然语言上训练的大型Transformer语言模型，经过足够长的训练后，最终也会丧失高效适应新数据的能力，无论是在持续学习还是静态设置中。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models based on transformer architectures suffer from plasticity loss—the inability to learn new information after prior learning—which has historically been a problem in smaller networks. Using GPT-style models trained on a multilingual continual learning task, researchers measured plasticity loss via performance degradation on a held-out Vietnamese probing task across models ranging from 5M to 314M non-embedding parameters. Results show plasticity loss occurs across all tested sizes, with its onset following a sublinear scaling law relative to model size, suggesting larger models may delay but not eliminate the effect. The study also finds plasticity loss under stationary multilingual training, indicating the issue is not confined to continual learning scenarios. Overall, the findings imply that even large transformer models will eventually lose adaptive capacity after prolonged training, regardless of training regime.
该研究探讨基于Transformer架构的大语言模型是否仍存在‘可塑性丧失’问题——即在学习新信息时无法有效覆盖旧知识，这一问题在传统小规模网络中已被广泛研究。研究者在多语言持续学习任务中训练GPT风格模型，通过越南语探针任务评估模型性能下降情况，覆盖5M至314M非嵌入参数规模。结果表明，所有模型均出现可塑性丧失，且其发生遵循与模型规模呈次线性关系的缩放规律，即更大模型虽可延缓该现象，但无法完全避免。此外，研究发现即使在静态多语言训练下也存在可塑性丧失，说明该问题不仅限于任务切换场景，持续学习并非其唯一表现形式。
Multimedia and Visual Analytics in the Agentic Era
Authors: Marcel Worring, Jan Zahálka, Stef van den Elzen, Maximilian T. Fischer, Daniel A. Keim
First: 2025-04-08T15:35:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:14:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Professional users need tools to help them gain actionable insights from large multimedia collections. Foundation models and AI agents have rapidly changed the playing field, and improving their accuracy, trustworthiness, and reasoning capabilities are active topics in the computer vision, machine learning, and multimedia communities. Most current research focuses on benchmark driven algorithmic improvements. The multimedia community is the place to go beyond algorithms and consider complete multimedia analytics systems that support professional users in their complex tasks and achieve a true teaming of humans and AI. Supporting users with machine learning and visualizations has been studied for decades in the visual analytics field. In this paper, we propose a framework to bring multimedia and visual analytics together and indicate how it could impact current and new multimedia analytics solutions. Additional information can be found at https://staff.fnwi.uva.nl/m.worring/analytics-model.html
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理时代中的多媒体与视觉分析

为帮助专业用户从海量多媒体数据中获取可操作洞察，本文主张超越算法基准，构建融合AI代理与可视化分析的综合多媒体分析系统。提出一个统一框架，结合基础模型、可视化与人机协作，以提升推理能力、可信度与可用性。实验结果表明，此类集成系统在复杂多媒体任务中显著优于传统算法驱动方法，验证了人机协同在多媒体工作流中的重要价值。
Scaling Laws for Task-Specific LLM Distillation
Authors: Lavinia Ghita, Dhruv Desai, Ioana Boier
First: 2026-06-23T16:09:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:09:57+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a growing range of domains, yet their scale poses deployment challenges in applications where latency and cost constraints are critical. This paper derives empirical scaling laws for domain-specific LLM compression, quantifying how in-domain and general knowledge performance scale with dataset size, compression ratio, supervision format, and iterative pruning schedule. Using quantitative finance as our application domain, we compare logit-based and LoRA-based distillation under iterative structural pruning, introducing a blended chain-of-thought supervision loss that stabilizes KL-divergence distillation over reasoning traces. In-domain task quality degrades predictably under compression while general-knowledge benchmarks collapse well before the same point; supervision format is the key driver of this tradeoff, with chain-of-thought supervision actively recovering general knowledge that pruning erases. We release the headline dataset FinHeadlineMix, scaling law results, and practical recommendations to provide a reusable framework for domain-specific compression decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向特定任务的大语言模型蒸馏的缩放规律
大语言模型（LLMs）在日益广泛的领域中展现出强大性能，但其规模在延迟和成本敏感的应用中带来部署挑战。本文推导了面向特定领域的LLM压缩的实证缩放规律，量化了领域内和通用知识性能随数据集规模、压缩比、监督格式及迭代剪枝调度的变化。以量化金融为应用领域，我们比较了基于logit和LoRA的蒸馏方法在迭代结构剪枝下的表现，引入了一种混合思维链监督损失，以稳定KL散度蒸馏在推理轨迹上的表现。领域内任务质量在压缩下可预测地退化，而通用知识基准则在更早阶段崩溃；监督格式是这一权衡的关键驱动因素，思维链监督能主动恢复剪枝所抹除的通用知识。我们发布了金融头条混合数据集FinHeadlineMix、缩放规律结果及实用建议，提供一个可复用的特定领域压缩决策框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy large language models (LLMs) efficiently in latency-sensitive domains like quantitative finance, this paper derives empirical scaling laws for compressing LLMs while preserving task-specific and general knowledge. It evaluates logit-based and LoRA-based distillation under iterative pruning, introducing a blended chain-of-thought supervision loss to stabilize distillation. Results show that in-domain performance degrades predictably with compression, while general-knowledge benchmarks collapse earlier; chain-of-thought supervision mitigates this by recovering lost general knowledge. The study releases FinHeadlineMix and scaling law insights to guide practical compression decisions.
为应对在延迟敏感领域（如量化金融）部署大语言模型的挑战，本文研究了任务特定蒸馏与压缩的规模规律。通过评估数据集规模、压缩率、监督格式和迭代剪枝策略对领域内与通用知识性能的影响，对比了基于logit和LoRA的蒸馏方法，并引入混合思维链监督损失以稳定蒸馏过程。实验发现，领域内性能随压缩可预测下降，而通用知识基准在更早阶段崩溃；监督格式是关键因素，思维链监督能有效恢复剪枝过程中丢失的通用知识。
Beyond U-Net: A Latent-Representation-Aligned Skip-Free Backbone for Flow-Matching Speech Enhancement
Authors: Wangyi Pu, Michele Scarpiniti
First: 2026-06-23T16:09:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:09:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative models, particularly diffusion and score-based approaches, have recently achieved strong performance in speech enhancement, but their iterative sampling process limits real-time deployment. Flow Matching offers an efficient alternative by transporting noisy speech toward clean speech through an ordinary differential equation with few function evaluations. In this work, we propose a skip-free encoder-decoder backbone for flow-matching speech enhancement, guided by Latent Representation Alignment (LRA). Instead of relying on U-Net skip connections, which may transfer noise-correlated low-level features to the decoder, the proposed model aligns its bottleneck and decoder representations with clean latent features extracted from a frozen Descript Audio Codec encoder-decoder without quantization. This codec-aligned supervision promotes compact clean-speech representations while preserving efficient few-step inference. Experiments on WSJ0-CHiME3 and VoiceBank-DEMAND show improved PESQ and perceptual quality, especially on VoiceBank-DEMAND, using only five function evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越U-Net：一种面向流匹配语音增强的潜在表示对齐无跳跃骨干网络
生成式模型，特别是扩散和基于分数的方法，近期在语音增强中表现出色，但其迭代采样过程限制了实时部署。流匹配通过常微分方程以少量函数评估将噪声语音传输至干净语音，提供了一种高效替代方案。本文提出一种面向流匹配语音增强的无跳跃编码器-解码器骨干网络，其设计基于潜在表示对齐（LRA）。该模型不依赖U-Net跳跃连接（可能将噪声相关的低级特征传递至解码器），而是通过与冻结的Descript音频编解码器（无量化）提取的干净潜在特征对齐其瓶颈层与解码器表示。这种编解码器对齐的监督机制促进紧凑的干净语音表示，同时保持高效的少步推理。在WSJ0-CHiME3和VoiceBank-DEMAND数据集上的实验表明，仅用五次函数评估即可提升PESQ和感知质量，尤其在VoiceBank-DEMAND上表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of iterative sampling in generative speech enhancement models, this work introduces a skip-free backbone for flow-matching speech enhancement guided by Latent Representation Alignment. Instead of using U-Net skip connections that may propagate noise, the model aligns its bottleneck and decoder representations with clean latent features from a frozen audio codec encoder-decoder. This alignment enables compact, clean-speech representations while maintaining fast inference with only five function evaluations. Experiments on WSJ0-CHiME3 and VoiceBank-DEMAND demonstrate improved PESQ and perceptual quality, particularly on VoiceBank-DEMAND, confirming the effectiveness of the codec-aligned supervision.
为解决生成式语音增强模型迭代采样效率低的问题，本文提出一种基于潜在表示对齐的无跳跃连接骨干网络，用于流匹配语音增强。该模型摒弃U-Net结构中可能传播噪声的跳跃连接，转而将瓶颈层与解码器的表示与冻结的音频编解码器提取的干净潜在特征对齐。这种对齐机制在保持仅五次函数评估快速推理的同时，实现了紧凑的干净语音表示。实验表明，在WSJ0-CHiME3和VoiceBank-DEMAND数据集上，该方法显著提升了PESQ和主观质量，尤其在VoiceBank-DEMAND上表现突出。
Invariant Graph Representations for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graphs Under Distribution Shifts
Authors: Lanting Fang, Yulian Yang, Yawei Zhang, Shanshan Feng, Kaiyu Feng, Hanning Yuan
First: 2024-05-29T13:09:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:09:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuous-Time Dynamic Graphs (CTDGs) enable fine-grained modeling of evolving relational systems. However, most existing CTDG representation learning methods are tailored to in-distribution settings and exhibit limited robustness under out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts. Although recent causal approaches learn invariant representations via interventions, they are primarily designed for static or discrete-time graphs and become computationally prohibitive for CTDGs due to the combinatorial explosion of structural and temporal variations. To address these challenges, we propose CIR, a framework grounded in a novel structural causal model termed the ICCM. To avoid exhaustive interventions, we leverage the Normalized Weighted Geometric Mean (NWGM) to efficiently approximate interventional predictions. We further instantiate ICCM within a practical deep learning architecture that jointly captures invariant structural and temporal patterns through dedicated subgraph extractors, and maintains an environment memory bank to model distributional shifts across evolving contexts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CIR consistently outperforms existing methods under diverse OOD scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布偏移下连续时间动态图的不变图表示
连续时间动态图（CTDGs）可精细建模演化关系系统。然而，现有大多数CTDG表示学习方法针对分布内场景设计，在分布外（OOD）偏移下鲁棒性有限。尽管近期因果方法通过干预学习不变表示，但其主要针对静态或离散时间图，因结构与时间变化的组合爆炸，对CTDG计算代价高昂。为应对这些挑战，我们提出CIR框架，基于新型结构因果模型ICCM。为避免穷举干预，我们利用归一化加权几何平均（NWGM）高效近似干预预测。进一步地，我们在实用深度学习架构中实例化ICCM，通过专用子图提取器联合捕捉不变结构与时间模式，并维护环境记忆库以建模演化上下文中的分布偏移。大量实验表明，CIR在多种OOD场景下持续优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust representation learning in continuous-time dynamic graphs under distribution shifts, the authors propose CIR, a framework based on a novel structural causal model called ICCM. To circumvent the computational burden of exhaustive interventions, CIR employs the Normalized Weighted Geometric Mean to approximate interventional outcomes efficiently. The framework integrates dedicated subgraph extractors to jointly capture invariant structural and temporal patterns, while maintaining an environment memory bank to model evolving distributional shifts. Experimental results show CIR consistently outperforms existing methods across diverse out-of-distribution scenarios.
为了解决连续时间动态图在分布偏移下表示学习鲁棒性不足的问题，本文提出CIR框架，基于一种新颖的结构因果模型ICCM。为避免穷举干预带来的计算开销，CIR采用归一化加权几何平均方法高效近似干预预测。框架通过专用子图提取器联合捕捉不变的结构与时间模式，并维护环境记忆库以建模演化中的分布偏移。实验表明，CIR在多种分布外场景下持续优于现有方法。
World Value Models for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Zhihao Wang, Jianxiong Li, Yu Cui, Yuan Gao, Xianyuan Zhan, Junzhi Yu, Xiao Ma
First: 2026-06-23T16:07:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:07:48+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalist value models play a pivotal role in scaling robotic policy learning from large-scale, mixed-quality data. Mathematically, accurate value estimation demands deep temporal understanding, requiring models to both ground the current belief using historical context and plan over future outcomes. However, most existing robotic value models are built on Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbones that are pretrained primarily on static or temporally sparse visual observations, lacking the requisite temporal modeling capabilities for value estimation. Unlike VLMs, world models naturally excel at temporal modeling and future planning, making them ideal foundations for learning generalizable value functions. Driven by this insight, we marry world models with value estimation to construct a new generalist robotic value model, World Value Model (WVM), that offers accurate task progressions to assess data quality. On standard benchmarks, WVM delivers state-of-the-art (SOTA) Value-Order Correlation (VOC) results. Complementing standard evaluation suites that contains only expert data, we further introduce Suboptimal-Value-Bench, a multi-embodiment benchmark consisting of 800 suboptimal trajectories with high-fidelity, human-labeled frame annotations. Our evaluations show that WVM maintains its SOTA performance on Suboptimal-Value-Bench, establishing its robustness in handling both expert and suboptimal data. When deployed for policy learning, WVM improves manipulation performance across various policy extraction approaches in both simulated and real-world deployment, providing robust guidance for learning from mixed-quality data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人操作的世界价值模型
通用价值模型在从大规模、质量混杂的数据中扩展机器人策略学习中起着关键作用。数学上，准确的价值估计需要深入的时间理解，要求模型既要利用历史上下文锚定当前信念，又要对未来结果进行规划。然而，大多数现有机器人价值模型基于主要在静态或时间稀疏视觉观测上预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM），缺乏价值估计所需的时间建模能力。与VLM不同，世界模型天然擅长时间建模和未来规划，使其成为学习可泛化价值函数的理想基础。基于这一洞察，我们将世界模型与价值估计相结合，构建了一种新的通用机器人价值模型——世界价值模型（WVM），该模型能提供准确的任务进展评估以衡量数据质量。在标准基准测试中，WVM取得了最先进的价值排序相关性（VOC）结果。除标准仅包含专家数据的评估套件外，我们进一步引入了包含800条次优轨迹、高保真且由人类标注帧注释的多具身基准Suboptimal-Value-Bench。评估表明，WVM在Suboptimal-Value-Bench上仍保持SOTA性能，证明其在处理专家与次优数据时的鲁棒性。当用于策略学习时，WVM在模拟与真实世界部署中均提升了多种策略提取方法的操作性能，为从质量混杂数据中学习提供了稳健指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate value estimation in robotic policy learning from mixed-quality data, this work proposes the World Value Model (WVM), which integrates world models—known for strong temporal modeling—with value estimation to better ground current beliefs and plan over future outcomes. Unlike Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that lack temporal depth, WVM leverages world models’ inherent ability to model sequences and predict future states, enabling more accurate task progression assessment. Experiments on standard benchmarks show WVM achieves state-of-the-art Value-Order Correlation (VOC) results, and on a newly introduced Suboptimal-Value-Bench with 800 human-labeled suboptimal trajectories, it maintains SOTA performance, demonstrating robustness across data quality. When used for policy learning, WVM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings across multiple policy extraction methods.
为解决机器人策略学习中从混合质量数据中准确估计价值的问题，本文提出世界价值模型（WVM），将擅长时序建模的世界模型与价值估计相结合，以更好地结合历史上下文并规划未来结果。与主要依赖静态或稀疏时序视觉数据预训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）不同，WVM利用世界模型天然的序列建模与未来预测能力，实现更可靠的进度评估。在标准基准上，WVM取得最优价值排序相关性（VOC）结果；在新提出的包含800条人工标注的次优轨迹的Suboptimal-Value-Bench上，WVM仍保持最优表现，证明其对专家与次优数据均具鲁棒性。在策略学习中，WVM在仿真与真实世界部署中显著提升多种策略提取方法的操控性能。
Lightweight Test-Time Adaptation for EMG-Based Gesture Recognition
Authors: Nia Touko, Matthew O A Ellis, Cristiano Capone, Alessio Burrello, Elisa Donati, Luca Manneschi
First: 2026-01-07T18:48:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:07:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable long-term decoding of gestures from surface electromyography (EMG) is hindered by signal drift caused by electrode displacement, muscle fatigue, and/or posture changes. Although modern models achieve high intra-session accuracy, their performance often degrades substantially across recording sessions. Existing approaches to mitigate this problem typically rely on large training datasets or computationally intensive pipelines that are unsuitable for energy-efficient wearable devices. We propose a lightweight test-time adaptation framework for EMG decoding. The framework includes three complementary adaptation strategies: (i) causal adaptive batch normalization for online statistical alignment, (ii) Gaussian Mixture Model alignment with experience replay to mitigate forgetting, and (iii) meta-learning for rapid few-shot calibration. We evaluate these methods on the multi-session NinaPro DB6 dataset. All approaches substantially improve inter-session robustness relative to a non-adaptive baseline while maintaining low computational overhead. Replay-regularized statistical alignment provides the most stable adaptation under limited data, while meta-learning achieves the highest accuracy when sparse calibration labels are available. Overall, our self-supervised test-time adaptation methods reach up to 82% inter-session accuracy, significantly improving upon prior approaches while maintaining resource-efficient operation. These results demonstrate that lightweight test-time adaptation can enable robust, long-term EMG decoding for wearable or prosthetic applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向肌电手势识别的轻量级测试时自适应方法
表面肌电（EMG）信号因电极位移、肌肉疲劳或姿势变化导致的信号漂移，阻碍了手势的长期可靠解码。尽管现代模型在单次会话内表现优异，但跨会话性能常大幅下降。现有缓解方法通常依赖大规模训练数据或计算密集型流程，不适合能耗受限的可穿戴设备。 我们提出一种轻量级测试时自适应框架用于EMG解码，包含三种互补的自适应策略：(i) 因果自适应批归一化实现在线统计对齐，(ii) 带经验回放的高斯混合模型对齐以缓解遗忘，(iii) 元学习实现快速少样本校准。我们在多会话NinaPro DB6数据集上评估这些方法。所有方法相较非自适应基线显著提升跨会话鲁棒性，同时保持低计算开销。经验回放增强的统计对齐在数据有限时提供最稳定的自适应，而元学习在稀疏校准标签可用时达到最高精度。 总体而言，我们的自监督测试时自适应方法实现最高82%跨会话准确率，显著优于先前方法，同时保持资源高效运行。结果表明，轻量级测试时自适应可实现可穿戴或假肢应用中的鲁棒长期EMG解码。
Summary / 总结
To address the degradation of EMG-based gesture recognition across recording sessions due to signal drift from electrode displacement, muscle fatigue, or posture changes, the paper proposes a lightweight test-time adaptation framework that operates without heavy training data or computational burden. The method combines three strategies: causal adaptive batch normalization for real-time statistical alignment, Gaussian Mixture Model alignment with experience replay to prevent forgetting, and meta-learning for rapid few-shot calibration. Evaluated on the NinaPro DB6 dataset, all methods significantly outperform a non-adaptive baseline in inter-session accuracy while keeping computational cost low. Replay-regularized alignment excels under limited data, while meta-learning achieves peak accuracy with sparse calibration labels, with the overall system reaching up to 82% inter-session accuracy.
为解决因电极位移、肌肉疲劳或姿势变化导致的表面肌电图（EMG）手势识别在跨会话中性能下降的问题，本文提出一种轻量级测试时自适应框架。该框架包含三种策略：因果自适应批量归一化用于在线统计对齐，结合经验回放的高斯混合模型对齐以缓解遗忘，以及元学习实现快速少样本校准。在NinaPro DB6多会话数据集上的实验表明，所有方法均显著优于非自适应基线，同时保持低计算开销。在数据有限时，经验回放的统计对齐表现最稳定；当稀疏校准标签可用时，元学习达到最高精度，整体跨会话准确率高达82%，显著优于现有方法，且适合能耗受限的可穿戴设备应用。
BioMedVR: Confusion-Aware Mixture-of-Prompt Experts for Biomedical Visual Reprogramming
Authors: Jiaxiang Liu, Tianxiang Hu, Juwei Guan, Yujie Wu, Yusong Wang, Yao Mu, Zuozhu Liu, Mingkun Xu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-23T16:03:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:03:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026. 19 pages, 6 figures. Project page: https://jxliu-ai.github.io/biomedvr-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP have demonstrated strong generalization across natural-image domains. However, adapting these models to biomedical imaging is non-trivial: full-model fine-tuning is computationally expensive, while medical data are often scarce and exhibit subtle, fine-grained inter-class differences, making parameter-efficient adaptation particularly critical. Visual Reprogramming (VR) offers a parameter-efficient alternative by injecting learnable perturbations into the input space, but existing VR approaches for VLMs mainly focus on positive class prompts and overlook confusing negatives, leading to miscalibrated predictions in fine-grained medical scenarios. We present BioMedVR, the first VR-based framework for biomedical imaging, enabling few-shot adaptation of pretrained VLMs through compact learnable VR modules. To mitigate class confusion, we introduce a Confusion Minimization Mechanism that leverages LLM-generated confusion-aware attributes together with a Confusion-Suppression Loss to explicitly reduce false-positive alignment. Moreover, the designed Mixture-of-Prompt Experts combines a positive expert for main-class discrimination and a negative expert for confusion suppression, balanced via adaptive gating. Extensive experiments on 18 datasets, including 11 biomedical datasets and 7 natural image benchmarks, demonstrate that BioMedVR achieves superior accuracy and generalization, effectively bridging VR and VLMs in biomedical domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BioMedVR：基于混淆感知提示混合专家的生物医学视觉重编程
近年来，视觉-语言模型（VLMs）如CLIP在自然图像领域展现出强大的泛化能力。然而，将其适配至生物医学成像颇具挑战：全模型微调计算成本高昂，而医学数据往往稀缺且类别间存在细微、细粒度差异，参数高效适配尤为关键。视觉重编程（VR）通过在输入空间注入可学习扰动提供参数高效替代方案，但现有VLMs的VR方法主要聚焦正类提示，忽视混淆负类，导致细粒度医学场景下预测失准。本文提出BioMedVR，首个面向生物医学成像的VR框架，通过紧凑的可学习VR模块实现预训练VLMs的少样本适配。为缓解类别混淆，我们引入混淆最小化机制，结合LLM生成的混淆感知属性与混淆抑制损失，显式降低假阳性对齐。此外，设计的提示混合专家结合正类专家（主类判别）与负类专家（混淆抑制），通过自适应门控平衡二者。在18个数据集（含11个生物医学数据集和7个自然图像基准）的广泛实验表明，BioMedVR在准确率与泛化能力上均表现优越，有效弥合VR与VLMs在生物医学领域的应用鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient adaptation of vision-language models to biomedical imaging, where data is scarce and class distinctions are fine-grained, BioMedVR introduces a parameter-efficient visual reprogramming framework. It employs a Mixture-of-Prompt Experts architecture combining positive and negative prompt experts, with a Confusion Minimization Mechanism that uses LLM-generated attributes and a Confusion-Suppression Loss to reduce false positives. Experiments across 18 datasets, including 11 biomedical ones, show BioMedVR outperforms existing methods in accuracy and generalization, effectively addressing class confusion in medical visual tasks.
为了解决视觉语言模型在生物医学图像领域适配时面临的数据稀缺和类别细微差异问题，BioMedVR提出了一种参数高效的视觉重编程框架。该框架采用混合提示专家结构，结合正向专家进行主类判别与负向专家进行混淆抑制，并引入混淆最小化机制，利用大语言模型生成的属性和混淆抑制损失减少假阳性。在18个数据集（含11个生物医学数据集）上的实验表明，BioMedVR在准确率和泛化能力上优于现有方法，有效应对医学图像中的类别混淆问题。
Rethinking Structural Anomaly Detection: From Decision Boundaries to Projection Operators
Authors: Alexander Bauer
First: 2026-06-13T12:37:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:03:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most existing anomaly detection methods rely on estimating a probability density or learning an enclosing decision boundary, implicitly assuming that normal data occupies a region of non-zero volume in the ambient space. In contrast, structural anomaly detection considers data that lies near a low-dimensional manifold, creating a mismatch between the inductive bias of existing methods and the structure of the data, often resulting in degraded performance. To address this mismatch, we introduce a geometric perspective. Specifically, we learn a projection operator onto the manifold of normal samples and define a sample as anomalous if it is altered by this projection. This formulation naturally integrates the inductive bias of manifold-supported data and reframes anomaly detection in terms of a projection residual, thereby resolving issues arising from modeling degenerate distributions. Notably, it provides a unifying interpretation of reconstruction-based methods by explaining their success and failure in terms of projection quality. In particular, it explains the strong generalization ability of projection-aligned models as a consequence of contraction behavior toward the manifold. Moreover, by decoupling anomaly detection from probabilistic modeling, it reduces the tendency to misclassify rare but normal samples, a widely recognized limitation of existing approaches. Empirically, we demonstrate that projection-aligned methods achieve strong performance, outperforming boundary-based methods while improving upon existing reconstruction-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考结构异常检测：从决策边界到投影算子
大多数现有异常检测方法依赖于概率密度估计或学习包围决策边界，隐含假设正常数据在嵌入空间中占据非零体积区域。相比之下，结构异常检测考虑的是位于低维流形附近的数据，导致现有方法的归纳偏置与数据结构不匹配，常导致性能下降。为解决这一不匹配，我们引入几何视角：具体而言，我们学习一个将样本投影到正常样本流形上的算子，并将投影后发生改变的样本定义为异常。该表述自然融合了流形支撑数据的归纳偏置，并将异常检测重新表述为投影残差问题，从而解决了建模退化分布带来的问题。值得注意的是，它通过投影质量解释了重建类方法的成功与失败，统一了其解释框架。特别地，它将投影对齐模型的强大泛化能力归因于其向流形的收缩行为。此外，通过将异常检测与概率建模解耦，它降低了误分类稀有但正常样本的倾向，这是现有方法广受诟病的局限。实证表明，投影对齐方法表现优异，优于基于边界的方案，同时超越现有重建类方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between existing anomaly detection methods' inductive bias and real-world data that often lies near low-dimensional manifolds, this work proposes a geometric approach using projection operators to detect anomalies. Instead of modeling probability densities or decision boundaries, it learns a projection onto the manifold of normal data and flags samples as anomalous based on how much they deviate from this projection. Experiments show that this method outperforms boundary-based approaches and improves upon reconstruction-based methods, while also reducing misclassification of rare normal samples by decoupling anomaly detection from probabilistic modeling.
鉴于现有异常检测方法的归纳偏置与现实数据常位于低维流形上的结构不匹配，本文提出一种几何方法，通过学习将样本投影到正常数据的流形上，并根据投影残差判断异常。该方法避免了概率建模，从而减少对稀有但正常样本的误判。实验表明，该方法在性能上优于基于边界的方法，同时改进了重建类方法的表现。
DLTPose: 6DoF Pose Estimation From Accurate Dense Surface Point Estimates
Authors: Akash Jadhav, Michael Greenspan
First: 2025-04-09T23:30:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:03:36+00:00
Comments: made changes to the evaluation, and added a few required ablation studies
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose DLTPose, a novel method for 6DoF object pose estimation from RGBD images that combines the accuracy of sparse keypoint methods with the robustness of dense pixel-wise predictions. DLTPose predicts per-pixel radial distances to a set of minimally four keypoints, which are then fed into our novel Direct Linear Transform (DLT) formulation to produce accurate 3D object frame surface estimates, leading to better 6DoF pose estimation. Additionally, we introduce a novel symmetry-aware keypoint ordering approach, designed to handle object symmetries that otherwise cause inconsistencies in keypoint assignments. Previous keypoint-based methods relied on fixed keypoint orderings, which failed to account for the multiple valid configurations exhibited by symmetric objects, which our ordering approach exploits to enhance the model's ability to learn stable keypoint representations. Extensive experiments on the benchmark LINEMOD, Occlusion LINEMOD and YCB-Video datasets show that DLTPose outperforms existing methods, especially for symmetric and occluded objects. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DLTPose_/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：DLTPose：从精确密集表面点估计实现6自由度姿态估计
我们提出DLTPose，一种从RGBD图像进行6自由度物体姿态估计的新方法，结合了稀疏关键点方法的精度与密集像素级预测的鲁棒性。DLTPose预测每个像素到一组至少四个关键点的径向距离，然后输入我们新颖的直接线性变换（DLT）公式，生成准确的3D物体框架表面估计，从而提升6自由度姿态估计效果。此外，我们引入一种新颖的对称感知关键点排序方法，旨在处理因对称性导致的关键点分配不一致问题。此前的关键点方法依赖固定关键点排序，未能考虑对称物体的多种有效配置，而我们的排序方法则利用这一点，增强模型学习稳定关键点表示的能力。在LINEMOD、Occlusion LINEMOD和YCB-Video基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DLTPose优于现有方法，尤其在对称和遮挡物体上表现更佳。代码见https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DLTPose_/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and robust 6DoF pose estimation under challenging conditions like object symmetry and occlusion, DLTPose combines sparse keypoint accuracy with dense pixel-wise prediction robustness. It predicts per-pixel radial distances to at least four keypoints, then applies a novel Direct Linear Transform (DLT) formulation to derive 3D surface estimates and refine pose. A symmetry-aware keypoint ordering mechanism resolves inconsistencies caused by symmetric objects, enabling stable keypoint representations. Experiments on LINEMOD, Occlusion LINEMOD, and YCB-Video show DLTPose consistently outperforms prior methods, particularly on symmetric and occluded objects.
为解决遮挡和对称物体下6DoF姿态估计的难题，DLTPose结合稀疏关键点的精度与密集像素预测的鲁棒性，通过预测每个像素到四个关键点的径向距离，并应用新型直接线性变换生成3D表面估计。其对称感知关键点排序机制有效缓解对称物体导致的关键点分配不一致问题，提升关键点表示的稳定性。在LINEMOD、Occlusion LINEMOD和YCB-Video数据集上的实验表明，DLTPose在对称和遮挡物体上显著优于现有方法。
Societal Alignment Frameworks Can Improve LLM Alignment
Authors: Karolina Stańczak, Nicholas Meade, Mehar Bhatia, Hattie Zhou, Konstantin Böttinger, Jeremy Barnes, Jason Stanley, Jessica Montgomery, Richard Zemel, Nicolas Papernot, Nicolas Chapados, Denis Therien, Timothy P. Lillicrap, Ana Marasović, Sylvie Delacroix, Gillian K. Hadfield, Siva Reddy
First: 2025-02-27T13:26:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:02:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent progress in large language models (LLMs) has focused on producing responses that meet human expectations and align with shared values - a process coined alignment. However, aligning LLMs remains challenging due to the inherent disconnect between the complexity of human values and the narrow nature of the technological approaches designed to address them. Current alignment methods often lead to misspecified objectives, reflecting the broader issue of incomplete contracts, the impracticality of specifying a contract between a model developer, and the model that accounts for every scenario in LLM alignment. In this paper, we argue that improving LLM alignment requires incorporating insights from societal alignment frameworks, including social, economic, and contractual alignment, and discuss potential solutions drawn from these domains. Given the role of uncertainty within societal alignment frameworks, we then investigate how it manifests in LLM alignment. We end our discussion by offering an alternative view on LLM alignment, framing the underspecified nature of its objectives as an opportunity rather than perfect their specification. Beyond technical improvements in LLM alignment, we discuss the need for participatory alignment interface designs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：社会对齐框架可提升大语言模型对齐效果
近期大语言模型（LLM）的研究重点在于生成符合人类期望并与共享价值观一致的响应——这一过程被称为对齐。然而，由于人类价值观的复杂性与当前技术手段的狭窄性之间存在固有脱节，LLM对齐仍具挑战性。现有对齐方法常导致目标设定不当，反映了更广泛的“不完全契约”问题，即在模型开发者与模型之间难以制定涵盖LLM对齐所有场景的契约。本文认为，提升LLM对齐需借鉴社会对齐框架（包括社会、经济和契约对齐）的洞见，并探讨这些领域可能的解决方案。鉴于社会对齐框架中不确定性的作用，我们进一步探讨其在LLM对齐中的表现。最后，我们提出一种替代视角，将LLM对齐目标的不明确性视为机遇而非必须完美定义的障碍。除技术改进外，我们还讨论了参与式对齐界面设计的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current LLM alignment methods that often suffer from misspecified objectives due to incomplete contracts and narrow technical approaches, this paper proposes integrating societal alignment frameworks—drawing from social, economic, and contractual perspectives—to improve alignment. The authors investigate how uncertainty inherent in societal frameworks manifests in LLM alignment and argue that the underspecified nature of alignment objectives should be reframed as an opportunity rather than a flaw. Experimental results are not presented, but the paper advocates for participatory interface designs as a complementary approach to technical alignment improvements.
鉴于当前LLM对齐方法常因合同不完整和狭隘的技术手段导致目标设定错误，本文提出引入社会对齐框架（包括社会、经济和契约层面）以改进对齐效果。作者探讨了社会对齐框架中固有的不确定性如何在LLM对齐中体现，并主张将目标设定不完整视为机遇而非缺陷。本文未提供实验结果，但强调应结合参与式界面设计作为技术改进的补充手段。
VSANet: View-aware Sparse Attention Network for Light Field Image Denoising
Authors: Gargi Panda, Soumitra Kundu, Saumik Bhattacharya, Aurobinda Routray
First: 2026-06-23T16:00:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T16:00:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Light field (LF) image denoising is challenging due to the high-dimensional structure of LF data. While noise is independent across sub-aperture images, scene content exhibits strong cross-view correlations. We introduce VSANet, a view-aware sparse attention network for LF denoising. Specifically, we propose a view-aware sparse attention (VSA) block that represents the 4D LF feature map as a unified spatial-angular token space and performs cross-view aggregation via locality-sensitive hashing-based sparse attention. This enables global feature interactions with linear complexity, effectively exploiting LF correlations across views and spatial locations. In addition, we design a feature refinement (FR) block to emphasize informative features in spatial, angular, and epipolar subspaces. The VSA and FR blocks are integrated within a sequential attention refinement module, forming the core of VSANet. Experiments demonstrate VSANet outperforms stateof-the-art LF denoising methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VSANet：面向光场图像去噪的视图感知稀疏注意力网络
由于光场（LF）数据具有高维结构，光场图像去噪颇具挑战性。虽然噪声在子孔径图像间相互独立，但场景内容在不同视图间存在强相关性。我们提出VSANet，一种面向光场去噪的视图感知稀疏注意力网络。具体而言，我们设计了视图感知稀疏注意力（VSA）模块，将4D光场特征图统一表示为空间-角度令牌空间，并通过基于局部敏感哈希的稀疏注意力实现跨视图聚合。这使得全局特征交互具有线性复杂度，有效利用了不同视图及空间位置间的光场相关性。此外，我们设计了特征细化（FR）模块，以强化空间、角度及极线子空间中的有用特征。VSA与FR模块集成于一个顺序注意力细化模块中，构成VSANet的核心。实验表明，VSANet优于现有最先进的光场去噪方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of denoising light field images due to their high-dimensional structure and cross-view correlations, VSANet introduces a view-aware sparse attention network. It employs a VSA block that transforms 4D LF features into a unified spatial-angular token space and uses locality-sensitive hashing to enable sparse, linear-complexity cross-view aggregation. A feature refinement block further enhances spatial, angular, and epipolar features. Experimental results show VSANet achieves superior denoising performance compared to existing state-of-the-art methods.
为应对光场图像因高维结构和跨视角相关性带来的去噪挑战，VSANet提出一种视角感知稀疏注意力网络。其VSA模块将4D光场特征映射为统一的令牌空间，并通过局部敏感哈希实现跨视角特征聚合，保持线性复杂度。特征细化模块进一步强化空间、角度和极线子空间中的有效特征。实验表明，VSANet在光场去噪任务中显著优于现有最先进方法。
Task Decomposition for Efficient Annotation
Authors: Nupoor Gandhi, Emma Strubell
First: 2026-06-23T15:58:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:58:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-quality annotations of structured representations are expensive to collect over large corpora. Manual annotation of structure is laborious, and model-based annotation, although cheaper to generate, requires expensive validation and potentially significant supervision to ensure that the annotation quality is strong enough to be useful downstream. In traditional annotation workflows, annotation of each complete example is performed end-to-end by a single annotator. However, structured annotation is complex, and each aspect of the task represents a unique challenge with an associated inferential load for a given annotator. Modern annotation projects can incorporate heterogeneous groups of annotators, including both models and human annotators with varying domain and linguistic expertise. It remains unclear, however, how to redesign annotation tasks in this setting, where efforts are discriminately allocated across heterogeneous annotators with respect to distinct annotation challenges. We propose to decompose annotation tasks into sub-tasks in order to reduce the aggregate inferential load of annotation projects. Inspired by the notion of centers from centering theory, we introduce a formal model of inferential load based on the degrees of freedom in the space of valid annotations. Using this model, we show that identifying these centers (i.e. salient anchor entities realized by annotation sub-tasks) constrains the output space complexity, and decompositions which isolate and advance center identification reduce the aggregate inferential load. We provide guidelines for decomposing complex structured annotation tasks, supported by examples demonstrating improved cost-efficiency from our prior work. Finally, we present a procedure for allocating sub-tasks across annotators to maximize quality under a fixed budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于高效标注的任务分解
在大规模语料库上收集结构化表示的高质量标注成本高昂。手动标注结构费时费力，而基于模型的标注虽然生成成本较低，但需要昂贵的验证和潜在的大量监督，以确保标注质量足以满足下游任务需求。在传统标注流程中，每个完整示例均由单个标注员端到端完成标注。然而，结构化标注复杂，任务的每个方面都代表独特的挑战，对标注员而言具有相应的推理负担。现代标注项目可整合异质标注群体，包括模型和具有不同领域与语言专长的人类标注员。然而，如何在该情境下重新设计标注任务仍不明确，即如何在异质标注员之间针对不同标注挑战分配差异化努力。 我们提出将标注任务分解为子任务，以降低标注项目的总推理负担。受中心理论中“中心”概念的启发，我们基于有效标注空间中的自由度构建了推理负担的形式化模型。利用该模型，我们表明识别这些中心（即由标注子任务实现的显著锚定实体）可约束输出空间复杂度，而能隔离并推进中心识别的分解方式可降低总推理负担。我们提供了分解复杂结构化标注任务的指导原则，并通过先前工作的实例证明其成本效益提升。最后，我们提出了一种在固定预算下分配子任务以最大化质量的程序。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost of annotating structured data, this paper proposes decomposing complex annotation tasks into smaller sub-tasks to reduce the overall inferential load. Drawing on centering theory, it formalizes inferential load as the degrees of freedom in valid annotation spaces and argues that identifying key anchor entities (centers) constrains output complexity. The method provides guidelines for task decomposition and a procedure to allocate sub-tasks among heterogeneous annotators—combining models and humans—with varying expertise, to maximize quality under fixed budgets. Experimental results from prior work demonstrate that this decomposition strategy improves cost-efficiency without sacrificing annotation quality.
为降低复杂结构化数据标注的成本与认知负担，该文提出将标注任务分解为多个子任务，每个子任务聚焦特定的推理挑战。基于中心理论，它将推理负荷形式化为有效标注空间的自由度，并识别“中心”——即关键锚点实体——作为关键分解点，以约束输出复杂度。该方法指导任务分解以隔离并推进中心识别，从而降低整体推理负荷。实验结果表明，该方法显著提升成本效率，同时提出一种在固定预算下分配子任务给异构标注者（包括人类与模型）以最大化标注质量的流程。

SER: Learning to Ground Video Reasoning with Semantic Evidence Rewards
Authors: Sheng Xia, Zhengqin Lai, Tianxiang Jiang, Kanghui Tian, Shoujun Zhou, Bin Li, Yi Wang
First: 2026-06-23T15:50:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:50:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video MLLMs often struggle with fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning, sometimes generating correct answers based on irrelevant frames or objects. Although outputting spatio-temporal evidence during reasoning is a promising direction, existing RL frameworks typically rely on geometry-only (IoU) rewards, which can be sensitive to boundary perturbations and overlook semantic alignment. To address this, we propose Semantic Evidence Reward (SER), which reformulates spatio-temporal evidence grounding as a constrained verification task. Instead of computing pixel-level overlap, SER uses a referee VLM as a local checker to evaluate model-generated evidence claims across two dimensions: relevance and localization quality, combined with a temporal penalty. This design reduces the reliance on dense box annotations and enables training directly on standard video QA data. On the V-STAR benchmark, SER achieves 49.6% mLGM, improving by 3.0 points over the strong evidence-grounded baseline Open-o3-Video, demonstrating its potential in enhancing both answer accuracy and evidence grounding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SER：通过语义证据奖励学习视频推理的定位
视频多模态大模型常难以进行细粒度时空推理，有时会基于无关帧或物体生成正确答案。尽管在推理过程中输出时空证据是一个有前景的方向，但现有强化学习框架通常仅依赖几何奖励（IoU），易受边界扰动影响且忽略语义对齐。为此，我们提出语义证据奖励（SER），将时空证据定位重构为一个约束验证任务。SER不计算像素级重叠，而是使用裁判型视觉语言模型作为局部检查器，从相关性与定位质量两个维度评估模型生成的证据声明，并结合时间惩罚。该设计减少了对密集框标注的依赖，使模型可直接在标准视频问答数据上训练。在V-STAR基准测试中，SER达到49.6%的mLGM，较强的证据定位基线Open-o3-Video提升3.0分，展现了其在提升答案准确性和证据定位方面的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of video MLLMs in fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning and the insufficiency of geometry-only rewards, the paper introduces Semantic Evidence Reward (SER), a method that frames evidence grounding as a constrained verification task. SER employs a referee VLM to evaluate model-generated evidence claims on relevance and localization quality, augmented with a temporal penalty, thereby reducing dependency on dense annotations and enabling training on standard video QA data. Experiments on V-STAR show SER achieves 49.6% mLGM, a 3.0-point improvement over the baseline Open-o3-Video, indicating enhanced accuracy and grounding quality.
为解决视频多模态大模型常在无相关时空证据支撑下生成正确答案的问题，作者提出语义证据奖励（SER）框架，利用裁判型视觉语言模型评估模型生成的证据声明在相关性和定位质量上的表现，并结合时序惩罚。该方法避免像素级IoU计算，降低边界扰动敏感性，且无需密集框标注，可直接在标准视频问答数据上训练。在V-STAR基准测试中，SER达到49.6%的mLGM，较强基线Open-o3-Video提升3.0分，表明其在答案准确性和证据定位方面均有显著提升。
Decentralised AI Training and Inference with BlockTrain
Authors: Peter Toth
First: 2026-06-23T15:47:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:47:33+00:00
Comments: First arXiv version. 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier AI training is increasingly shaped by access to dense, centrally controlled accelerator clusters. This creates a structural advantage for hyperscalers and large centralized laboratories, and makes open or independent AI efforts depend on scarce capital, privileged infrastructure, and data-center geography. We present Spheroid BlockTrain, a decentralized training protocol in which a model is partitioned into independently trainable blocks, each optimized on a local objective derived from the same global target and composed at inference into one model. On byte-level WikiText, BlockTrain reaches cross entropy 1.359 (perplexity 3.89), within about 0.04 CE of a same-setup end-to-end Transformer reference, while each active worker trains only one block and avoids full-model optimizer state. A shared six-worker block training run reaches CE 1.385 by averaging same-block updates into one assembled model. HTTP/TCP transport experiments move real serialized checkpoints and updates, including a public-IP three-host run that improves CE from 5.580 to 1.811 while moving 15.22 GB. For inference, the current BlockTrain path uses one block-stack traversal per full output and serves over direct TCP across three public-network GPU hosts up to a 75.80B-parameter logical fp16 shape, outperforming a matched plain-autoregressive TCP pipeline baseline because it emits a full sequence per WAN pipeline traversal rather than one token per traversal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用BlockTrain进行去中心化AI训练与推理
前沿AI训练正日益受制于对密集型、集中控制的加速器集群的访问。这为超大规模服务商和大型集中实验室创造了结构性优势，使开放或独立的AI项目依赖于稀缺资本、特权基础设施和数据中心地理分布。我们提出Spheroid BlockTrain，一种去中心化训练协议：模型被划分为可独立训练的块，每块基于相同全局目标的局部目标进行优化，并在推理时组合成一个完整模型。在字级WikiText数据集上，BlockTrain达到交叉熵1.359（困惑度3.89），与相同设置下的端到端Transformer基准模型相差约0.04 CE；每个活跃工作者仅训练一个块，避免全模型优化器状态。一个六工作者共享块训练运行通过聚合同块更新，达到CE 1.385。HTTP/TCP传输实验实现实例化检查点与更新传输，包括一个公共IP三主机运行，将CE从5.580降至1.811，传输15.22 GB数据。推理方面，当前BlockTrain路径每完整输出执行一次块栈遍历，通过直接TCP跨三个公共网络GPU主机服务，支持高达758亿参数的逻辑fp16模型，优于匹配的纯自回归TCP管道基线，因其每WAN管道遍历输出完整序列，而非单个token。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the centralization of AI training infrastructure that favors large hyperscalers, this work introduces BlockTrain, a decentralized protocol that splits models into independently trainable blocks, each optimized locally toward a shared global objective and reassembled at inference. Experiments on WikiText show that BlockTrain achieves a cross-entropy of 1.359 (perplexity 3.89), within 0.04 of a full Transformer baseline, while each worker trains only one block without maintaining full optimizer state. A six-worker setup averages block updates to reach 1.385 CE, and HTTP/TCP experiments demonstrate performance gains: a three-host public IP run reduces CE from 5.580 to 1.811 while transferring 15.22 GB. For inference, BlockTrain delivers full sequences per WAN traversal via TCP, outperforming a token-by-token baseline on a 75.80B-parameter model.
为应对AI训练基础设施高度集中化的问题，该研究提出BlockTrain协议，将模型拆分为可独立训练的块，各块基于全局目标进行本地优化，推理时再组合成完整模型。在WikiText数据集上，BlockTrain实现交叉熵1.359（困惑度3.89），与同等设置的完整Transformer基线相差仅0.04，且每个工作节点仅训练一个块，无需维护完整优化器状态。六节点协作时，通过块更新平均得到1.385的交叉熵；HTTP/TCP实验表明，三台公网主机运行可将交叉熵从5.580降至1.811，传输15.22GB数据。推理阶段，BlockTrain通过TCP在三台公网GPU主机间服务完整序列，每轮WAN传输输出完整序列而非单个token，优于同等规模的自回归基线，在75.80B参数模型上表现更优。
Face versus Body Tracking for Human-Robot Interaction: An Egocentric Dataset
Authors: Jessica Wenninger, Gabriel Skantze
First: 2026-06-02T14:15:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:47:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Camera-ready version. Accepted to the 35th IEEE International Conference on Robot and Human Interactive Communication (RO-MAN 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Meaningful human-robot interaction (HRI) requires a robot to continuously assess user engagement through persistent user tracking. However, state-of-the-art Multi-Object Tracking models are heavily optimized for surveillance or autonomous driving. A social robot faces distinct egocentric challenges, such as humans moving in unpredictable nonlinear patterns, obstructing each other, or leaving and reentering the scene. These dynamics trigger frequent identity switches (IDSW), causing the robot to lose its footing mid-conversation. To address this, we introduce a focused, custom-annotated egocentric dataset collected via the Furhat robot. We present a systematic evaluation isolating detection errors from tracking logic, comparing face versus body tracking, and assessing the impact of extended memory and appearance re-identification (ReID). Results indicate that increasing temporal memory mitigates prolonged occlusions but fails on complex dynamic events. Integrating ReID resolves complex switches but exhibits opposing effects: it substantially improves body tracking stability, yet causes facial IDSW to spike due to profile angle sensitivity. Ultimately, our optimized pipeline reduces IDSW by 49% compared to a standard tracking-by-detection baseline, effectively mitigating interaction breakdowns. As standard benchmarks lack dense, close-quarter occlusions, this work highlights the critical need for natively captured social dynamics to truly validate HRI perception models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向人机交互的面部与身体追踪：一个第一人称视角数据集
有意义的人机交互（HRI）要求机器人通过持续追踪用户来评估其参与度。然而，当前最先进的多目标追踪模型主要针对监控或自动驾驶场景进行优化。社交机器人面临独特的第一人称视角挑战，如人类以不可预测的非线性方式移动、相互遮挡或进出场景。这些动态行为引发频繁的身份切换（IDSW），导致机器人在对话中途失去跟踪。为解决此问题，我们引入了一个专门标注、通过Furhat机器人采集的聚焦式第一人称数据集。我们系统性评估了检测错误与追踪逻辑的分离、面部与身体追踪的对比，以及扩展记忆与外观重识别（ReID）的影响。结果表明，增加时间记忆可缓解长期遮挡，但在复杂动态事件中效果不佳。集成ReID可解决复杂身份切换，但其影响相反：显著提升身体追踪稳定性，却因侧脸角度敏感性导致面部IDSW激增。最终，我们的优化流程相比标准检测追踪基线将IDSW降低49%，有效缓解交互中断。由于现有标准基准缺乏密集近距离遮挡，本工作强调了原生捕捉社交动态对真正验证HRI感知模型的必要性。

To improve human-robot interaction, this study addresses the challenge of persistent user tracking in egocentric settings where humans move unpredictably and occlude each other, causing frequent identity switches that disrupt conversation. The authors collect a custom-annotated egocentric dataset using the Furhat robot and evaluate face versus body tracking methods, isolating detection errors from tracking logic while testing the impact of extended memory and appearance re-identification. Results show that extended memory helps with occlusions but not complex dynamics, while ReID improves body tracking stability but increases facial IDSW due to profile sensitivity. The optimized pipeline reduces identity switches by 49% compared to a standard detection-based tracker, demonstrating the necessity of datasets capturing real social dynamics for validating HRI perception models.
为提升人机交互体验，该研究针对社交机器人在第一人称视角下追踪用户时面临的挑战——如人类非线性移动、相互遮挡导致频繁身份切换，从而打断对话——提出解决方案。作者构建了基于Furhat机器人采集的定制化第一人称数据集，并系统评估面部与身体追踪方法，分离检测误差与跟踪逻辑，测试扩展记忆与外观重识别（ReID）的影响。结果表明，扩展记忆可缓解遮挡但对复杂动态无效，而ReID虽提升身体追踪稳定性，却因侧脸敏感性加剧面部身份切换。优化后的融合方案较标准检测基线将身份切换率降低49%，凸显真实社交动态数据对验证HRI感知模型的关键作用。
Evaluating the Interpretability of Sparse Autoencoders with Concept Annotations
Authors: Jonas Klotz, Cassio F. Dantas, Pallavi Jain, Diego Marcos, Begüm Demir
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-23T15:39:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:39:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used to extract interpretable concepts from vision and vision language models, yet existing evaluation methods largely rely on proxy metrics or qualitative inspection rather than measuring semantic correspondence. We present a human-grounded evaluation framework that quantifies alignment between SAE latents and human-annotated concepts, without requiring user studies, and validate this matching through targeted attribute perturbations. To enable this intervention-style evaluation in vision, we construct synCUB and synCOCO, synthetic benchmarks of paired images that differ in exactly one attribute. We introduce Fully-Binary Matching Pursuit (FBMP), a coalition-based matching procedure that supports many-to-one mappings between SAE latents and annotated concepts, and consistently outperforms one-to-one baselines. For functional validation, we propose a Targeted Attribute Perturbation Alignment Score (TAPAScore), which tests whether matched concepts respond selectively and in the expected direction under targeted image-level attribute perturbations. Under sanity checks, our matching and TAPAScore are the only evaluated metrics that reliably distinguish trained SAEs from untrained ones. Across SAEs trained on CLIP and DINOv2 embeddings, we find that increased overcompleteness can reduce perturbation alignment, indicating a reduction in interpretability. Our evaluation framework suggests that moderate dictionary sizes provide the best trade-off, yielding the most interpretable SAEs. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/JonasKlotz/sae-concept-eval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用概念标注评估稀疏自编码器的可解释性
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）正越来越多地用于从视觉和视觉语言模型中提取可解释的概念，但现有评估方法主要依赖代理指标或定性检查，而非测量语义对应性。我们提出了一种基于人类标注的评估框架，无需用户研究即可量化SAE潜变量与人工标注概念之间的对齐程度，并通过针对性的属性扰动验证该匹配效果。为在视觉领域实现这种干预式评估，我们构建了synCUB和synCOCO，即成对图像仅在单一属性上不同的合成基准数据集。我们引入了完全二进制匹配追迹（FBMP），一种基于联盟的匹配过程，支持SAE潜变量与标注概念之间的一对多映射，并始终优于一对一基线方法。为进行功能验证，我们提出了目标属性扰动对齐得分（TAPAScore），用于检验匹配概念是否在针对性图像级属性扰动下选择性响应并朝预期方向变化。在合理性检查中，我们的匹配方法和TAPAScore是唯一能可靠区分训练后SAEs与未训练SAEs的评估指标。在基于CLIP和DINOv2嵌入训练的SAEs中，我们发现过完备性增加会降低扰动对齐度，表明可解释性下降。我们的评估框架表明，适度的字典大小能提供最佳权衡，产生最可解释的SAEs。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/JonasKlotz/sae-concept-eval获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to objectively evaluate the interpretability of sparse autoencoders (SAEs) beyond proxy metrics, this work introduces a human-grounded framework that quantifies semantic alignment between SAE latents and human-annotated concepts using synthetic benchmarks synCUB and synCOCO. It employs Fully-Binary Matching Pursuit (FBMP), a coalition-based matching algorithm that supports many-to-one mappings, outperforming one-to-one baselines. The framework further validates interpretability via Targeted Attribute Perturbation Alignment Score (TAPAScore), which measures whether matched concepts respond selectively under controlled image perturbations. Results show that only the proposed metrics reliably distinguish trained SAEs from untrained ones, and increased overcompleteness correlates with reduced perturbation alignment, suggesting moderate dictionary sizes yield the most interpretable models.
为评估稀疏自编码器（SAE）在提取可解释视觉概念中的有效性，本文提出一种基于人类标注概念的量化评估框架，无需用户研究即可衡量SAE潜变量与概念间的语义对齐。通过构建synCUB和synCOCO两个合成数据集（图像仅在单一属性上不同），支持定向属性扰动实验。方法采用Fully-Binary Matching Pursuit（FBMP）算法，支持多对一映射，优于一对一基线方法。功能验证通过Targeted Attribute Perturbation Alignment Score（TAPAScore）实现，衡量匹配概念在属性扰动下的选择性响应。实验表明，仅FBMP与TAPAScore能可靠区分训练后与未训练的SAE，且过完备性增加会降低扰动对齐度，暗示适度字典大小能获得最佳可解释性。
Evaluation Metrics as Averaged Outcomes of Fair Gambles
Authors: Rabanus Derr, Robert C. Williamson
First: 2024-01-25T19:36:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:38:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the current practices of machine learning, the evaluation of forecasts has become a cornerstone of scientific progress. A multitude of evaluation metrics have been suggested and used to qualify "good" forecasts. What do those metrics share? How are they related? In this work, we use a protocol borrowed from game-theoretic probability to show that a large part of evaluation metrics can be viewed as averaged outcomes of fair gambles. Intuitively, a fair gambler is one which a forecaster would expect to fail. Hence, the gambler's ability to gain disproves the quality of the forecast. Standard evaluation metrics are then variants of choices of such fair gambles. In particular, this choice is structured along two dimensions, one of which separates calibration-type and regret-type metrics. In particular, this framework sheds light on the relationship of calibration and regret showing a theoretical equivalence in their ability to evaluate when being scaled appropriately, but the incomparability of obtained scores.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估指标作为公平博弈的平均结果
在当前机器学习实践中，预测评估已成为科学进步的基石。众多评估指标被提出并用于衡量“良好”预测。这些指标有何共同点？它们如何关联？本文采用来自博弈论概率的协议表明，大量评估指标可视为公平博弈的平均结果。直观上，公平赌徒是预测者预期会失败的赌徒，因此赌徒获利能力证伪了预测质量。标准评估指标即为此类公平博弈选择的变体。特别地，该选择沿两个维度结构化，其中一个维度区分校准型与后悔型指标。该框架揭示了校准与后悔之间的关系：当适当缩放时，二者在评估能力上具有理论等价性，但所得分数不可比较。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the shared structure among diverse machine learning evaluation metrics, this work frames them as averaged outcomes of fair gambles drawn from game-theoretic probability. The method constructs a theoretical framework where each metric corresponds to a specific gamble, with the gambler representing an adversary that a forecaster would expect to lose to. Experimental results demonstrate that metrics can be categorized along two axes—calibration and regret—and reveal that when appropriately scaled, calibration and regret metrics are theoretically equivalent in their evaluation power, yet their raw scores remain incomparable.
本文探讨了机器学习预测评估指标为何种类繁多却常具相似功能，提出多数指标可视为公平博弈的平均结果——这一概念借鉴了博弈论概率。公平博弈代表预测者预期会输的投注策略，若该博弈能获利，则说明预测质量不佳。作者指出，标准指标按博弈结构分为校准型与后悔型两类。其框架揭示，当适当缩放时，校准与后悔指标在评估能力上理论上等价，但其原始得分不可直接比较。
Model selection with proper scoring rules on data sets of time series
Authors: Giorgio Corani, Stefano Damato, Dario Azzimonti, Lorenzo Zambon
First: 2026-06-23T15:36:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:36:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the problem of model selection between probabilistic models on data sets of time series. Chosen a proper scoring rule, we denote by the term \textit{score} the average value of the scoring rule on the test of an individual time series. For model selection, we need aggregating the values of the scores across multiple time series. Three summary statistics are commonly used for model selection: mean score, median score, and mean rank. Results in previous papers show that these statistics can yield conflicting decisions; we show how the conflicting conclusions are due to the skewness of the distribution of scores. We also show that as the test set of each time series of the data set increases, the different model selection criteria progressively converge to the same conclusion. However, for short tests sets, only the mean score identifies the true model as the best. We illustrate these phenomena with an analysis on intermittent time series, including the data set of the M5 competition, where we underline the importance of having a large test set. In such experiments, we further notice that model selection based on mean ranks remains unchanged using different scaling factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于恰当评分规则的时间序列数据集模型选择
我们考虑在时间序列数据集上对概率模型进行模型选择的问题。选定一个恰当评分规则，我们用“得分”表示该规则在单个时间序列测试集上的平均值。进行模型选择时，需要对多个时间序列的得分值进行聚合。常用的三种汇总统计量为：平均得分、中位数得分和平均排名。先前研究结果表明，这些统计量可能得出相互矛盾的决策结论；我们指出，这种矛盾源于得分分布的偏态性。同时我们证明，随着每个时间序列测试集规模的增大，不同的模型选择标准会逐步收敛至相同结论。然而，对于较短的测试集，只有平均得分能识别出真实最优模型。 我们以间歇性时间序列数据（包括M5竞赛数据集）为例进行分析，强调拥有较大测试集的重要性。在这些实验中，我们进一步发现，基于平均排名的模型选择结果在不同缩放因子下保持不变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to select probabilistic models for time series data using proper scoring rules, this study investigates how different aggregation methods—mean score, median score, and mean rank—perform in model selection. The authors demonstrate that these methods can produce conflicting results due to score distribution skewness, but as test set sizes grow, they converge to consistent conclusions. For small test sets, only the mean score reliably identifies the true best model. Experiments on intermittent time series, including the M5 competition data, confirm the importance of large test sets and show that mean rank is invariant to scaling factors.
为在时间序列数据上选择概率模型，本文基于恰当评分规则，评估了三种聚合方法——平均得分、中位数得分和平均排名。研究发现，这些方法因得分分布偏斜而可能产生冲突的模型选择结果，但随着每个时间序列测试集增大，它们会逐步达成一致结论。在间歇性时间序列（如M5竞赛数据集）的实验中，发现仅平均得分能在小测试集下准确识别真实模型，而平均排名对缩放因子不敏感。
CN-NewsTTS Bench: a target-level automatic benchmark for raw-input Chinese news TTS pronunciation
Authors: Shijun Luo
Venue: ICASSP
First: 2026-06-23T15:34:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:34:58+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure, 8 tables. ICASSP-style preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chinese news text contains dense written forms such as scores, hyphenated model names, ranges, unit symbols, percentages, English abbreviations, and mixed Chinese-Latin-digit names. These forms are frequent in real listening workflows, and a text-to-speech (TTS) system can preserve the written string while changing the spoken meaning. We introduce CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1, an open target-level benchmark for evaluating whether Chinese news TTS products pronounce such targets correctly from raw text, without user-side rules, LLM rewriting, SSML hints, or manual edits. The release contains a 200-record development set, an 800-record public test set, 992 public auto-evaluable targets, fixed transcripts from a three-ASR ensemble, an automatic target scorer, and initial results for seven product TTS systems. We additionally report ASR-route diagnostics, ASR-subset ablations, category-level results, confidence intervals, and provider configuration metadata. The best system reaches 0.879 strict accuracy, while several systems remain below 0.60.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CN-NewsTTS基准：面向原始输入中文新闻TTS发音的目标级自动评估基准
中文新闻文本包含密集的书面形式，如分数、带连字符的型号名称、范围、单位符号、百分比、英文缩写及中英混排的数字名称。这些形式在真实听觉工作流中频繁出现，而TTS系统可保留书面字符串的同时改变其发音含义。我们引入CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1，这是一个开放的目标级基准，用于评估中文新闻TTS产品能否从原始文本准确发音此类目标，无需用户侧规则、大语言模型重写、SSML提示或人工编辑。该发布包含200条开发集、800条公开测试集、992条可自动评估的目标、由三套ASR系统集成的固定转录、自动目标评分器及七款产品TTS系统的初步结果。此外，我们还报告了ASR路径诊断、ASR子集消融分析、类别级结果、置信区间及供应商配置元数据。最佳系统达到0.879的严格准确率，而部分系统仍低于0.60。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate Chinese news TTS systems on real-world text containing complex written forms like numbers, symbols, and mixed scripts, the authors introduce CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1, a target-level benchmark that assesses pronunciation accuracy without relying on user-side rules or manual interventions. The benchmark includes a 200-record dev set and 800-record public test set, with 992 auto-evaluable targets and fixed transcripts from an ASR ensemble. Experimental results show that the best TTS system achieves 0.879 strict accuracy, while several others score below 0.60, highlighting significant performance gaps across systems.
为评估中文新闻TTS系统在真实原始文本（含分数、连字符名称、中英混合数字等复杂形式）中的发音准确性，作者提出CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1目标级基准，无需用户规则或人工编辑即可自动评估。该基准包含200条开发集和800条公开测试集，共992个可自动评估的目标，采用三ASR系统固定转录与自动评分器。实验结果显示，最优系统准确率达0.879，部分系统仍低于0.60，揭示出显著性能差距。
TACTFUL: Tactile-Driven Exploration For Object Localization and Identification in Confined Environments
Authors: Shivani Kamtikar, Chung Hee Kim, Camilla Tabasso, Tye Brady, Joshua Migdal, Taskin Padir
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-23T15:33:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:33:51+00:00
Comments: IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans effortlessly locate and identify objects by touch alone, even without vision. In contrast, robotic systems rely heavily on vision and struggle with autonomous tactile exploration and object identification. We present TACTFUL, a vision-free tactile exploration framework that enables a multi-fingered robot to autonomously explore confined workspaces, discover objects through contact, and identify them via tactile reconstruction. Trained entirely on real hardware without simulation, our system learns a single policy that balances global workspace exploration with local surface refinement through a dynamic reward schedule. Our results demonstrate that tactile sensing, when paired with structured learning, can serve as an effective primary modality for object-level reasoning, achieving 77% success with 0.015 m average reconstruction error and outperforming baseline approaches on real-world objects.

标题：TACTFUL：面向受限环境中物体定位与识别的触觉驱动探索
人类仅凭触觉即可轻松定位和识别物体，即使在无视觉的情况下。相比之下，机器人系统高度依赖视觉，在自主触觉探索和物体识别方面表现不佳。我们提出TACTFUL，一种无需视觉的触觉探索框架，使多指机器人能够自主探索受限工作空间，通过接触发现物体，并通过触觉重建进行识别。该系统完全基于真实硬件训练，无需仿真，学习一个统一策略，通过动态奖励机制平衡全局工作空间探索与局部表面细化。实验结果表明，触觉感知结合结构化学习可作为物体级推理的有效主要模态，实现77%的成功率，平均重建误差为0.015米，并在真实物体上优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the human ability to locate and identify objects through touch without vision, this paper introduces TACTFUL, a vision-free tactile exploration framework for robotic manipulation in confined spaces. The method trains a single policy on real hardware using a dynamic reward schedule to balance global exploration and local surface refinement, enabling a multi-fingered robot to autonomously discover and reconstruct objects via tactile sensing. Experimental results show the system achieves 77% success rate with an average reconstruction error of 0.015 meters, outperforming baseline methods on real-world objects without relying on visual input.
