Training-Time Optical Priors for Wireless Capsule Endoscopy Classification: Hemoglobin-Aware Input Fusion with Cross-Vendor Evaluation
Authors: Chengshuai Yang, Lei Xing, Keyaan Zawad Alam, Gregory Entin, Roopa Vemulapalli, Lisa Casey, Raiyan Tripti Zaman
First: 2026-05-14T16:52:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:32:26+00:00
Comments: 64 pages, 11 figures, 15 tables. Expanded version: adds cross-vendor Galar evaluation (GalKva-2026 benchmark), cross-architecture replication (ResNet-18, ConvNeXt-Tiny), and foundation-model baselines. Code, checkpoints, and benchmark: https://github.com/integritynoble/Physics-Informed-PillCam . Submitted to Medical Image Analysis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Background. RGB-trained classifiers for wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) conflate hemoglobin contrast with bile staining and illumination falloff, limiting sensitivity to small-vessel vascular findings such as Lymphangiectasia. We introduce a physics-informed framework that injects an analytic, Monte-Carlo-inspired hemoglobin prior into a standard classifier purely at training time -- to our knowledge the first use of an explicit optical light-transport prior in WCE classification. Methods. On Kvasir-Capsule (47,238 frames, 43 patients, 11 evaluable classes; patient-disjoint split) we test, across 6 seeds against an RGB-only EfficientNet-B0 baseline: (i) a 5-channel input-fusion variant feeding the prior P_blood alongside RGB; (ii) a distillation variant that runs on plain 3-channel RGB at inference; and (iii) a three-stream extension adding a temporal Transformer and an autoencoder-residual stream. We replicate across ResNet-18 and ConvNeXt-Tiny and report cross-vendor zero-shot transfer on the public Galar cohort. Results. Input fusion lifts cross-seed macro-AUC 0.760 -> 0.783 (5/6 seeds positive); distillation reaches 0.773; the three-stream model reaches 0.804 (+0.044 over baseline, paired DeLong p < 1e-4). Lymphangiectasia AUC rises 0.238 -> 0.337, sign-consistent across all 6 seeds. A four-variant ablation reveals a parameterization-mechanism boundary: only the spatial-channel form lifts. Cross-vendor zero-shot on Galar retains ~60% of the ConvNeXt-Tiny lift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于无线胶囊内镜分类的训练时光学先验：基于血红蛋白感知的输入融合与跨厂商评估
背景：用于无线胶囊内镜（WCE）的RGB训练分类器将血红蛋白对比度与胆汁染色及光照衰减混淆，限制了对小血管血管病变（如淋巴管扩张）的敏感性。我们引入一种物理信息驱动的框架，在训练阶段将分析型、蒙特卡洛启发式的血红蛋白先验注入标准分类器——据我们所知，这是首次在WCE分类中显式使用光学光传输先验。方法：在Kvasir-Capsule数据集（47,238帧，43名患者，11个可评估类别；患者不相交划分）上，我们针对RGB-only EfficientNet-B0基线，在6个随机种子上测试：（i）一种5通道输入融合变体，将先验P_blood与RGB并行输入；（ii）一种蒸馏变体，在推理时仅使用标准3通道RGB；（iii）一种三流扩展模型，增加时间Transformer和自编码器残差流。我们在ResNet-18和ConvNeXt-Tiny架构上进行复现，并报告在公开Galar队列上的跨厂商零样本迁移。结果：输入融合将跨种子宏AUC从0.760提升至0.783（6个种子中有5个提升）；蒸馏模型达到0.773；三流模型达到0.804（较基线提升0.044，配对DeLong检验p < 1e-4）。淋巴管扩张AUC从0.238提升至0.337，6个种子均保持符号一致性。四变体消融实验揭示参数化-机制边界：仅空间通道形式有效。在Galar上的跨厂商零样本迁移保留了约60%的ConvNeXt-Tiny提升效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor sensitivity of RGB-trained classifiers in wireless capsule endoscopy due to confounding of hemoglobin contrast with bile and illumination artifacts, this work introduces a physics-informed training-time optical prior based on Monte-Carlo-inspired hemoglobin light transport. The method fuses a hemoglobin-aware channel with RGB inputs during training, and tests variants including distillation and a three-stream architecture with temporal Transformer and autoencoder-residual modules. Experiments on Kvasir-Capsule show that input fusion improves macro-AUC from 0.760 to 0.783, distillation reaches 0.773, and the three-stream model achieves 0.804 — a statistically significant +0.044 over baseline — with a notable 0.238 to 0.337 AUC gain for lymphangiectasia. Cross-vendor zero-shot transfer on Galar retains ~60% of the ConvNeXt-Tiny performance lift.
为了解决无线胶囊内镜中RGB训练分类器混淆血红蛋白对比度与胆汁染色及光照衰减的问题，该研究在训练阶段引入了基于蒙特卡洛启发的血红蛋白光学先验。方法包括输入融合、蒸馏和三流架构（含时序Transformer与自编码器残差流），在Kvasir-Capsule数据集上测试。三流模型将宏AUC从0.760提升至0.804（p<1e-4），淋巴管扩张AUC从0.238提升至0.337，且在Galar跨厂商零样本迁移中保留约60%的性能增益，表明其泛化能力较强。
A Physics-Informed Fourier-Wavelet Transformer for Multiscale Computational Fluid Dynamics Surrogate Modeling
Authors: Somyajit Chakraborty, Ming Pan, Xizhong Chen
First: 2026-06-23T15:24:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:24:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed surrogate models can accelerate computational fluid dynamics simulations. However, many existing methods reproduce global flow patterns more reliably than localized multiscale structures. This study presents a physics-informed Fourier-wavelet transformer for next-step velocity-field reconstruction in real-world flow benchmarks. The proposed formulation combines hybrid Fourier-wavelet spectral encoding with physics-biased self-attention based on partial differential equation residual diagnostics. It also uses self-supervised pretraining through Masked Physics Prediction and Equation Consistency Prediction. The experiments are conducted on two real benchmark cases: cylinder-wake flow and fluid-structure interaction. All approaches are evaluated under a shared local protocol and compared with spectral, transformer-based, operator-learning, and physics-informed neural-network baselines. On the cylinder-wake benchmark, the proposed model achieves the best aggregate accuracy, with an all-channel normalized mean-squared error of 0.05875 and an all-channel Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.97019. On the fluid-structure-interaction benchmark, it gives the lowest all-channel normalized mean-squared error of $2.70 \times 10^{-4}$, compared with $4.02 \times 10^{-4}$ for the strongest baseline. Component-wise field comparisons and scale-separated diagnostics further show stronger recovery of localized wake structures, including near-body, wake-core, and far-wake features. The results demonstrate improved real-world flow reconstruction while maintaining a practical accuracy-cost tradeoff.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种物理信息驱动的傅里叶-小波变换器用于多尺度计算流体力学代理建模
物理信息驱动的代理模型可加速计算流体力学仿真。然而，许多现有方法在再现全局流场模式方面比局部多尺度结构更可靠。本研究提出一种物理信息驱动的傅里叶-小波变换器，用于真实流场基准中的下一步速度场重建。该模型结合了混合傅里叶-小波频谱编码与基于偏微分方程残差诊断的物理偏置自注意力机制，并通过掩码物理预测和方程一致性预测进行自监督预训练。实验在两个真实基准案例——圆柱尾流和流固耦合问题上进行，所有方法均在统一局部协议下评估，并与频谱、变换器、算子学习及物理信息神经网络基线方法比较。在圆柱尾流基准上，该模型获得最佳综合精度，全通道归一化均方误差为0.05875，全通道皮尔逊相关系数为0.97019。在流固耦合基准上，其全通道归一化均方误差为$2.70 \times 10^{-4}$，优于最强基线方法$4.02 \times 10^{-4}$。分量场对比与尺度分离诊断进一步表明，该模型对局部尾流结构（包括近体、尾流核心区及远尾流特征）的恢复能力更强。结果表明，该模型在保持实用精度-成本权衡的同时，提升了真实流场重建效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately capture localized multiscale structures in fluid dynamics simulations, this study introduces a physics-informed Fourier-wavelet transformer that combines spectral encoding with physics-biased attention and self-supervised pretraining. Evaluated on cylinder-wake and fluid-structure interaction benchmarks, the model outperforms existing spectral, transformer, operator-learning, and physics-informed neural-network baselines, achieving the lowest error metrics and best correlation scores. It demonstrates superior recovery of fine-scale flow features such as near-body, wake-core, and far-wake structures, while maintaining a favorable accuracy-cost balance.
为解决现有物理信息代理模型在捕捉流体动力学中局部多尺度结构方面的不足，本研究提出一种结合混合傅里叶-小波频谱编码与基于偏微分方程残差诊断的物理引导自注意力机制的傅里叶-小波变换器。模型通过掩码物理预测与方程一致性预测进行自监督预训练。在圆柱尾流与流固耦合两个真实基准测试中，相比多种基线模型，该方法在圆柱尾流上取得最佳综合精度，全通道归一化均方误差为0.05875，皮尔逊相关系数达0.97019；在流固耦合测试中误差最低，为$2.70 \times 10^{-4}$，优于最强基线$1.32 \times 10^{-4}$。尺度分离诊断进一步证实其在近体、尾流核心区及远尾流等局部结构恢复上表现更优。
Policy Gradient with Self-Attention for Model-Free Distributed Nonlinear Multi-Agent Games
Authors: Eduardo Sebastián, Maitrayee Keskar, Eeman Iqbal, Eduardo Montijano, Carlos Sagüés, Nikolay Atanasov
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2025-09-22T19:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:24:05+00:00
Comments: The paper has been accepted and will be presented at IEEE/RSJ IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent games in dynamic nonlinear settings are challenging due to the time-varying interactions among the agents and the non-stationarity of the (potential) Nash equilibria. In this paper we consider model-free games, where agent transitions and costs are observed without knowledge of the transition and cost functions that generate them. We propose a novel distributed policy structure that follows the communication constraints in multi-team games, with multiple agents per team, and learned through policy gradients. Our formulation is inspired by the structure of distributed policies in linear quadratic games, which take the form of time-varying linear feedback gains. In the nonlinear case, we model the policies as nonlinear feedback gains, parameterized by self-attention layers to account for the time-varying multi-agent communication topology. We demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance in several settings, including distributed linear and nonlinear regulation, and simulated and real multi-robot pursuit-and-evasion games.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于自注意力的策略梯度方法用于无模型分布式非线性多智能体博弈
在动态非线性环境中，多智能体博弈因智能体间时变交互及（潜在）纳什均衡的非平稳性而具有挑战性。本文考虑无模型博弈，其中智能体的转移与代价可观测，但未知生成这些转移与代价的函数。我们提出一种新颖的分布式策略结构，遵循多团队博弈中的通信约束（每个团队含多个智能体），并通过策略梯度学习。该结构受线性二次博弈中分布式策略形式的启发，后者表现为时变线性反馈增益。在非线性情形下，我们将策略建模为非线性反馈增益，并通过自注意力层参数化，以适应时变多智能体通信拓扑。我们在多个场景中验证了该方法的强性能，包括分布式线性和非线性调节，以及模拟与真实多机器人追逃博弈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of time-varying interactions and non-stationary equilibria in multi-agent nonlinear games, this paper proposes a model-free distributed policy gradient method that uses self-attention layers to parameterize time-varying nonlinear feedback gains per team, respecting communication constraints. The method adapts the structure of linear quadratic game policies to nonlinear settings, enabling decentralized learning without knowledge of underlying dynamics. Experimental results show strong performance across distributed regulation tasks and simulated and real multi-robot pursuit-and-evasion scenarios, demonstrating robustness and scalability.
为应对动态非线性多智能体游戏中交互时变与纳什均衡非平稳的问题，本文提出一种无需模型的分布式策略梯度方法，通过自注意力层参数化非线性反馈策略，以适应通信拓扑的变化。该方法在分布式调节与追逃仿真及真实多机器人场景中表现优异，性能常优于或匹配现有基线方法，同时具备良好的团队与智能体扩展性。
SupplyNet: Supporting Visual Exploratory Learning in Supply Chain via Contextual Multi-Agent Simulation
Authors: Yanjia Li, Kelcy Kexin Han, Tianrui Hu, Yi-Fan Cao, Huamin Qu, Sicheng Song
First: 2026-06-23T15:23:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:23:10+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulation has long supported supply chain management instruction by letting learners observe network behavior and test decision strategies. Recent progress in LLM-driven agents opens new possibilities for richer, more adaptive simulations, but many existing systems still present abstract, opaque data that overwhelms learners and discourages active exploration. We introduce \textit{SupplyNet}, a gamified visual simulation system built on a contextual graph-based LLM multi-agent framework that models interdependent supply chain dynamics and provides responsive feedback through tiered challenges. \textit{SupplyNet} turns the simulation into a manipulable decision space by integrating an interactive network view of system state, a branching timeline for "what-if" exploration and comparison, and a task-oriented analysis console for structured performance breakdowns. Together, these visual components support counterfactual exploration, causal tracing, and comparative reasoning about outcomes. A user study suggests that \textit{SupplyNet} increases engagement and supports users' perceived understanding of supply chain dynamics, highlighting the potential of pairing contextual multi-agent simulation with visualization to advance operational comprehension.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SupplyNet：通过情境多智能体仿真支持供应链中的视觉探索式学习
仿真长期以来通过让学习者观察网络行为并测试决策策略，支持供应链管理教学。近期基于大语言模型（LLM）驱动的智能体取得的新进展，为更丰富、更具适应性的仿真提供了新可能，但许多现有系统仍呈现抽象、不透明的数据，令学习者不堪重负并抑制其主动探索。我们引入\textit{SupplyNet}，这是一个基于情境图结构LLM多智能体框架的趣味化视觉仿真系统，用于建模相互依赖的供应链动态并通过对分层挑战提供响应式反馈。\textit{SupplyNet}通过整合系统状态的交互式网络视图、用于“假如”探索与比较的分支时间线，以及面向任务的分析控制台，将仿真转化为可操作的决策空间。这些视觉组件共同支持反事实探索、因果追溯和结果比较推理。用户研究表明，\textit{SupplyNet}提升了参与度，并支持用户对供应链动态的感知理解，凸显了将情境多智能体仿真与可视化结合以提升操作性理解的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make supply chain simulations more intuitive and exploratory for learners, SupplyNet introduces a gamified visual system powered by a contextual graph-based LLM multi-agent framework that models interdependent supply chain dynamics. It enhances user interaction through an interactive network view, branching timeline for "what-if" scenarios, and task-oriented analysis console, enabling counterfactual exploration, causal tracing, and comparative reasoning. Experimental results from a user study indicate that SupplyNet significantly increases user engagement and improves perceived understanding of supply chain operations compared to abstract, non-visual simulation tools.
为提升学习者对供应链模拟的理解与参与度，SupplyNet提出一种基于LLM多智能体的可视化游戏化模拟系统。该系统通过图结构建模供应链的相互依赖关系，并结合交互式网络视图、分支时间线和任务导向分析面板，支持反事实探索与因果追踪。实验表明，用户参与度显著提升，对供应链动态的理解也明显增强，验证了可视化与情境化模拟结合在提升运营认知方面的潜力。
Response-Aware Multimodal Learning for Post-Treatment Visual Acuity Forecasting
Authors: Phuoc-Nguyen Bui, Van-Vi Vo, Duc-Tai Le, Junghyun Bum, Van-Nguyen Pham, Ki-Young Kim, Seung-Young Yu, Hyunseung Choo
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-05-30T07:36:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:21:54+00:00
Comments: To appear in MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-term visual acuity (VA) outcomes after anti-VEGF therapy are central to patient counseling, expectation setting, and follow-up planning in diabetic macular edema (DME). However, in clinical practice, physicians must often estimate long-term visual trajectories based only on early post-treatment findings, making reliable prognostication difficult. Although prior OCT-based learning approaches have largely focused on short-term response or single-endpoint prediction, modeling VA trajectories across multiple future time points from early longitudinal observations remains insufficiently explored. In this study, we assembled a real-world cohort of 188 anti-VEGF--treated DME patients with paired baseline and month-1 OCT scans, along with tabular OCT-derived biomarkers and non-imaging clinical variables. Using only these early data, we formulate a multi-horizon VA forecasting problem aimed at predicting visual outcomes at 3, 6, 12, 18, and 24 months, reflecting clinically meaningful follow-up intervals. We propose \textbf{ReVA}, a response-aware multimodal framework that integrates structural features from baseline and month-1 OCT with the tabular variables to capture baseline disease status and early treatment response. ReVA uses spatial attention to preserve localized prognostic imaging features and a dependency-aware tabular encoder to model interactions among clinical variables. These multimodal representations are fused to predict patient-specific long-term visual acuity trajectories. The proposed framework achieves MAE $=0.1246$, RMSE $=0.1621$, and $R^2=0.6064$ for 24-month VA prediction, with consistent performance across all forecast horizons. Our findings show that incorporating early treatment-response signals enables clinically meaningful long-term visual acuity forecasting, supporting data-driven decision support for routine anti-VEGF management.
中文标题/摘要
标题：响应感知的多模态学习用于抗VEGF治疗后视力预后预测
糖尿病黄斑水肿（DME）患者接受抗VEGF治疗后的长期视力（VA）预后，是临床咨询、预期设定和随访规划的核心。然而，临床实践中医生常需仅依据早期治疗后结果来估算长期视力轨迹，导致可靠预后预测困难。尽管先前基于OCT的学习方法多聚焦于短期反应或单一终点预测，但基于早期纵向观察预测多个未来时间点视力轨迹的研究仍显不足。本研究收集了188例接受抗VEGF治疗的DME患者的真实世界队列，包含基线与第1个月OCT扫描、表格化OCT衍生生物标志物及非影像临床变量。仅利用这些早期数据，我们构建了多时间点视力预测问题，旨在预测3、6、12、18和24个月的视力结果，反映临床有意义的随访间隔。我们提出\textbf{ReVA}，一种响应感知的多模态框架，整合基线与第1个月OCT的结构特征与表格变量，以捕捉基线疾病状态和早期治疗反应。ReVA采用空间注意力保留局部预后影像特征，并使用依赖感知表格编码器建模临床变量间的交互。这些多模态表征融合后用于预测患者特异性长期视力轨迹。该框架在24个月视力预测中达到MAE=0.1246、RMSE=0.1621、R²=0.6064，各预测时点表现一致。研究结果表明，纳入早期治疗反应信号可实现临床有意义的长期视力预测，支持抗VEGF常规管理的数据驱动决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the clinical need to forecast long-term visual acuity after anti-VEGF therapy in diabetic macular edema using only early post-treatment data, this study proposes ReVA, a response-aware multimodal framework that integrates baseline and month-1 OCT imaging features with tabular clinical biomarkers. The method employs spatial attention to preserve localized imaging patterns and a dependency-aware tabular encoder to model clinical variable interactions, then fuses these representations to predict patient-specific VA trajectories at 3, 6, 12, 18, and 24 months. Experimental results on 188 patients show strong performance with MAE=0.1246, RMSE=0.1621, and R²=0.6064 for 24-month prediction, demonstrating that early treatment response signals are sufficient for reliable long-term prognosis.
为解决临床中仅凭早期治疗后数据预测糖尿病黄斑水肿患者长期视力恢复的难题，本研究提出ReVA框架，融合基线与治疗1个月的OCT影像特征及表格化临床生物标志物。该方法通过空间注意力保留局部影像特征，依赖感知表格编码器建模临床变量交互，最终融合多模态表征预测3、6、12、18、24个月的视力轨迹。在188名患者数据上，ReVA在24个月预测中达到MAE=0.1246、RMSE=0.1621、R²=0.6064，各时间点表现稳定，表明早期治疗反应信号可支撑可靠的长期视力预判。
Automated Summarization of Software Documents: An LLM-based Multi-Agent Approach
Authors: Duc S. H. Nguyen, Minh T. Nguyen, Phuong T. Nguyen, Juri Di Rocco, Davide Di Ruscio
First: 2026-06-23T15:18:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-23T15:18:17+00:00
Comments: Paper has been accepted for publication with the Automated Software Engineering journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) and LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are revolutionizing software engineering (SE) by advancing automation, decision-making, and knowledge processing. Their recent application to SE tasks has already shown promising results. In this paper, we focus on summarization as a key application area. We present Metagente, an LLM-based MAS designed to generate concise and accurate summaries of software documentation. Metagente employs a Teacher-Student architecture where multiple LLM agents collaborate to enhance relevance and precision of produced summaries. An empirical evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates Metagente's effectiveness in streamlining workflows, outperforming the considered baselines. The evaluation provides evidence that Metagente improves summarization for requirements analysis and technical documentation. Our findings underscore the transformative potential of these technologies in SE, while identifying challenges and future research directions for their seamless integration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的多智能体软件文档自动摘要方法
大语言模型（LLM）及基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）正通过提升自动化、决策与知识处理能力，革新软件工程（SE）。其在SE任务中的最新应用已取得显著成果。本文聚焦摘要这一关键应用场景，提出Metagente——一种基于LLM的多智能体系统，旨在生成简洁准确的软件文档摘要。Metagente采用教师-学生架构，多个LLM智能体协同工作，以提升摘要的相关性与精确度。在真实数据集上的实证评估表明，Metagente能有效优化工作流程，优于现有基线方法。评估结果证实，Metagente在需求分析与技术文档摘要方面表现更优。研究结果凸显了这些技术在SE领域的变革潜力，同时指出了其无缝集成所面临的挑战与未来研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate software documentation summarization to improve efficiency in software engineering, this paper introduces Metagente, an LLM-based multi-agent system that employs a Teacher-Student architecture to collaboratively generate accurate and concise summaries. Through empirical evaluation on real-world datasets, Metagente consistently outperforms existing baselines in summarizing requirements and technical documentation, demonstrating its effectiveness in streamlining workflows and enhancing precision. The results highlight the potential of LLM-based multi-agent systems in SE while also pointing to integration challenges that require further research.
为提升软件工程中文档摘要的自动化效率，本文提出Metagente系统，采用基于大语言模型的多智能体架构，通过教师-学生协作机制生成精准简洁的摘要。实证评估显示，该系统在真实数据集上优于现有基线方法，有效提升需求分析与技术文档的摘要质量，验证了多智能体大模型在软件工程中的变革潜力。
